Koat(i) kerm kuraiy, huoun dhaaray.   Sherm(u) paavaiy suglay birthaaray.  Anik tpusiya, kuray uhunkaar.  Nerk soorg phir(i) phir(i) avtar.  Anik jutun kur(i) aatum nuhee druvaiy.  Hur(i) Durgeh kuhou kaiysay guvaiy?  Aapus kuou jo bhulaa kuhaavaiy. Tiseh(i) bhulaayee nicket(i) na aavaiy.  Serb kee rayn ja ka mun(u) hoyay.  Kuhou Nanak ta kee nirmul soyay.   3. 

Word Meaning: Kerm = rituals.   Huoun = ego.    Dhaaray = wears; behaves.  Sherm = hard work.   Birthaaray = useless.   Tpusiya = worship; hard labour with dedication.   Avtar = reincarnation.   Jutun = efforts.     Aatum = soul; mind.   Druvaiy = melts; softens.   Guvaiy = go; reach.   Nicket(i) = nearby.   Rayn = dust; (implying) humbleness.    Nirmul = pure; noble.   Soyay = fame; reputation. 

MEANING:  If a man performs millions of accepted rituals, but is conceited about them, all his hard work becomes worthless.  If a man performs countless types of worship, but is conceited about that, he gets into the birth-death cycle and goes through paradise-like and hell-like lives (depending upon his deeds) again and again. If his countless efforts do not soften his heart, how can he reach Waheguru’s Court (connect with Waheguru)?  A man, who calls himself a noble person, does not have any nobleness come even close to him.  (On the other hand), A man who is so humble and is happy to serve others has a very noble reputation, oh Nanak.   3.

ਜਬ ਲਗੁ ਜਾਨੈ ਮੁਝ ਤੇ ਕਛੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਤਬ ਇਸ ਕਉ ਸੁਖੁ ਨਾਹੀ ਕੋਇ॥ ਜਬ ਇਹ ਜਾਨੈ ਮੈ ਕਿਛੁ ਕਰਤਾ॥ ਤਬ ਲਗੁ ਗਰਭ ਜੋਨਿ ਮਹਿ ਫਿਰਤਾ॥ ਜਬ ਧਾਰੈ ਕੋਊ ਬੈਰੀ ਮੀਤੁ॥ ਤਬ ਲਗੁ ਨਿਹਚਲੁ ਨਾਹੀ ਚੀਤੁ॥ ਜਬ ਲਗੁ ਮੋਹ ਮਗਨ ਸੰਗਿ ਮਾਇ॥ ਤਬ ਲਗੁ ਧਰਮਰਾਇ ਦੇਇ ਸਜਾਇ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਤੇ ਬੰਧਨ ਤੂਟੈ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਹਉ ਛੂਟੈ॥ 4॥

Jub lug(u) jaanaiy moojh tay kuchh(u) hoyay.  Tub iss kuou sookh(u) naahi koyay.  Jub eh jaanaiy mein kichh(u) kerta.  Tub lug(u) gerbh joan(i) meh(i) phirta.  Jub dhaaraiy koyoo baiyri meet(u).  Tub lug(u) nihchul(u) naahi cheet(u).  Jub lug(u) moh mugun sung(i) Maayay. Tub lug(u) Dherm Raayay dayay sujaayay. Prubh kirpa tay bundhun tootay. Gur persaad(i) Nanak huoun chhootaiy.  4. 

Word Meaning:   Jub Lug(u) = as long as.  Kichh(u) Kerta = doing some favours to others.  Jaanaiy = is conceited about.   Gerbh Joan = life forms in the womb.   Dhaaraiy = thinks of; treats.   Nihchul = stable; peaceful.   Cheet(u) = mind.   Mugun = absorbed; focused.    Maayay = Maya.   Bundhun = bonds.   Huoun = ego.    4.

MEANING:  As long as a man thinks that he is capable of doing something big, he enjoys no comfort (suffers hardships to accomplish his goal, and still may not succeed). As long as a man is conceited that he is doing some favours to others (people depend upon him), he travels through many life forms in the womb. As long as a man treats others as his friends or enemies (discriminates between people), he never attains peace of mind.   As long as a man is absorbed in the love of Maya, he suffers punishment at the hands of the Angel of justice.   When he takes Guru’s shelter and is able to overcome his haumein, his bonds of Maya are broken by Waheguru’s grace (he is salvaged), oh Nanak.  4.

ਸਹਸ ਖਟੇ ਲਖ ਕਉ ਉਠਿ ਧਾਵੈ॥

(ਅੰਗ 279)॥    ਤ੍ਰਿਪਤਿ ਨ ਆਵੈ ਮਾਇਆ ਪਾਛੈ ਪਾਵੈ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਭੋਗ ਬਿਖਿਆ ਕੇ ਕਰੈ॥ ਨਹ ਤ੍ਰਿਪਤਾਵੈ ਖਪਿ ਖਪਿ ਮਰੈ॥ ਬਿਨਾ ਸੰਤੋਖ ਨਹੀ ਕੋਊ ਰਾਜੈ॥ ਸੁਪਨ ਮਨੋਰਥ ਬ੍ਰਿਥੇ ਸਭ ਕਾਜੈ॥ ਨਾਮ ਰੰਗਿ ਸਰਬ ਸੁਖੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਬਡਭਾਗੀ ਕਿਸੈ ਪਰਾਪਤਿ ਹੋਇ॥ ਕਰਨ ਕਰਾਵਨ ਆਪੇ ਆਪਿ॥ ਸਦਾ ਸਦਾ ਨਾਨਕ ਹਰਿ ਜਾਪਿ॥ 5॥

Sehs khutay lukh kuou ootth(i) dhaavaiy. 

(PAGE 279):                          Triput na aavaiy, Maya paachhaiy paavaiy. Anik bhoag bikhiya kay kuraiy.  Neh triptaavaiy, khup(i) khup(i) muraiy. Bina suntoakh nuhee koyoo raajaiy. Soopun munoruth, birthay subh kaajaiy. Naam rung(i) serb sookh(u) hoyay.  Budbhaagi kisaiy praput(i) hoyay.  Kern kraavun Aapay Aap(i).  Suda suda Nanak Hur(i) jaap(i).   5.

Word Meaning:  Sehs = thousand.   Lukh = hundred thousand.   Dhaavaiy = rushes to.   Triput(i) = contentment.   Paachhaiy Paavaiy = chases.   Bikhiya = Maya; sensual pleasures.     Khup(i) Khup(i) = hard labour without much reward.     Suntoakh = patience; contentment.     Raajaiy = satisfied.    Soopun = dream.   Munoruth = objective.   Kaajaiy = tasks; efforts.   Budbhaagi = fortunate.     5.

MEANING: The man is never content with the amount of money he has, and always keeps chasing it; when he has earned one thousand rupees (a huge amount in those days), he rushes to earn a hundred thousand.  He enjoys money in countless ways to please his senses, but is still not satisfied, and kills (exhausts) himself in (spiritually) useless hard labour. Nobody‘s appetite for more wealth goes away until he is contented (which he never does), and all his efforts to achieve the objective go down the drain like a dream.  However, all comforts are achieved when one loves Waheguru’s Naam; but only a rare, lucky person gets to embed His Naam in his heart.   It is Waheguru, who does everything Himself, and gets others to do whatever He wishes.  Recite His Naam all the time, oh Nanak.    5.

ਕਰਨ ਕਰਾਵਨ ਕਰਨੈਹਾਰੁ॥ ਇਸ ਕੈ ਹਾਥਿ ਕਹਾ ਬੀਚਾਰੁ॥ ਜੈਸੀ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਕਰੇ ਤੈਸਾ ਹੋਇ॥ ਆਪੇ ਆਪਿ ਆਪਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਸੋਇ॥ ਜੋ ਕਿਛੁ ਕੀਨੋ ਸੁ ਅਪਨੈ ਰੰਗਿ॥ ਸਭ ਤੇ ਦੂਰਿ ਸਭਹੂ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ॥ ਬੂਝੈ ਦੇਖੈ ਕਰੈ ਬਿਬੇਕ॥ ਆਪਹਿ ਏਕ ਆਪਹਿ ਅਨੇਕ॥ ਮਰੈ ਨ ਬਿਨਸੈ ਆਵੈ ਨ ਜਾਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸਦ ਹੀ ਰਹਿਆ ਸਮਾਇ॥ 6॥

Kern kraavun kernaihaar(u). Iss kaiy haath(i) kuhaan beechaar(u). Jaiysi drisht(i) kuray taiysa hoyay. Aapay Aap(i) Aap(i) prubh(u) soyay. Jo kichh(u) keeno su Apnnaiy rung(i).  Subh tay door(i) subhoon kaiy sung(i).  Boojhaiy daykhaiy kuraiy bibayk.  Aapeh(i) aik, Aapeh(i) anayk.  Muraiy, na binsaiy, aavaiy na jaayay.  Nanak, sud hee ruhiya smaayay.  6. 

Word Meaning:   Kern = to do.   Kraavun = make others do.   Kernaiyhaar(u) = having the capability to do anything (Waheguru).    Iss = this (man).    Drisht(i) = sight.    Rung(i) = indifference toward the world.   Bibayk = distinction.   Binsaiy = vanishes.    6.

MEANING: If one thinks deeply, he will realize that the man has no control over anything; it is Waheguru that does everything and gets people to do whatever He wishes.   An individual behaves exactly the way the kind of sight Waheguru casts on him (in whatever way Waheguru wants him to behave) because it is Waheguru that operates everywhere.   He is close to everybody, yet detached from them, and He does everything in His own way. He watches, understands, and distinguishes between the good and the bad.   He is the only power in the universe (but being omnipresent), and He manifests Himself in countless ways.  He is eternal – never takes birth nor dies.  He is always present, oh Nanak.   6.

ਆਪਿ ਉਪਦੇਸੈ ਸਮਝੈ ਆਪਿ॥ ਆਪੇ ਰਚਿਆ ਸਭ ਕੈ ਸਾਥਿ॥ ਆਪਿ ਕੀਨੋ ਆਪਨ ਬਿਸਥਾਰੁ॥ ਸਭੁ ਕਛੁ ਉਸ ਕਾ ਓਹੁ ਕਰਨੈਹਾਰੁ॥ ਉਸ ਤੇ ਭਿੰਨ ਕਹਹੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਥਾਨ ਥਨੰਤਰਿ ਏਕੈ ਸੋਇ॥ ਅਪੁਨੇ ਚਲਿਤ ਆਪਿ ਕਰਣੈਹਾਰ॥ ਕਉਤਕ ਕਰੈ ਰੰਗ ਆਪਾਰ॥ ਮਨ ਮਹਿ ਆਪਿ ਮਨ ਅਪੁਨੇ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਕੀਮਤਿ ਕਹਨੁ ਨ ਜਾਇ॥ 7॥

Aap(i) oopdaysaiy sumjhaiy Aap(i).  Aapay ruchiya subh kaiy saath(i). Aap(i) keeno Aapun bisthaar(u).  Subh(u) kuchh(u) oos ka oh(u) kernaiyhaar(u). Oos tay bhinn kuhou kichh(u) hoyay. Thaan thununtar(i) aikaiy Soyay. Apunay chulit Aap(i) kernaiyhaar. Kaoutak kuraiy rung appar. Mun meh(i) Aap(i), mun Apunay maah(i).  Nanak keemit(i) kehn(u) na jaayay.   7.

Word Meaning:    Oopdaysaiy = preaches.   Ruchiya = mingled.    Bisthaar(u) = expanse.   Bhinn = separate.   Thaan Thununtar(i) = everywhere.    Chulit = Kaoutuk amazing feats.   Rung = colourful.     Aapaar = endless.    7.

MEANING: Waheguru is omniscient, and (being present in the Guru), He also is the preacher, and has mingled with everybody.   He is omnipotent, did His own expansion, and everything belongs to Him.  Nothing is independent of Him; He is present everywhere and in everything.  He is behind all amazing feats and performs them in countless, colourful ways.  He is present in every heart, and His thoughts are in all His creation.  No one can evaluate Him (find His limits), oh Nanak. 7.

ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਸੁਆਮੀ॥ ਗੁਰਪਰਸਾਦਿ ਕਿਨੈ ਵਖਿਆਨੀ॥ ਸਚੁ ਸਚੁ ਸਚੁ ਸਭੁ ਕੀਨਾ॥ ਕੋਟਿ ਮਧੇ ਕਿਨੈ ਬਿਰਲੈ ਚੀਨਾ॥ ਭਲਾ ਭਲਾ ਭਲਾ ਤੇਰਾ ਰੂਪ॥ ਅਤਿ ਸੁੰਦਰ ਅਪਾਰ ਅਨੂਪ॥ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਤੇਰੀ ਬਾਣੀ॥ ਘਟਿ ਘਟਿ ਸੁਨੀ ਸ੍ਰਵਨ ਬਖ੍ਹਾਣੀ॥ ਪਵਿਤ੍ਰ ਪਵਿਤ੍ਰ ਪਵਿਤ੍ਰ ਪੁਨੀਤ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪੈ ਨਾਨਕ ਮਨਿ ਪ੍ਰੀਤਿ॥ 8॥12॥

Sut(i), Sut(i), Sut(i) Prubh(u) Soami.  Gur persaad(i) kinaiy vukhiyaani.  Sach(u), Sach(u), Sach(u) subh(u) keena. Koat(i) mudhay kinaiy birlaiy cheena. Bhulaa, bhulaa, bhulaa; Tayra roop.  Ut(i) soondar apaar anoop.  Nirmul, nirmul, nirmul; Tayri Bani. Ghut(i) ghut(i) sooni, servun bukhaani. Puvittar, puvittar, puvittar, pooneet. Naam(u) jupaiy Nanak mun(i) preet(i).  8.12.

Word Meaning:  Sut(i) = eternal.   Soami = Lord.   Vukhiyaani = explained.   Sach(u) = truth; reality.   Keena = created.   Mudhay = in.    Birlaiy = rare.   Cheena = realized; experienced.  Roop = looks; personality.   Apaar = endless.  Anoop = unparalleled.    Baani = His Naam.   Bukhaani = described; explained.    Puvittar = Pooneet = holy; pious.  8.12.

MEANING:  The fact that the Lord, Waheguru, is eternal, is explained (internalized) by some rare individual by the grace of the Guru.  Everything that He has created is for real (some Hindu mystics professed that the world creation is an illusion), but only one in millions has recognized this fact.   Waheguru’s personality is very pleasant; He is extremely handsome, and His beauty is unparalleled (beyond any comparison). His 

Naam is extremely holy, and is heard (is present) in every heart, and is described by the Guru to be heard by the ears.   I recite Waheguru’s holy Naam with deep love and reverence, oh Nanak.    8.12.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਸੰਤ ਸਰਨਿ ਜੋ ਜਨੁ ਪਰੈ ਸੋ ਜਨੁ ਉਧਰਨਹਾਰ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੀ ਨਿੰਦਾ ਨਾਨਕਾ ਬਹੁਰਿ ਬਹੁਰਿ ਅਵਤਾਰ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Sunt shern(i) jo jun(u) puraiy, so jun(u) oodhrunhaar.  Sunt kee ninda Nanaka, buhour(i) buhour(i) avtar.  1. 

Word Meaning:  Jun(u) = devotee.   Oodhrunhaar = salvageable.   Ninda = malicious criticism; vilification.      Avtar = rebirths.     1.

MEANING:  A devotee, who takes the Guru’s shelter, attains salvation.   Vilification of the Guru causes the person to be born again and again, oh Nanak.   1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਆਰਜਾ ਘਟੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਜਮ ਤੇ ਨਹੀ ਛੁਟੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਸੁਖੁ ਸਭੁ ਜਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਨਰਕ ਮਹਿ ਪਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਮਤਿ ਹੋਇ ਮਲੀਨ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਸੋਭਾ ਤੇ ਹੀਨ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਹਤੇ ਕਉ ਰਖੈ ਨ ਕੋਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਥਾਨ ਭ੍ਰਸਟੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕ੍ਰਿਪਾਲ ਕ੍ਰਿਪਾ ਜੇ ਕਰੈ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੰਤ ਸੰਗਿ ਨਿੰਦਕੁ ਭੀ ਤਰੈ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi:  Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) aarja ghutaiy.  Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) Jum tay nuhee chhootaiy. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) sookh(u) subh(u) jaayay. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) nerk meh(i) paayay. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) mut(i) hoyay muleen. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) sobha tay heen.  Sunt kaiy hutay kuou rukhaiy na koyay. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) thaan bhrusht(u) hoyay.  Sunt kirpaal, kirpa jay kuraiy. Nanak Sunt sung(i) ninduk(u) bhee turaiy.   1.

Word Meaning:  Dookhun(i) = causing trouble (out of jealousy).     Aarja = life span.    Jum = angel of death.  Muleen = filthy.    Heen = without; devoid of.     Hutay = killed; condemned.   Rukhaiy = protect; help.   Thaan = heart; environment.     Bhrusht(u) = polluted.   Ninduk(u) = malicious critic; character assassin.    Turaiy = is salvaged.   1.

MEANING:  The life span of a man who causes trouble for the Guru out of jealousy is lowered, and the Angel of death does not let him go free.  Jealousy against the Guru takes away all the comforts of the aggressor, and his life becomes hellish.   His thinking becomes ignoble, and he becomes devoid of any fame.  Jealousy against the Guru not only pollutes the individual's heart but also the environment around him, and nobody can help the person who has been condemned by the Guru.  However, if the kind Guru shows kindness to His vilifier, even he finds salvation, oh Nanak.   1.

ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਤੇ ਮੁਖੁ ਭਵੈ॥ ਸੰਤਨ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਕਾਗ ਜਿਉ ਲਵੈ॥ ਸੰਤਨ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਸਰਪ ਜੋਨਿ ਪਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਤ੍ਰਿਗਦ ਜੋਨਿ ਕਿਰਮਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤਨ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਤ੍ਰਿਸਨਾ ਮਹਿ ਜਲੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਸਭੁ ਕੋ ਛਲੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਤੇਜੁ ਸਭੁ ਜਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੂਖਨਿ ਨੀਚੁ ਨੀਚਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਾ ਥਾਉ ਕੋ ਨਾਹਿ॥

(ਅੰਗ 280)॥  ਨਾਨਕ ਸੰਤ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਓਇ ਭੀ ਗਤਿ ਪਾਹਿ॥ 2॥

Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) tay mookh(u) bhuvaiy. Suntun kaiy dookhun(i) kaag jiyoun luvaiy. Suntun kaiy dookhun(i) serp joan(i) paayay. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) trigud joan(i) kirmaayay. Suntun kaiy dookhun(i) trishna meh(i) julaiy. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) subh(u) ko chhulaiy. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) tayj(u) subh(u) jaayay. Sunt kaiy dookhun(i) neech neechaayay.  Sunt doakhi ka thaaoun ko naah(i).

(PAGE 280):       Nanak Sunt bhaavaiy ta oyay bhee gut(i) paah(i).   2.

Word Meaning: Dookhun(i) = jealousy.  Mookh(u) Bhuvaiy = face turned away.     Kaag = crow.   Luvaiy = crows; talks gibberish.   Trigud Joan(i) = animals that crawl.    Trishna = burning desire for Maya.    Chhulaiy = deceives; cheats.   Tayj(u) = splendour.   Thaaoun = shelter (in Waheguru’s Court).   Gut(i) = higher spiritual state; salvation.    2.

MEANING: Jealousy with the Guru turns the face of the jealous away from the Guru (he becomes a non-believer and he cannot do any noble deed either), and he always talks gibberish.  Causing any trouble for the Guru results in the jealous person being born as a snake and as other creatures which crawl on the ground (he stoops so low in morality that he becomes vicious like a snake, and acts so low that he cannot fly high in spirits like the crawling creatures).  The individual, jealous of the Guru, suffers in the fire of desires and tries to cheat everybody.  He loses all his splendour (if he had any) and becomes the lowest of the lowly.  There is no shelter for the jealous (in Waheguru’s Court); however, if the Guru wishes, he too is salvaged, oh Nanak.   2.

ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਨਿੰਦਕੁ ਮਹਾ ਅਤਿਤਾਈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਨਿੰਦਕੁ ਖਿਨੁ ਟਿਕਨੁ ਨਾ ਪਾਈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਨਿੰਦਕੁ ਮਹਾ ਹਤਿਆਰਾ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਨਿੰਦਕੁ ਪਰਮੇਸੁਰਿ ਮਾਰਾ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਨਿੰਦਕੁ ਰਾਜ ਤੇ ਹੀਨੁ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਨਿੰਦਕੁ ਦੁਖੀਆ ਅਰੁ ਦੀਨੁ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਨਿੰਦਕ ਕਉ ਸਰਬ ਰੋਗ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਨਿੰਦਕ ਕਉ ਸਦਾ ਬਿਜੋਗ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੀ ਨਿੰਦਾ ਦੋਖ ਮਹਿ ਦੋਖੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੰਤ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਉਸ ਕਾ ਭੀ ਹੋਇ ਮੋਖੁ॥ 3॥

Sunt ka ninduk(u) muha ut-taayee.  Sunt ka ninduk(u) khin(u) ticken(u) na paayee.  Sunt ka ninduk(u) muha hutiyaara. Sunt ka ninduk(u) Permayshur(i) maara. Sunt ka ninduk(u) raj tay heen. Sunt ka ninduk(u) dookhiya ur(u) deen(u).  Sunt kay ninduk kuou serb roag.  Sunt kay ninduk kuou suda bijoag.  Sunt kee ninda doakh meh(i) doakh(u).  Nanak Sunt bhaavaiy, ta oos ka bhee hoyay moakh(u) .   

Word Meaning: Ut-taayee = extremist; trouble maker.  Ticken(u) = peaceful    Hutiyaara = killer; sinner.   Maara = condemned.   Raj = control over senses. Heen = devoid of.     Deen(u) = helpless.   Bijoag = separation; isolation.   Doakh = blame; sin.   3.

MEANING:  The character-assassin of the Guru is a big trouble-maker and does not rest peacefully, even for a small moment. Such an individual is a big sinner, and is condemned by Waheguru.   The slanderer of the Guru has no control over his senses and suffers in discomfort and becomes helpless.  The vilifier of the Guru suffers from all ailments, and also from isolation (no moral person wants to be friends with him).   Vilifying the Guru is the greatest sin; however, even such an individual can find salvation if the Guru wishes, oh Nanak.   3.

ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਸਦਾ ਅਪਵਿਤੁ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਿਸੈ ਕਾ ਨਹੀ ਮਿਤੁ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਉ ਡਾਨੁ ਲਾਗੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਉ ਸਭ ਤਿਆਗੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਮਹਾ ਅਹੰਕਾਰੀ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਸਦਾ ਬਿਕਾਰੀ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਜਨਮੈ ਮਰੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੀ ਦੂਖਨਾ ਸੁਖ ਤੇ ਟਰੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਉ ਨਹੀ ਠਾਉ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੰਤ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਲਏ ਮਿਲਾਇ॥ 4॥

Sunt ka doakhi suda upvit(u).  Sunt ka doakhi kisaiy ka nuhee mit(u).  Sunt kay doakhi kuou daan(u) laagaiy. Sunt kay doakhi kuou subh tiyaagaiy. Sunt ka doakhi muha ahunkaari.  Sunt ka doakhi suda bikaari.  Sunt ka doakhi junmaiy muraiy.  Sunt kee dookhna sookh tay turaiy.  Sunt kay doakhi kuou nuhee tthaaoun.  Nanak Sunt bhaavaiy ta luyay milaayay.   4.

Word Meaning:  Doakhi = opponent; jealous.  Upvit = unholy; impious.    Daan(u) = punishment.   Tiyaagaiy = abandon.   Bikaari = lustful; immoral.   Turaiy = is passed by.   Tthaaoun = shelter.  4.

MEANING:  The jealousy of the Guru is always impious, and in fact, is nobody’s friend.  Such an individual is punished in many ways, and everybody abandons him.  The malicious critic of the Guru is highly conceited and is always lustful.  All comforts pass by the individual who vilifies the Guru, and he is caught in the rebirth cycle.  There is no place (shelter) for such an individual in Waheguru’s Court (he is not allowed in there); however, if the Guru wishes, He can connect even such a mean and jealous person with Waheguru, oh Nanak.   4.

ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਅਧ ਬੀਚ ਤੇ ਟੂਟੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਿਤੈ ਕਾਜਿ ਨ ਪਹੂਚੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਉ ਉਦਿਆਨ ਭ੍ਰਮਾਈਐ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਉਝੜਿ ਪਾਈਐ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਅੰਤਰ ਤੇ ਥੋਥਾ॥ ਜਿਉ ਸਾਸ ਬਿਨਾ ਮਿਰਤਕ ਕੀ ਲੋਥਾ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕੀ ਜੜ ਕਿਛੁ ਨਾਹਿ॥ ਆਪਨ ਬੀਜਿ ਆਪੇ ਹੀ ਖਾਹਿ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਉ ਅਵਰੁ ਨ ਰਾਖਨਹਾਰੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੰਤ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਲਏ ਉਬਾਰਿ॥ 5॥

Sunt ka doakhi udh beech tay tootaiy. Sunt ka doakhi kitaiy kaaj(i) na puhoochaiy. Sunt kay doakhi kuou oodiyaan bhermaayiyaiy.   Sunt ka doakhi oojhurr(i) paayiyaiy.  Sunt ka doakhi untar tay thoatha. Jiyoun saas bina mirtuk kee loatha.  Sunt kay doakhi kee jurrh kichh(u) naah(i).  Aapun beej(i) aapay hee khaah(i).  Sunt kay doakhi kuou uver(u) na raakhunhaar(u). Nanak Sunt bhaavaiy ta luyay oobaar(i).  5.

Word Meaning:  Udh Beech = in the middle.   Tootaiy = cut down.    Kaaj(i) = task; efforts (to malign the Guru).   Puhoochaiy = gets completed.    Oodiyaan Bhermaayiyaiy = Oojhurr(i) Paayiyaiy = is misled from the real objective like a man in the wilderness. .  Thoatha = hollow.   Loatha = corpse.   Jurrh = root; base; support.   Uver(u) = other; else.  Oobaar(i) = salvage.    5.

MEANING:  The jealousy of the Guru does not succeed in any of his efforts to malign the Guru, as all of his mean efforts are cut down in the middle.  The maligner of the Guru is misguided, like an individual lost in the wilderness without a guide.  He is hollow inside (no sound or firm mind) like a corpse which has no breath.  The maligner of the Guru has no support; he reaps what he has sown. But if the Guru wishes, He can also salvage his maligner.  But nobody else can help him in any way, oh Nanak.  5.

ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਇਉ ਬਿਲਲਾਇ॥ ਜਿਉ ਜਲ ਬਿਹੂਨ ਮਛੁਲੀ ਤੜਫੜਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਭੂਖਾ ਨਹੀ ਰਾਜੈ॥ ਜਿਉ ਪਾਵਕੁ ਈਧਨਿ ਨਹੀ ਧ੍ਰਾਪੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਛੁਟੈ ਇਕੇਲਾ॥ ਜਿਉ ਬੂਆੜੁ ਤਿਲੁ ਖੇਤ ਮਾਹਿ ਦੁਹੇਲਾ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਧਰਮ ਤੇ ਰਹਤ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਸਦ ਮਿਥਿਆ ਕਹਤ॥ ਕਿਰਤੁ ਨਿੰਦਕ ਕਾ ਧੁਰਿ ਹੀ ਪਇਆ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜੋ ਭਾਵੈ ਸੋਈ ਥਿਆ॥ 6॥

Sunt ka doakhi iyoun bil-laayay. Jiyoun jul bihoon muchhuli turrphurrayay. Sunt ka doakhi bhookha nuhee raajay. Jiyoun paavuk eedhun(i) nuhee dhraapaiy. Sunt ka doakhi chhootaiy ikayla.  Jiyoun booaarr(u) til(u) khayt maah(i) doohayla.  Sunt ka doakhi dherm tay reht. Sud ka doakhi sud mithiya keht.  Kirt(u) ninduk ka Dhoor(i) hee puyya. Nanak jo bhaavaiy, soyee thiya. 

Word Meaning: Bil-laayay = wails; suffers.   Bihoon = without.   Turrphurrayay = 

wriggles; struggles (to escape).    Paavuk = fire.   Eedhun(i) = fuel.     Dhraapaiy = satisfied.  Ikayla = alone.   Booaarr(u) Til = a sesame plant with ash in its pods.   Doohayla = in discomfort.   Dherm = moral principles.  Reht = without; empty.  Mithiya = lies.       Kirt(u) = character; behaviour.    Dhoor(i) = right from the source (Waheguru).   Thiya = happens.   6.

MEANING:  The maligner of the Guru wails (cries for help) like a fish which wriggles for survival without water.  Like fire, which is never tired of consuming fuel, the maligner of the guru never feels satisfied with his big hunger (desires) for worldly things. Like a sesame plant that feels unhappy when left alone in the field because its pods are filled with ash rather than seeds, the maligner of the Guru also suffers the pain of being left alone (no friends to help him).  Such an individual has no moral principles and lies all the time.  The character of the maligner had been designed by Waheguru, and only that happens whatever He (God) wishes, oh Nanak.  6.

ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਬਿਗੜ ਰੂਪੁ ਹੋਇ ਜਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕਉ ਦਰਗਹ ਮਿਲੈ ਸਜਾਇ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਸਦਾ ਸਹਕਾਈਐ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਮਰੈ ਨ ਜੀਵਾਈਐ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੇ ਦੋਖੀ ਕੀ ਪੁਜੈ ਨ ਆਸਾ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕਾ ਦੋਖੀ ਉਠਿ ਚਲੈ ਨਿਰਾਸਾ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕੈ ਦੋਖਿ ਨਾ ਤ੍ਰਿਸਟੈ ਕੋਇ॥ ਜੈਸਾ ਭਾਵੈ ਤੈਸਾ ਕੋਈ ਹੋਇ॥ ਪਇਆ ਕਿਰਤੁ ਨ ਮੇਟੈ ਕੋਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਾਨੈ ਸਚਾ ਸੋਇ॥ 7॥

Sunt ka doakhi bigurr roop(u) hoyay jaayay.  Sunt kay doakhi kuou Durgeh milaiy sujaayay. Sunt ka doakhi suda sehkaayiyaiy.  Sunt ka doakhi muraiy na jeevaayiyaiy.  Sunt kay doakhi kee poojaiy na aasa.  Sunt ka doakhi ootth(i) chulaiy niraasa.  Sunt kaiy doakh(i) na trishtaiy koyay.  Jaiysa bhaavaiy taiysa koyee hoyay. Puyya kirt(u) na maytaiy koyay. Nanak jaanaiy Sacha soyay.   7.

Word Meaning: Bigurr = distorted; deformed.  Roop(u) = image; personality.  Sehkaayiyaiy = struggle for breath; uneasy life.   Poojaiy = fulfilled.   Ootth(i) Chulaiy = leaves the world; dies.   Niraasa = disappointed.   Trishtaiy = contented.   Kirt(u) = character; thinking.      Maytaiy = erase; obliterate.   7.

MEANING:  The personality (thinking) of the malicious critic of the Guru undergoes deformation (gets twisted), and he is punished in Waheguru’s Court.  The maligner of the Guru has a very uneasy life, and he is like a half-dead man.  His desires go unfulfilled, and he leaves the world disappointed.  Nobody ever gets contentment by maligning the Guru.  An individual inherits the character given to him by Waheguru, and no one can ever erase it through their own efforts.  Only Waheguru knows this secret, oh Nanak.  7. 

ਸਭ ਘਟ ਤਿਸ ਕੇ ਓਹੁ ਕਰਨੈਹਾਰੁ॥ ਸਦਾ ਸਦਾ ਤਿਸ ਕਉ ਨਮਸਕਾਰੁ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੀ ਉਸਤਤਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਦਿਨੁ ਰਾਤਿ॥ ਤਿਸਹਿ ਧਿਆਵਹੁ ਸਾਸਿ ਗਿਰਾਸਿ॥ ਸਭੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਵਰਤੈ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਕੀਆ॥ ਜੈਸਾ ਕਰੇ ਤੈਸਾ ਕੋ ਥੀਆ॥ ਅਪਨਾ ਖੇਲੁ ਆਪਿ ਕਰਨੈਹਾਰੁ॥ ਦੂਸਰ ਕਉਨੁ ਕਹੈ ਬੀਚਾਰੁ॥

(ਅੰਗ 281)॥  ਜਿਸ ਨੋ ਕ੍ਰਿਪਾ ਕਰੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਆਪਨ ਨਾਮੁ ਦੇਇ॥ ਬਡਭਾਗੀ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਨ ਸੇਇ॥ 8॥13॥

Subh ghut Tis kay, Ohu kernaiyhaar(u).  Suda suda Tis kuou numuskaar.  Prubh kee oostut(i) kurhou din(u) raat(i).  Tiseh(i) dhiyaavhou saas(i) giraas(i).  Subh kichh(u) vertaiy Tis ka keeya. Jaiysa kuray taiysa ko theeya. Apuna khayl(u) Aap(i) kernaiyhaar(u). Doosur kaoun(u), kuhaiy beechaa(u). 

(PAGE 281):       Jis nu kirpa kuraiy, tis(u) Aapun Naam(u) dayay.  Budbhaagee 

Nanak jun sayay.  8.13.

Word Meaning:   Tis Kay = of Waheguru.   Oostut(i) = praise.   Giraas(i) = bite; nibble.   Khayl(u) = drama.    8.13.

MEANING: Waheguru is the creator of everything, and all the hearts are His (He is present in each of them); I bow to Him all the time.  Sing Waheguru’s praise day and night, and remember Him with every breath and with every nibble of food, oh my friend.  Everything that is happening is His creation (He controls it), and an individual inherits the behaviour He gives.  Waheguru Himself is the creator of the drama of the world, and no one can even think about His drama (plans). Waheguru gives His Naam to embed in the heart of those whom He is kind to.  Such individuals are highly fortunate, oh Nanak. 8.13.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਤਜਹੁ ਸਿਆਨਪ ਸੁਰਿਜਨਹੁ ਸਿਮਰਹੁ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਰਾਇ॥ ਏਕ ਆਸ ਹਰਿ ਮਨਿ ਰਖਹੁ ਨਾਨਕ ਦੂਖੁ ਭਰਮੁ ਭਉ ਜਾਇ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Tujhou siyaanup soorjunhou, simerhou Hur(i) Hur(i) Raayay. Aik aas Hur(i) mun(i) rukhou, Nanak dookh(u) bherm(u) bhuou jaayay.  1.

Word Meaning:   Tujhou = give up.      Siyaanup = cleverness.    Soorjunhou = god-like noble folks.     Aas = dependence.   Bherm(u) = misgivings.     Bhuou = fears.    1.

MEANING:  Give up (get rid of) your own cleverness (shrewdness), and recite Waheguru’s Naam, oh noble folks.  Rest your hope (dependence) only on Waheguru, and get rid of your discomforts, misgivings, and all fears thereby, oh Nanak.  1.   

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਮਾਨੁਖ ਕੀ ਟੇਕ ਬ੍ਰਿਥੀ ਸਭ ਜਾਨੁ॥ ਦੇਵਨ ਕਉ ਏਕੈ ਭਗਵਾਨੁ॥ ਜਿਸ ਕੈ ਦੀਐ ਰਹੈ ਅਘਾਇ॥ ਬਹੁਰਿ ਨ ਤ੍ਰਿਸਨਾ ਲਾਗੈ ਆਇ॥ ਮਾਰੈ ਰਾਖੈ ਏਕੋ ਆਪਿ॥ ਮਾਨੁਖ ਕੈ ਕਿਛੁ ਨਾਹੀ ਹਾਥਿ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਹੁਕਮੁ ਬੂਝਿ ਸੁਖੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਨਾਮੁ ਰਖੁ ਕੰਠਿ ਪਰੋਇ॥ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਸੋਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਬਿਘਨੁ ਨ ਲਾਗੈ ਕੋਇ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Manookh kee tayk birthee subh jaan(u).  Dayvun kuou aikaiy Bhugvaan(u).  Jis kaiy deeyaiy ruhaiy aghaayay. Buhour(i) na trishna laagaiy aayay. Maaraiy, raakhaiy, Aiko Aap(i). Manookh kaiy kichh(u) naahi haath(i).  Tis ka Huqam(u) boojh(i) sookh(u) hoyay.  Tis ka Naam(u) rukh kuntth(i) proyay. Simer(i), simer(i), simer(i) Prubh(u) soyay.  Nanak, bighun(u) na laagaiy koyay.   1.

Word Meaning:   Tayk = support; dependence.    Birthi = hollow; unsound.   Aghaayay = contented.    Trishana = desire for worldly possessions.    Boojh(i) = understanding; obeying.   Kuntth(i) = (with the) neck.   Proayay = stitched.    Bighun(u) = obstacle; hindrance.   1.

MEANING: Realize the fact that dependence on anybody (other than Waheguru) is hollow (unreliable), oh my friends; Waheguru is the only Provider.  One is content with His largesse, and he has no further desires.  Waheguru alone protects you and causes death; the man has no control over anything.  The man lives in comfort by obeying His Huqam; so, stitch His Naam to your neck (recite His Naam with the tongue). By remembering Waheguru, one experiences no hindrances on the path to union with God, oh Nanak.   1.

ਉਸਤਤਿ ਮਨ ਮਹਿ ਕਰਿ ਨਿਰੰਕਾਰ॥ ਕਰਿ ਮਨ ਮੇਰੇ ਸਤਿ ਬਿਉਹਾਰ॥ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਰਸਨਾ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤੁ ਪੀਉ॥ ਸਦਾ ਸੁਹੇਲਾ ਕਰਿ ਲੇਹਿ ਜੀਉ॥ ਨੈਨਹੁ ਪੇਖੁ ਠਾਕੁਰ ਕਾ ਰੰਗੁ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਬਿਨਸੈ ਸਭ ਸੰਗੁ॥ ਚਰਨ ਚਲਉ ਮਾਰਗਿ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ॥ ਮਿਟਹਿ ਪਾਪ ਜਪੀਐ ਹਰਿ ਬਿੰਦ॥ ਕਰ ਹਰਿ ਕਰਮ ਸ੍ਰਵਨਿ ਹਰਿ ਕਥਾ॥ ਹਰਿ ਦਰਗਹ ਨਾਨਕ ਊਜਲ ਮਥਾ॥ 2॥

Oostutu(i) mun meh(i) kur(i) Nirunkaar.  Kur(i) mun mayray Sat biyohaar.  Nirmul rusna, Amrit peeyo. Suda soohayla kur(i) layhou jeeo.  Nainhou paykh(u) Tthaakur ka rung(u).  Sadh sung(i) binsaiy subh sung(u).  Churn chulou maarug(i) Gobind.  Miteh(i) paap, jupiyaiy Hur(i) bind.  Ker Hur(i) kerm, servun(i) Hur(i) kutha.  Hur(i) Durgeh Nanak oojul mutha.   2.

Word Meaning:   Oostut(i) = praise.   Nirunkaar = formless Waheguru.    Sat(i) = everlasting.    Biyohaar = business; deal.    Rusna = tongue.    Soohayla = blissful.    Naiynhou = (with) eyes.     Paykh(u) = see.     Rung(u) = colour: beauty of nature.     Sung(i) = (in the) company.    Sung(u) = company; attachment.    Churn = feet.   Maarug(i) = on the path.   Bind = moment.   Ker = hands.    Kerm = deeds; service.    Servun = ears.     Kutha = story (of virtues).     Oojul = shiny; unblemished.     Mutha = forehead; face.    2.

MEANING: Praise Waheguru in your mind; this is the real (everlasting) deal, oh my mind.  With a pure mind, drink the Amrit of Waheguru’s Naam with your tongue, and make your life blissful forever.  Enjoy the beauty of nature as created by Waheguru with your eyes, but take the shelter of the Guru in whose company all your worldly attachments will vanish. Walk with your feet on the path which leads to union with Waheguru.  All sins are washed away when we recite His Naam (with a pure mind), even if for a moment. When a devotee serves Waheguru (His people) with hands, and listens to His virtues with his ears, he goes to Waheguru’s Court with a shiny (unblemished) face, oh Nanak.   2.

ਬਡਭਾਗੀ ਤੇ ਜਨ ਜਗ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਸਦਾ ਸਦਾ ਹਰਿ ਕੇ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਹਿ॥ ਰਾਮ ਨਾਮ ਜੋ ਕਰਹਿ ਬੀਚਾਰ॥ ਸੇ ਧਨਵੰਤ ਗਨੀ ਸੰਸਾਰ॥ ਮਨਿ ਤਨਿ ਮੁਖਿ ਬੋਲਹਿ ਹਰਿ ਮੁਖੀ॥ ਸਦਾ ਸਦਾ ਜਾਨਹੁ ਤੇ ਸੁਖੀ॥ ਏਕੋ ਏਕੁ ਏਕੁ ਪਛਾਨੈ॥ ਇਤ ਉਤ ਕੀ ਓਹੁ ਸੋਝੀ ਜਾਨੈ॥ ਨਾਮ ਸੰਗਿ ਜਿਸ ਕਾ ਮਨੁ ਮਾਨਿਆ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਨਹਿ ਨਿਰੰਜਨੁ ਜਾਨਿਆ॥ 3॥

Budbhaagi tay jun jug maah(i). Suda, suda Hur(i) kay goon gaah(i).  Ram Naam jo kureh(i) beechaar. Say dhunvunt gunee sunsaar. Mun(i), tun(i) mookh(i) boleh(i) Hur(i) mookhi. Suda, suda jaanhou tay sookhi.  Aiko, Aik(u) Aik(u) puchhaanaiy. It oot kee oh(u) sojhee jaanaiy.  Naam sung(i) jis ka mun(u) maaniya.  Nanak, tinheh Nirunjun jaaniya.   3.

Word Meaning:   Tay Jun = those devotees.    Dhunvunt = wealthy.    Mookhi = Leader; Lord.   Tay = those.    Puchhaanaiy = recognize.   It Oot = here and there; this world and the next world.   Sojhi = understanding; knowledge.    Maaniya = won over; became comfortable.   Nirunjun = Waheguru.   Jaaniya = realized.   3. 

MEANING:  The devotees, who sing Waheguru’s praise all the time, are very lucky in the world; and they are truly wealthy who ponder on Waheguru’s Naam.  Those who recite the Naam of the Lord, Waheguru, with their body and mind, are always blissful.  Those who recognize (make a connection with) Waheguru acquire the knowledge of this world and the next (what to do here, and what happens there).  Those whose minds are won over by Waheguru realize (get connected with) the Waheguru which is above the effects of Maya, oh Nanak.   3.

ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਆਪਨ ਆਪੁ ਸੁਝੈ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੀ ਜਾਨਹੁ ਤ੍ਰਿਸਨਾ ਬੁਝੈ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਜਸੁ ਕਹਤ॥ ਸਰਬ ਰੋਗ ਤੇ ਓਹੁ ਹਰਿ ਜਨੁ ਰਹਤ॥ ਅਨਦਿਨੁ ਕੀਰਤਨ ਕੇਵਲ ਬਖ੍ਹਾਨੁ॥ ਗ੍ਰਿਹਸਤ ਮਹਿ ਸੋਈ ਨਿਰਬਾਨੁ॥ ਏਕ ਊਪਰਿ ਜਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਕੀ ਆਸਾ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੀ ਕਟੀਐ ਜਮ ਕੀ ਫਾਸਾ॥ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਕੀ ਜਿਸੁ ਮਨਿ ਭੂਖ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਸਹਿ ਨ ਲਾਗਹਿ ਦੂਖ॥ 4॥

Gur persaad(i) aapun aap(u) soojhaiy. Tis kee jaanhou trishna boojhaiy.  Sadh sung(i) Hur(i) Hur(i) jus(u) keht.  Serb roag tay oh(u) Hur(i) jun(u) reht.  Undin(u) keertun kayvul bukhaan(u).  Grihst meh(i) soyee nirbaan(u).  Aik oopur(i) jis(u) jun kee aasa.  Tis kee kutiyaiy Jum kee phaasa.  Parbraham kee jis mun(i) bhookh. Nanak tiseh(i) na laageh(i) dookh.   4.

Word Meaning:   Soojhaiy = understanding of.    Trishna = fire of desires.   Bukhaan(u) = recitation.    Nirbaan(u) = detached from Maya.   Aasa = hope; dependence.    Phaasa = noose; lasso     4.

MEANING: An individual who understands self (self-realization) in the shelter of the Guru gets the fire of his worldly desires doused.  An individual who recites Waheguru’s praise in the company of holy men becomes free from all ailments. An individual who recites Waheguru’s Naam day and night is the devotee who is really detached from Maya while living in the family.  An individual who rests his hopes only on the “One” gets the noose of the Angel of death cut for him.  An individual who has an appetite for Waheguru’s Naam in his mind never suffers from any discomfort, oh Nanak.    4.   

 

ਜਿਸ ਕਉ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਮਨਿ ਚਿਤਿ ਆਵੈ॥ ਸੋ ਸੰਤੁ ਸੁਹੇਲਾ ਨਹੀ ਡੁਲਾਵੈ॥ ਜਿਸ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਅਪੁਨਾ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਕਰੈ॥ ਸੋ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਕਹੁ ਕਿਸ ਤੇ ਡਰੈ॥ ਜੈਸਾ ਸਾ ਤੈਸਾ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਾਇਆ॥ ਅਪੁਨੇ ਕਾਰਜ ਮਹਿ ਆਪਿ ਸਮਾਇਆ॥ ਸੋਧਤ ਸੋਧਤ ਸੋਧਤ ਸੀਝਿਆ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਤਤੁ ਸਭੁ ਬੂਝਿਆ॥ ਜਬ ਦੇਖਉ ਤਬ ਸਭੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਮੂਲੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੋ ਸੂਖਮੁ ਸੋਈ ਅਸਥੂਲੁ॥ 5॥

Jis kuou Hur(i) Prubh(u) mun(i) chit(i) aavaiy.  So Sunt(u) soohayla, nuhee doolaavaiy. Jis Prubh apuna kirpa kuraiy. So sayvuk(u) kuhou kis tay duraiy? Jaiysa sa, taiysa drishtaaya. Apunay kaaruj meh(i) Aap(i) smaaya.  Sodhut, sodhut, sodhut seejhiya. Gur persaad(i) tut(u) subh(u) boojhiya.  Jub daykhoon, tub subh kichh(u) Mool(u). Nanak So sookhum, Soyee usthool.  5.

Word Meaning:   Soohayla = blissful.    Doolaavaiy = wavering (weakening) of the mind.   Drishtaaya = showed; presented.    Kaaruj = task; objective.   Smaaya = present; doing the job Himsel.  Sodhut = pondering on deeply.  Seejhiya = succeeded; realized.  Tut(u) = Truth; root.   Boojhiya = found out.   So = that (Waheguru).    Sookhum = minuscule; microscopic.    Usthhool = huge; enormous.   5.

MEANING:  A devotee, who remembers Waheguru in his mind, is a real saint who is blissful, and does not allow his mind to weaken (has full control on mind).  A devotee, whom Waheguru makes His own, is not afraid of anyone.  The devotee of Waheguru should look like Him, and Waheguru does adorn him to look like that (for example, fearless, kind, indiscriminating, etc.); and Waheguru achieves His objective (of adorning His devotees) Himself (without any assistance from anyone).   Pondering deeply on the amazing feats of Waheguru in the shelter of the Guru, I found out the reality that no matter where I look, it is Waheguru at the root of all happenings – He Himself is in the microscopic and Himself in the macroscopic forms, oh Nanak.  5.

ਨਹ ਕਿਛੁ ਜਨਮੈ ਨਹ ਕਿਛੁ ਮਰੈ॥ ਆਪਨ ਚਲਿਤੁ ਆਪ ਹੀ ਕਰੈ॥ ਆਵਨੁ ਜਾਵਨੁ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਅਨਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ॥ ਆਗਿਆਕਾਰੀ ਧਾਰੀ ਸਭ ਸ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ॥

(ਅੰਗ 282)॥  ਆਪੇ ਆਪਿ ਸਗਲ ਮਹਿ ਆਪਿ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਜੁਗਤਿ ਰਚਿ ਥਾਪਿ ਉਥਾਪਿ॥ ਅਬਿਨਾਸੀ ਨਾਹੀ ਕਿਛੁ ਖੰਡ॥ ਧਾਰਣਿ ਧਾਰਿ ਰਹਿਓ ਬ੍ਰਹਮੰਡ॥ ਅਲਖ ਅਭੇਵ ਪੁਰਖ ਪਰਤਾਪ॥ ਆਪਿ ਜਪਾਏ ਤਾ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਾਪ॥6॥

Neh kichh(u) junmaiy, neh kichh(u) muraiy.  Aapun chulit Aap hee kuraiy.  Aavun, jaavun, drisht(i) undrisht(i).  Aagiyakaari dhaari subh srisht(i). 

(PAGE 282):    Aapay Aap(i) sugul meh(i) Aap(i). Anik joogut(i) ruch(i) thaap(i) oothaap(i). Abinaashi, naahi kichh(u) khund.  Dhaarun(i) dhaar(i) ruhiyo brehmund.  Alukh, abhayv Poorakh pertaap.  Aap(i) jupaayay ta Nanak jaap.   6.

Word Meaning: Chulit = amazing feats.  Drisht(i) = visible.   Aagiyakaari = in His Huqam.   Dhaari = supported; created.   Srisht(i) = world.   Joogut(i) = ways.   Thaap(i) = create.  Oothaap(i) = destroy.    Abinaashi = eternal; immortal.    Khund = parts; intervals.    Brehmund = universe.   Alukh = incomprehensible.   Abhayv = whose secrets cannot be known; indeterminable.   Pertaap = magnificence.    6.

MEANING: (The soul being immortal as a part of Waheguru), there is nothing that really takes birth, and nothing really dies.  These are just amazing feats of Waheguru which He has generated.  Birth, death, visible, and invisible in the world have been created by the Huqam of Waheguru.  Waheguru is present in all of His creation, and creates and destroys it in many different ways.  Waheguru is immortal (eternal) and omnipresent, and does not exist in intervals or in certain places only (as some people believe). He has created and is supporting the whole universe.  He is incomprehensible, indeterminable, and omnipresent with great magnificence. Only that person recites His Naam whom He prompts Himself, oh Nanak.   6.

ਜਿਨ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਜਾਤਾ ਸੁ ਸੋਭਾਵੰਤ॥ ਸਗਲ ਸੰਸਾਰੁ ਉਧਰੈ ਤਿਨ ਮੰਤ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਸਗਲ ਉਧਾਰਨ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਦੂਖ ਬਿਸਾਰਨ॥ ਆਪੇ ਮੇਲਿ ਲਏ ਕਿਰਪਾਲ॥ ਗੁਰ ਕਾ ਸਬਦੁ ਜਪਿ ਭਏ ਨਿਹਾਲ॥ ਉਨ ਕੀ ਸੇਵਾ ਸੋਈ ਲਾਗੈ॥ ਜਿਸ ਨੋ ਕ੍ਰਿਪਾ ਕਰਹਿ ਬਡਭਾਗੈ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਤ ਪਾਵਹਿ ਬਿਸ੍ਰਾਮੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਨ ਪੁਰਖ ਕਉ ਊਤਮ ਕਰਿ ਮਾਨੁ॥ 7॥

Jin Prubh(u) jaata, su sobhavunt.  Sugul sunsaar(u) oodhraiy tin munt.  Prubh kay sayvuk sugul oodhaarun.  Prubh kay sayvuk dookh bisaarun.  Aapay mayl(i) luyay Kirpaal.  Gur ka Shabad(u) jup(i) bhuyay nihaal.  Oon kee sayva soyee laagaiy.  Jis nu kirpa kureh(i) budbhaagaiy.  Naam(u) juput paaveh(i) bisraam(u).  Nanak tin Poorakh kuou ootum kur(i) maan(u).    7.

Word Meaning: Sobhavunt = praise worthy.    Munt = mantra; Teachings.  Oodharun = salvager.      Bisaarun = remover; destroyer.   Nihaal = blissful.   Bisraam = peace; freedom from rebirths.   Purakh = men; devotees.   Ootum = superior.   7.

MEANING:  Those devotees who have realized Waheguru are worthy of praise, and the whole world can find salvation by acting on their Teachings.  The devotees of Waheguru (do not discriminate between people) and work for the salvation of everybody. They are the destroyers of all discomfort.  They feel blissful by reciting the Guru’s Shabad, and Waheguru Himself connects them with Him.  Only those highly fortunate individuals who are kind to Waheguru get to serve them.   They find rest (get free from the cycle of rebirths) by reciting Waheguru’s Naam. Such individuals are acknowledged as being highly superior (holy – connected with God), oh Nanak.   7.

ਜੋ ਕਿਛੁ ਕਰੈ ਸੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੈ ਰੰਗਿ॥ ਸਦਾ ਸਦਾ ਬਸੈ ਹਰਿ ਸੰਗਿ॥ ਸਹਜ ਸੁਭਾਇ ਹੋਵੈ ਸੋ ਹੋਇ॥ ਕਰਣੈਹਾਰੁ ਪਛਾਣੈ ਸੋਇ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕਾ ਕੀਆ ਜਨ ਮੀਠ ਲਗਾਨਾ॥ ਜੈਸਾ ਸਾ ਤੈਸਾ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਾਨਾ॥ ਜਿਸ ਤੇ ਉਪਜੇ ਤਿਸੁ ਮਾਹਿ ਸਮਾਏ॥ ਓਇ ਸੁਖ ਨਿਧਾਨ ਉਨਹੂ ਬਨਿ ਆਏ॥ ਆਪਸ ਕਉ ਆਪਿ ਦੀਨੋ ਮਾਨੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਜਨੁ ਏਕੋ ਜਾਨੁ॥ 8॥14॥

Jo kichh(u) kuraiy, su Prubh kaiy rung(i).  Suda, suda busaiy Hur(i) sung(i).  Sehj soobhaayay hovaiy so hoyay.  Kernaiyhaar(u) puchhaanaiy soyay.  Prubh ka keeya jun meetth lugaana.  Jaiysa sa taiysa drishtaana.  Jis tay oopjay, Tis(u) maah(i) smaayay. Oye nidhaan, oonhoo bun(i) aayay.  Aapus kuou Aap(i) deeno maan(u). Nanak Prubh jun(u) aiko jaan(u).    8.14.

Word Meaning:  Rung(i) = in love of.    Sejh Soobhaayay = without any special effort.   Kernaiyhaar = the Creator (Waheguru).  Puchhanaiy = recognize; get connected with.     Drishtaana = is seen.    Nidhaan = treasure; source.   Bun(i) Aayay = suits; deserve.   Maan(u) = honour; recognition.   Jaan(u) = think of.   8.14.

MEANING: Whatever a devotee does, he does that in the love for God, and Waheguru is always with him.  He accepts things as they happen naturally without putting in any special effort. Such a devotee recognizes (has a close connection with) Waheguru.  Whatever happens, the devotee accepts that gladly as the sweet Will of Waheguru, and there is no show off on the part of the devotee for that achievement; what he seems outwardly, he is really that inwardly too (his words and actions are the same).  Such devotees get fully absorbed in Waheguru, who created them.  Only the true devotees deserve such blissfulness.   Waheguru’s devotees are “one” with Him, so when Waheguru honours His devotees, He is, in a way, bestowing that honour on Himself, oh Nanak.   8.14.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਸਰਬ ਕਲਾ ਭਰਪੂਰ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਬਿਰਥਾ ਜਾਨਨਹਾਰ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਸਿਮਰਨਿ ਉਧਰੀਐ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਸੁ ਬਲਿਹਾਰ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Serb kulaa bhurpoor Prubh birtha jaanunhaar.  Ja kaiy simrun(i) oodhuriyaiy,  Nanak Tis bulihaar.   1.

Word Meaning:   Kula Bhurpoor  = full of all capabilities; omnipotent.    Bhurpoor =  filling the whole universe (omnipresent).   Birtha = condition; mental state.   Jaanunhaar = omniscient.    Oodhuriyaiy = salvation.   1.

MEANING:  The omnipotent Waheguru is present everywhere (is omnipresent), and knows the state of our minds (is omniscient).  I am glad to offer my life to Waheguru, reciting whose Naam leads to salvation, oh Nanak.   1. 

 

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਟੂਟੀ ਗਾਢਨਹਾਰ ਗੁੋਪਾਲ॥ ਸਰਬ ਜੀਆ ਆਪੇ ਪ੍ਰਤਿਪਾਲ॥ ਸਗਲ ਕੀ ਚਿੰਤਾ ਜਿਸੁ ਮਨ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਤਿਸ ਤੇ ਬਿਰਥਾ ਕੋਈ ਨਾਹਿ॥ ਰੇ ਮਨ ਮੇਰੇ ਸਦਾ ਹਰਿ ਜਾਪਿ॥ ਅਬਿਨਾਸੀ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਆਪੇ ਆਪਿ॥ ਆਪਨ ਕੀਆ ਕਛੂ ਨ ਹੋਇ॥ ਜੇ ਸਉ ਪ੍ਰਾਨੀ ਲੋਚੈ ਕੋਇ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਬਿਨੁ ਨਾਹੀ ਤੇਰੈ ਕਿਛੁ ਕਾਮ॥ ਗਤਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਪਿ ਏਕ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Tooti gaandhunhaar Gopaal.  Serb jeeyaan Aapay pritpaal.  Sugul kee chinta Jis(u) mun maah(i).  Tis tay birtha koyee naah(i).  Ray mun mayray suda Hur(i) jaap(i).  Abinaashi Prubh(u) aapay aap(i).  Aapun keeya kuchhoo na hoyay. Jay suou praani loachaiy koyay.  Tis(u) bin(u) naahi tayraiy kichh(u) kaam. Gut(i) Nanak jup(i) aik Hur(i) Naam.  1.

Word Meaning:  Tooti = broken; disconnected.   Gaandhunhaar = ability to reconnect; unify.      Pritpaal = nourish and raise.    Chinta = worry; responsibility to look after.   Birtha = without; devoid of.      Abinaashi = immortal.    Loachaiy = longs for; wishes.   Tis(u) = that (Waheguru).    Gut(i) = high spiritual state; salvation.   1.

MEANING: Waheguru is capable of reconnecting people who have been disconnected from Him.  He nourishes all living beings.  He looks after everybody, and nobody is devoid of His kindness.  The eternal Waheguru operates everywhere all by Himself; so, always recite His Naam, oh my mind.   The man is not capable of doing anything on his own, no matter how much he longs (tries hard) to do that.   Nothing else is any good for you than Waheguru, oh man.  So, recite the Naam of Waheguru to get salvation (unite with Him), oh Nanak.   1.

ਰੂਪਵੰਤੁ ਹੋਇ ਨਾਹੀ ਮੋਹੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੀ ਜੋਤਿ ਸਗਲ ਘਟ ਸੋਹੈ॥ ਧਨਵੰਤਾ ਹੋਇ ਕਿਆ ਕੋ ਗਰਬੈ॥ ਜਾ ਸਭ ਕਿਛੁ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਦੀਆ ਦਰਬੈ॥ ਅਤਿ ਸੂਰਾ ਜੇ ਕੋਈ ਕਹਾਵੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੀ ਕਲਾ ਬਿਨਾ ਕਹ ਧਾਵੈ॥ ਜੇ ਕੋ ਹੋਇ ਬਹੈ ਦਾਤਾਰੁ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਦੇਨਹਾਰੁ ਜਾਨੈ ਗਾਵਾਰੁ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਗੁਰਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਤੂਟੈ ਹਉ ਰੋਗੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੋ ਜਨੁ ਸਦਾ ਅਰੋਗੁ॥ 2॥

Roopvunt(u) hoyay naahi mohaiy. Prubh kee Joat(i) sugul ghut sohaiy.   Dhunvunta hoyay kiya ko gerbaiy. Ja subh kichh(u) Tis ka deeya derbaiy.  Ut(i) soora jay koyee kuhaavaiy.  Prubh kee kula bina keh dhaavaiy.  Jay ko hoyay buhaiy dataar(u).  Tis Daynhaar(u) jaanaiy gavaar(u).  Jis(u) Gur persaad(i) tootaiy huoun roag(u).  Nanak so jun suda aroag(u).   2.

Word Meaning:  Roopvunt(u) = handsome; beautiful.   Mohaiy = captivates; charms.  Sohaiy = looks pretty; is pleasing.  Dhunvunta = wealthy.     Gerbaiy = feels conceited.      Derbaiy = wealth.    Kula = power.    Dhaavaiy = rush to attack.    Dataar(u) = big donor.    Daynhaar = the real source (Waheguru).   Gavaar(u) = unrefined; stupid.     Huoun = ego and selfishness.   Roag = ailment.      2.

MEANING:  The good looks of an individual do not charm Waheguru; (He likes everybody equally) as His pleasing Light is present in every heart.  How can a man be conceited of his wealth when it is all given by Waheguru?  A person cannot rush to attack his target without the power bestowed on him by Waheguru - no matter how great a valiant he thinks himself to be.   Waheguru considers the individual an idiot who claims to be a big philanthropist. An individual who overcomes the ailment of his ego and selfishness in the shelter of the Guru becomes free from all such ailments for good, oh Nanak.   2.

ਜਿਉ ਮੰਦਰ ਕਉ ਥਾਮੈ ਥੰਮਨੁ॥ ਤਿਉ ਗੁਰ ਕਾ ਸਬਦੁ ਮਨਹਿ ਅਸਥੰਮਨੁ॥ ਜਿਉ ਪਾਖਾਣੁ ਨਾਵ ਚੜਿ ਤਰੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਾਣੀ ਗੁਰ ਚਰਣ ਲਗਤੁ ਨਿਸਤਰੈ॥ ਜਿਉ ਅੰਧਕਾਰ ਦੀਪਕ ਪਰਗਾਸੁ॥ ਗੁਰ ਦਰਸਨੁ ਦੇਖਿ ਮਨਿ ਹੋਇ ਬਿਗਾਸੁ॥ ਜਿਉ ਮਹਾ ਉਦਿਆਨ ਮਹਿ ਮਾਰਗੁ ਪਾਵੈ॥ ਤਿਉ ਸਾਧੂ ਸੰਗਿ ਮਿਲਿ ਜੋਤਿ ਪ੍ਰਗਟਾਵੈ॥ ਤਿਨ ਸੰਤਨ ਕੀ ਬਾਛਉ ਧੂਰਿ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਕੀ ਹਰਿ ਲੋਚਾ ਪੂਰਿ॥ 3॥

Jiyoun mundar kuou thaamaiy thummun(u). Tiyoun Gur ka Shabad(u) muneh(i) usthummun(u). Jiyoun paakhaan(u) naav churrh(i) turaiy.  Praani Gur Churn lugut(u) nisturaiy. Jiyoun undhkaar deepuk pergaas(u).  Gur dershun(u) daykh(i) mun(i) hoyay bigaas(u).  Jiyoun muha oodiyaan meh(i) maarug(u) paavaiy. Tiyoun Sadhu sung(i) mil(i) Jot(i) pergtaavaiy. Tin suntun kee baanchoon dhoor(i). Nanak kee Hur(i) loacha poor(i).  3.

Word Meaning:  Mundar = big mansion.    Thaamaiy = supports.   Thummun(u) = pillars.    Paakhaan = stone.     Naav = boat.    Turaiy = floats; goes across.    Undhkaar = complete darkness.    Deepuk = lamp.   Pergaas(u) = light.    Bigaas(u) = blooms; is enlightened.    Oodiyaan = wilderness.     Joat(i) Pergtaavaiy = enlightenment.   Baanchhoon = strongly wish for.   Dhoor(i) = dust of the feet.    Loacha = longing.    3. 

MEANING:  Just as pillars support the structure of a big mansion, Guru’s Shabad provides a big support for the mind (which becomes very strong and stops wavering).  Just as a stone gets across a body of water in a boat, a man gets across the world-sea by taking shelter at the Guru’s feet (surrendering himself to the Guru).  Just as a lamp produces light in darkness, a devotee’s heart is enlightened (blooms) by the holy sight of the Guru.   Just like a man lost in a vast wilderness, somehow finds his way out, man’s path to union with Waheguru is enlightened in the shelter of the Guru.   I, Nanak, wish for the dust of the feet of such devotees (wish to serve them); kindly fulfil my longing, oh Waheguru.    3.

ਮਨ ਮੂਰਖ ਕਾਹੇ ਬਿਲਲਾਈਐ॥

(ਅੰਗ 283)॥   ਪੁਰਬ ਲਿਖੇ ਕਾ ਲਿਖਿਆ ਪਾਈਐ॥ ਦੂਖ ਸੂਖ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਦੇਵਨਹਾਰੁ॥ ਅਵਰ ਤਿਆਗਿ ਤੂ ਤਿਸਹਿ ਚਿਤਾਰੁ॥ ਜੋ ਕਛੁ ਕਰੈ ਸੋਈ ਸੁਖੁ ਮਾਨੁ॥ ਭੂਲਾ ਕਾਹੇ ਫਿਰਹਿ ਅਜਾਨੁ॥ ਕਉਨੁ ਬਸਤੁ ਆਈ ਤੇਰੈ ਸੰਗ॥ ਲਪਟਿ ਰਹਿਓ ਰਸਿ ਲੋਭੀ ਪਤੰਗ॥ ਰਾਮ ਨਾਮ ਜਪਿ ਹਿਰਦੇ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਪਤਿ ਸੇਤੀ ਘਰਿ ਜਾਹਿ॥ 4॥

Mun moorukh kaahay bil-laayiyaiy.

(PAGE 283):           Poorub likhay ka likhiya paayiyaiy. Dookh sookh Prubh dayvunhaar(u).  Uver tiyaag(i) tu Tiseh(i) chitaar(u). Jo kuchh(u) kuraiy soyee sookh maan(u).  Bhoola kaahay phireh(i) ajaan(u).  Kaun(u) bust(u) aayee tayraiy sung.  Lupt(i) ruhiyo rus(i) loabhi ptung.  Ram Naam jup(i) hirday maah(i). Nanak put(i) sayti ghur(i) jaah(i).    4.

Word Meaning:  Bila-laayiyaiy = wail; complain loudly.    Poorub Likhiya = prewritten.   Tiyaag(i) = give up; forget.    Tiseh(i) = Him.    Chitaar(u) = remember.   Maan(u) = accept; take comfort.    Ajaan = ignorant.     Bust(u) = article; item.   Lupt(i) = cling.   Ptung = bumble-bee, which sucks the sap from the flowers, but gets trapped in there.  Sayti = with.   Ghur(i) = final destination (Waheguru’s Court).     4. 

MEANING:   Why are you crying and complaining loudly, oh my foolish mind? You are only reaping the rewards of your prewritten destiny (based on your previous deeds).   Comforts and discomforts are given by Waheguru; so, you remember Him and forget everything else.  Take comfort in whatever He does (whatever happens to you); where are you lost in misgivings, oh, ignorant?  What things accompanied you at your birth!! Now you are clinging to Maya in greed like a bumblebee, which is trapped in the flowers while trying to suck their sap.  Recite Waheguru’s Naam in your heart, and go to your final destination (Waheguru’s Court) with dignity, oh Nanak.    4.

ਜਿਸੁ ਵਖਰ ਕਉ ਲੈਨਿ ਤੂ ਆਇਆ॥ ਰਾਮ ਨਾਮ ਸੰਤਨ ਘਰਿ ਪਾਇਆ॥ ਤਜਿ ਅਭਿਮਾਨੁ ਲੇਹੁ ਮਨ ਮੋਲਿ॥ ਰਾਮ ਨਾਮ ਹਿਰਦੇ ਮਹਿ ਤੋਲਿ॥ ਲਾਦਿ ਖੇਪ ਸੰਤਹ ਸੰਗਿ ਚਾਲੁ॥ ਅਵਰ ਤਿਆਗਿ ਬਿਖਿਆ ਜੰਜਾਲ॥ ਧੰਨਿ ਧੰਨਿ ਕਹੈ ਸਭ ਕੋਇ॥ ਮੁਖ ਊਜਲ ਹਰਿ ਦਰਗਹ ਸੋਇ॥ ਇਹੁ ਵਾਪਾਰੁ ਵਿਰਲਾ ਵਾਪਾਰੈ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਾ ਕੈ ਸਦ ਬਲਿਹਾਰੈ॥5॥

Jis vukkhur kuou laiyn(i) tu aaya. Ram Naam Suntun ghur(i)  paaya.  Tuj(i) abhimaan(u) layhou mun moal(i). Ram Naam hirday meh(i) toal(i).  Laad(i) khayp Suntaah sung(i) chaal(u).  Uver tiyaag(I) bikhiya junjaal.  Dhunn(i) dhunn(i) kuhaiy subh koyay. Mookh oojul Hur(i) Durgeh soyay. Ehou vapaar(u) virla vapaaraiy.  Nanak ta kaiy sud bulihaaraiy.   5.

Word Meaning: Vukkhur = merchandise (Waheguru’s Naam).    Tuj(i) = give up.   Abhimaan(u) = ego.   Moal(i) = buy.     Toal(i) = evaluate.    Laad(i) = load up.    Khayp = merchandise (Waheguru’s Naam).   Bikhiya Junjaal = network (bonds) of Maya.    Oojul = clean; unblemished.    Vaapaar(u) = business.     Virla = rare.    5. 

MEANING:   The merchandise (Waheguru’s Naam), which you came to acquire (took birth for), can be had from the holy saints, oh my friend.   Give up your ego, and surrender your mind to them to buy Waheguru’s Naam to acquire it in your mind.   Abandon all activities that bind you with Maya, and tread the path of the Guru to load up Waheguru’s Naam.  Everybody will praise you, and you will have a shiny (unblemished) face (will be honoured) in Waheguru’s Court.  But some rare individual engages in such business (dealing in Waheguru’s Naam).  I always wish to lay down my life for such a great devotee, oh Nanak.   5.

ਚਰਨ ਸਾਧ ਕੇ ਧੋਇ ਧੋਇ ਪੀਉ॥ ਅਰਪਿ ਸਾਧ ਕਉ ਅਪਨਾ ਜੀਉ॥ ਸਾਧ ਕੀ ਧੂਰਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਇਸਨਾਨੁ॥ ਸਾਧ ਊਪਰਿ ਜਾਈਐ ਕੁਰਬਾਨੁ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੇਵਾ ਵਡਭਾਗੀ ਪਾਈਐ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਹਰਿ ਕੀਰਤਨੁ ਗਾਈਐ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਬਿਘਨ ਤੇ ਸਾਧੂ ਰਾਖੈ॥ ਹਰਿ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਇ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤ ਰਸੁ ਚਾਖੈ॥ ਓਟ ਗਹੀ ਸੰਤਹ ਦਰਿ ਆਇਆ॥ ਸਰਬ ਸੂਖ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਹ ਪਾਇਆ॥ 6॥

Churn Sadh kay dhoyay dhoyay peeyo.  Urp(i) Sadh kuou apna jeeyo. Sadh kee dhoor(i) kurhou ishnaan(u).  Sadh oopur(i) jaayiyaiy koorbaan(u).  Sadh sayva vudbhaageen paayiyaiy.  Sadh sung(i) Hur(i) keertun gaayiyaiy.  Anik bighun tay Sadhu raakhaiy.  Hur(i) goon gaayay, Amrit rus(u) chaakhaiy.  Oat guhee Suntaah dur(i) aaya.  Serb sookh Nanak tih paaya.   6.

Word Meaning:  Churn = feet.  Dhoyay, Dhoyay Peeyo = Dhoor(i) Ishnaan =  serve with deep reverence.   Urp(i) = offer.   Jeeyo = soul; mind.   Anik = countless.   Bighun = hindrances; impediments.    Oat = shelter.   Guhee = held; took.     6. 

MEANING:  Serve the Guru with deep reverence, and surrender your mind to Him, oh my friend.  Obey the Guru with reverence, and offer your life (act like a slave) to Him.  It is with great fortune that one gets to serve the Guru; sing Waheguru’s virtues in His company, oh my mind. Guru protects you from countless impediments in life, and one tastes the Amrit of Waheguru’s Naam by singing His praise.   An individual who takes the Guru’s shelter realizes all the comforts of life, oh Nanak.  6.

ਮਿਰਤਕ ਕਉ ਜੀਵਾਲਨਹਾਰ॥ ਭੂਖੇ ਕਉ ਦੇਵਤ ਅਧਾਰ॥ ਸਰਬ ਨਿਧਾਨ ਜਾ ਕੀ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟੀ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਪੁਰਬ ਲਿਖੇ ਕਾ ਲਹਣਾ ਪਾਹਿ॥ ਸਭੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਓਹੁ ਕਰਨੈ ਜੋਗੁ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਬਿਨੁ ਦੂਸਰ ਹੋਆ ਨਾ ਹੋਗੁ॥ ਜਪਿ ਜਨ ਸਦਾ ਸਦਾ ਦਿਨੁ ਰੈਣੀ॥ ਸਭ ਤੇ ਊਚ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਇਹ ਕਰਣੀ॥ ਕਰਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਜਿਸ ਕਉ ਨਾਮੁ ਦੀਆ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੋ ਜਨੁ ਨਿਰਮਲੁ ਥੀਆ॥ 7॥

Mirtuk kuou jeevalunhaar.  Bhookhay kuou dayvut adhaar.  Serb nidhaan ja kee drishti maah(i).  Poorub likhay ka lehna paah(i). Subh(u) kichh(u) Tis ka, Oh(u) kernaiy joag(u). Tis(u) bin(u) doosur hoa na hoag(u). Jup(i) jun suda suda din(u) raiyni. Subh tay ooch nirmul eh kerni. Kur(i) kirpa jis kuou Naam(u) deeya. Nanak so jun nirmul(u) theeya.  7.

Word Meaning:   Jeevaalunhaar = injector of life; reviving the dead.     Adhaar = food.   Nidhan = treasures.   Drishti = sight.    Poorub likhay = prewritten.   Lehna Paayay = is reaping the reward.   Raiyni = night.   Nirmul = holy.   Kerni = deed; living.   Theeya = became.    7.

MEANING: Waheguru can revive a dead person, and serves food to the hungry. (It also means that the Guru injects spiritual life in the mind, which is spiritually dead, and serves spiritual food for his soul).  His kind sight is a source of all treasures, but everybody reaps the rewards of their prewritten destiny.  He controls everything and is capable of doing anything; no one has ever been like Him, and nobody will ever be.  Recite Waheguru’s Naam day and night, oh man; this is the highest, and holy living.  The individual, whom Waheguru kindly gives His Naam, becomes pure (qualified to connect with God), oh Nanak.    7.

ਜਾ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਗੁਰ ਕੀ ਪਰਤੀਤਿ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਆਵੈ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਚੀਤਿ॥ ਭਗਤੁ ਭਗਤੁ ਸੁਨੀਐ ਤਿਹੁ ਲੋਇ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਹਿਰਦੈ ਏਕੋ ਹੋਇ॥ ਸਚੁ ਕਰਣੀ ਸਚੁ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਰਹਤ॥ ਸਚੁ ਹਿਰਦੈ ਸਤਿ ਮੁਖਿ ਕਹਤ॥ ਸਾਚੀ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਸਾਚਾ ਆਕਾਰੁ॥ ਸਚੁ ਵਰਤੈ ਸਾਚਾ ਪਾਸਾਰੁ॥ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਜਿਨਿ ਸਚੁ ਕਰਿ ਜਾਤਾ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੋ ਜਨੁ ਸਚਿ ਸਮਾਤਾ॥ 8॥15॥

Ja kaiy mun(i) Gur kee purteet(i). Tis(u) jun aavaiy Hur(i) Prubh(u) cheet(i). Bhugut(u), bhugut(u) sooniyaiy tihou loyay. Ja kaiy hirdaiy Aiko hoyay. Sach(u) kerni, Sach(u) ta kee reht. Sach(u) hirdaiy, Sat(i) mookh(i) keht. Saachi drisht(i) Saacha akaar(u). Sach(u) vertaiy, Sacha pasaar(u). Parbraham jin(i) Sach(u) kur(i) jaata. Nanak so jun(u) Sach(i) smaata.  8.15.

Word Meaning:   Purteet(i) = firm faith.   Tihou Loyay = in all three layers – earth, underworld, and the sky.  Sach(u) = holy.    Kerni = deeds.    Reht = living.   Sach(u) = Waheguru.  Sat(i) = truth.     Drisht(i) = viewing.   Aakaar = body; personality.    Pasaar(u) = ceation.    Smaata = merges into.   8.15.

MEANING:  A devotee, who has firm faith in Waheguru, remembers Him in his mind.   A devotee, who has only Waheguru’s Naam at heart, becomes famous as a devotee of Waheguru in all three layers of the universe (all over the country). His deeds are pure, and so is his daily living.  He has Waheguru at heart and always tells the truth.  His vision is pure (he treats everybody the same way) and has an honest body (personality).  He firmly believes that it is Waheguru who is operating everywhere and that He is present in all of His creation.   An individual who firmly believes that Waheguru is eternal becomes “one” with Him, oh Nanak.   8.15. 

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਰੂਪੁ ਨ ਰੇਖ ਨ ਰੰਗੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਤ੍ਰਿਹੁ ਗੁਣ ਤੇ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਭਿੰਨ॥ ਤਿਸਹਿ ਬੁਝਾਏ ਨਾਨਕਾ ਜਿਸੁ ਹੋਵੈ ਸੁਪ੍ਰਸੰਨ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Roop(u) na raykh, na rung(i) kichh(u), trihou goon tay Prubh bhinn. Tiseh(i) boojhaayay Nanaka, jis(u) hovaiy soopersunn.  1.

Word Meaning:  Roop Na Raykh = no physical features.   Trihou Goon = the three effects of Maya. Bhinn = different; above.  Boojhaayay = reveals.    1.

MEANING: Waheguru has no physical features and is beyond the three effects of Maya. He reveals Himself only to those whom He is overly pleased with, oh Nanak. 1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਅਬਿਨਾਸੀ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਮਨ ਮਹਿ ਰਾਖੁ॥ ਮਾਨੁਖ ਕੀ ਤੂ ਪ੍ਰੀਤਿ ਤਿਆਗੁ॥ ਤਿਸ ਤੇ ਪਰੈ ਨਾਹੀ ਕਿਛੁ ਕੋਇ॥ ਸਰਬ ਨਿਰੰਤਰਿ ਏਕੋ ਸੋਇ॥ ਆਪੇ ਬੀਨਾ ਆਪੇ ਦਾਨਾ॥ ਗਹਿਰ ਗੰਭੀਰੁ ਗਹੀਰੁ ਸੁਜਾਨਾ॥ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਪਰਮੇਸੁਰ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ॥ ਕ੍ਰਿਪਾ ਨਿਧਾਨ ਦਇਆਲ ਬਖਸੰਦ॥ ਸਾਧ ਤੇਰੇ ਕੀ ਸਰਨੀ ਪਾਉ॥

(ਅੰਗ 284)॥    ਨਾਨਕ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਇਹੁ ਅਨਰਾਉ॥1॥

Ashtpudi: Abinaashi Prubh(u) mun meh(i) raakh(u). Manookh kee tu preet(i) tiyaag(u).  Tis tay puraiy naahi kichh(u) koyay.  Serb niruntar(i) aiko Soyay.  Aapay beena, Aapay daana.  Gehr gumbheer(u) guheer(u) soojaana.  Parbraham, Permayshur, Gobind. Kirpa nidhaan, duyaal Bukhshund. Sadh Tayray kee sherni paaou. 

(PAGE 284):        Nanak kaiy mun(i) eho unraaou.   1.

Word Meaning: Abinaashi = immortal.   Tiyaag(u) = give up.     Puraiy = beyond; without His control.    Niruntar(i) = continuous.   Beena = know-all.   Daana = wise; provider.    Gehr = deep.    Gumbheer = never changing; has no emotions.    Guheer(u) = treasure.   Parbraham = beyond the universe. Permayshur = Supreme Lord.  Gobind = nourisher of the world.  Kirpa Nidhaan = treasure of kindness.  Soojaana = noble.     Bukhshund = forgiving.    Unraaou = longing; zest.   1. 

MEANING:  Give up the love for the men (relatives), and keep the immortal Waheguru in your mind, oh my friend.  There is nothing beyond the control of Waheguru, and He is continuously (without any gaps) present in everybody.  He knows everybody’s needs and provides for their necessities.  He is very deep (unfathomable), has no emotions, and is the treasure of everything.  He is bigger than the universe, is the supreme Lord, and the nourisher of the world.  He is the treasure of kindness, benevolence, and forgiveness.   Kindly provide me the shelter of the Guru, oh Waheguru; that’s the longing in my mind, oh Nanak.    1.

ਮਨਸਾ ਪੂਰਨ ਸਰਨਾ ਜੋਗ॥ ਜੋ ਕਰਿ ਪਾਇਆ ਸੋਈ ਹੋਗੁ॥ ਹਰਨ ਭਰਨ ਜਾ ਕਾ ਨੇਤ੍ਰ ਫੋਰੁ॥ ਤਿਸਕਾ ਮੰਤ੍ਰੁ ਨ ਜਾਨੈ ਹੋਰੁ॥ ਅਨਦ ਰੂਪ ਮੰਗਲ ਸਦ ਜਾ ਕੈ॥ ਸਰਬ ਥੋਕ ਸੁਨੀਅਹਿ ਘਰਿ ਤਾ ਕੈ॥ ਰਾਜ ਮਹਿ ਰਾਜੁ ਜੋਗ ਮਹਿ ਜੋਗੀ॥ ਤਪ ਮਹਿ ਤਪੀਸਰੁ ਗ੍ਰਿਹਸਤ ਮਹਿ ਭੋਗੀ॥ ਧਿਆਇ ਧਿਆਇ ਭਗਤਹ ਸੁਖੁ ਪਾਇਆ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਸੁ ਪੁਰਖ ਕਾ ਕਿਨੈ ਅੰਤੁ ਨ ਪਾਇਆ॥ 2॥

Munsa poorun, sherna joag.  Jo kur(i) paaya, soyee hoag(u).  Hern, bhern, Ja ka naytur phoar(u).  Tis ka Muntar(u) na jaaaiy hoar(u).  Anund-Roop mungul sud Ja kaiy. Serb thoak sooniyeh(i) ghur(i) Ta kaiy.  Raj meh(i) raj(u), joag meh(i) joagi.  Tup meh(i) tupeesur(u), grehst meh(i) bhoagi. Dhiyaayaiy, dhiyaayaiy bhugtaah sookh(u) paaya. Nanak Tis(u) Purakh ka kinaiy unt(u) na paaya.   2.

Word Meaning:  Munsa = wish.   Poorun = fulfiller.    Hern = steal; take away.   Bhern = fill; replenish.   Naytur Phoar(u) = in the twinkling of an eye.   Muntar = planning; thoughts.    Anund = blissfulness.  Mungul = joy.   Serb Thoak = everything.   Tupeesur(u) = the endurer of hardships.   Bhoagi = one who enjoys gifts of life.    2.

MEANING: Waheguru fulfills the wishes of, and provides shelter to His devotees, oh my friend.  However, only that will happen which is predestined by Him for the individuals.  He can take away things or replenish them in the twinkling of an eye, and nobody knows what he has in mind.  There is always bliss and joy in His House (Kingdom), and He is heard to have everything in His treasures.  (Being present in everybody), He is the King among kings and a yogi among the yogis.  (Being present in everybody), He endures all the tough hardships inflicted by the worshippers on those who try to please their deities by self-affliction, and He enjoys all the gifts of nature (enjoyed by the family men).  His devotees enjoy bliss by reciting His Naam, but nobody has ever found His limits, oh Nanak.   2.

ਜਾ ਕੀ ਲੀਲਾ ਕੀ ਮਿਤਿ ਨਾਹਿ॥ ਸਗਲ ਦੇਵ ਹਾਰੇ ਅਵਗਾਹਿ॥ ਪਿਤਾ ਕਾ ਜਨਮੁ ਕਿ ਜਾਨੈ ਪੂਤੁ॥ ਸਗਲ ਪਰੋਈ ਅਪੁਨੈ ਸੂਤਿ॥ ਸੁਮਤਿ ਗਿਆਨੁ ਧਿਆਨੁ ਜਿਨ ਦੇਇ॥ ਜਨ ਦਾਸ ਨਾਮੁ ਧਿਆਵਹਿ ਸੇਇ॥ ਤਿਹੁ ਗੁਣ ਮਹਿ ਜਾ ਕਉ ਭਰਮਾਏ॥ ਜਨਮਿ ਮਰੈ ਫਿਰਿ ਆਵੈ ਜਾਏ॥ ਊਚ ਨੀਚ ਤਿਸ ਕੇ ਅਸਥਾਨ॥ ਜੈਸਾ ਜਨਾਵੈ ਤੈਸਾ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਾਨ॥ 3॥

Ja kee leela kee mit(i) naah(i). Sugul dayv haaray uvgaah(i).  Pita ka junum(u) k(i) jaanaiy poot(u).  Sugul proyee Apunaiy soot(i).  Soomut(i), Giyan(u), dhiyaan(u) jin dayay. Jun daas Naam(u) dhiyaveh(i) sayay. Tihou goon meh(i) ja kuou bhermaayay.  Junum(i) muraiy, phir(i) aavaiy jaayay.  Ooch, neech, tis kay usthaan. Jaiysa junaavaiy, taiysa Nanak jaan.   3.

Word Meaning:   Leela = amazing feats.     Mit(i) = measurement.    Dayv = gods.   Uvgaahay = searching all over.   Proyee = stitched.   Soot = thread; laws.   Soomut(i) = noble advice.  Sayay = those.  Tihou Goon = the three effects of Maya.   Bhermaayay = get captivated.  Ooch = high.  Neech = low.   Junaavaiy = makes known; projects. 3 

MEANING:  There is no limit to His amazing feats; all gods have exhausted searching for His limits, but they failed badly.  How can a son have the knowledge of the birth of his father?  Everything is stitched to (controlled by) the thread of Waheguru’s laws. The devotees, who are given noble advice, Divine Knowledge, and the ability to concentrate, recite His Naam. On the other hand, those who are misled to be captivated by the three effects of Maya keep taking births and dying.  They keep shifting between low and high mental states, and are recognized exactly the way they are projected (designed to be) by Waheguru, oh Nanak.   3.

ਨਾਨਾ ਰੂਪ ਨਾਨਾ ਜਾ ਕੇ ਰੰਗ॥ ਨਾਨਾ ਭੇਖ ਕਰਹਿ ਇਕ ਰੰਗ॥ ਨਾਨਾ ਬਿਧਿ ਕੀਨੋ ਬਿਸਥਾਰੁ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਅਬਿਨਾਸੀ ਏਕੰਕਾਰੁ॥ ਨਾਨਾ ਚਲਿਤ ਕਰੇ ਖਿਨ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਪੂਰਿ ਰਹਿਓ ਪੂਰਨੁ ਸਭ ਠਾਇ॥ ਨਾਨਾ ਬਿਧਿ ਕਰਿ ਬਨਤ ਬਨਾਈ॥ ਅਪਨੀ ਕੀਮਤਿ ਆਪੇ ਪਾਈ॥ ਸਭ ਘਟ ਤਿਸ ਕੇ ਸਭ ਤਿਸ ਕੇ ਠਾਉ॥ ਜਪਿ ਜਪਿ ਜੀਵੈ ਨਾਨਕ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਉ॥4॥

Nana roop, nana Ja kay rung.  Nana bhaykh kureh(i) ikk rung.  Nana bidh(i) keeno bisthaar(u). Prubh abinaashi Ek Kunkar(u).  Nana chulit kuray khin maah(i).  Poor ruhiyo, Poorun(u) subh tthaayayn.  Nana bidh(i) kur(i) bunut bunaayee.  Apni keemut(i) Aapay payee. Subh ghut Tis kay, subh Tis kay tthaaoun. Jup(i), jup(i) jeevaiy Nanak Hur(i) Naaoun.  4.

Word Meaning:   Nana = different.   Bhaykh = outfits.   Rung = nature.     Bisthaar(u) = expansion.   Chulit = uncanny acts.   Poorun(u) = perfect.   Bunut Bunaayee = creation.   Ghut = hearts.   4.

MEANING: (Being present in everything), Waheguru appears in many different forms, and in many different colourful ways.  He wears many different looks, but His nature is always the same.  He has expanded the universe in many different ways, but Himself is immortal and uniform (never changing).  He can perform amazing feats in a moment, and the Perfect Waheguru fills the whole space.  He created the world in many different ways, and only He knows His worth (majesty). He dwells in every heart and every nook and corner.  I survive by reciting His Naam, oh Nanak.   4. 

ਨਾਮ ਕੇ ਧਾਰੇ ਸਗਲੇ ਜੰਤ॥ ਨਾਮ ਕੇ ਧਾਰੇ ਖੰਡ ਬ੍ਰਹਮੰਡ॥ ਨਾਮ ਕੇ ਧਾਰੇ ਸਿਮ੍ਰਿਤਿ ਬੇਦ ਪੁਰਾਨ॥ ਨਾਮ ਕੇ ਧਾਰੇ ਸੁਨਨ ਗਿਆਨ ਧਿਆਨ॥ ਨਾਮ ਕੇ ਧਾਰੇ ਆਗਾਸ ਪਾਤਾਲ॥ ਨਾਮ ਕੇ ਧਾਰੇ ਸਗਲ ਆਕਾਰ॥ ਨਾਮ ਕੇ ਧਾਰੇ ਪੁਰੀਆ ਸਭ ਭਵਨ॥ ਨਾਮ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ ਉਧਰੇ ਸੁਨਿ ਸ੍ਰਵਨ॥ ਕਰਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਜਿਸੁ ਆਪਨੈ ਨਾਮਿ ਲਾਏ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਚਉਥੇ ਪਦ ਮਹਿ ਸੋ ਜਨ ਗਤਿ ਪਾਏ॥ 5॥

Naam kay dhaaray suglay junt.  Naam kay dhaaray khund Brahmund.  Naam kay dhaaray Simrit(i) Bayd Puran.  Naam kay dhaaray soonun, giyan, dhiyaan.  Naam kay dhaaray agaas pataal. Naam kay dhaaray sugul aakaar. Naam kay dhaaray puriyaan subh bhuvun.  Naam kaiy sung(i) oodhray soon(i) servun.  Kur(i) kirpa jis(u) Aapnaiy Naam(i) laayay. Nanak, chauthay pud meh(i) so jun gut(i) paayay.   5.

Word meaning: Naam = Laws of Waheguru.  (Naam and Waheguru have also been used interchangeably in Guru Granth Sahib).   Dhaaray = supported by; created by.  Junt = living beings.  Khund = masses of land.   Pataal = underworld.    Pooriyaan = lands (kingdoms) of gods.     Bhuvun = galaxies.   Chautha Pud = the spiritual state above the three effects of Maya.    5.

MEANING:  All living beings survive by Waheguru’s support, and so do all the land masses and the universe.  All the Vedas, the Simritis, and the Puranas were created according to the Laws of Waheguru.  All listening, knowledge, and ability to concentrate happen according to the Laws of Waheguru.  The sky and the underworld are in place by the Laws of Waheguru.  All bodies obey Waheguru’s Laws, and so do the kingdoms of the gods, and the galaxies.   Many individuals have found salvation by listening to Waheguru’s Naam with their ears. The individuals who are made to recite Waheguru’s Naam by His benevolence attain salvation by attaining the spiritual level above the three effects of Maya, oh Nanak.   5.

NOTE:  When Guru Sahib writes that the Vedas are written according to the laws of Waheguru, He does not mean that these books contain Divine Knowledge. He simply implies that there are people who are given the character (nature) to do things like that. As discussed, people are predestined to do certain things.  For example, some individuals even vilify the Guru by Natural law. 

ਰੂਪ ਸਤਿ ਜਾ ਕਾ ਸਤਿ ਅਸਥਾਨੁ॥ ਪੁਰਖੁ ਸਤਿ ਕੇਵਲ ਪਰਧਾਨੁ॥ ਕਰਤੂਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਜਾ ਕੀ ਬਾਣੀ॥ ਸਤਿ ਪੁਰਖ ਸਭ ਮਾਹਿ ਸਮਾਣੀ॥ ਸਤਿ ਕਰਮੁ ਜਾ ਕੀ ਰਚਨਾ ਸਤਿ॥ ਮੂਲੁ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਉਤਪਤਿ॥ ਸਤਿ ਕਰਣੀ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਨਿਰਮਲੀ॥ ਜਿਸਹਿ ਬੁਝਾਏ ਤਿਸਹਿ ਸਭ ਭਲੀ॥ ਸਤਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕਾ ਸੁਖਦਾਈ॥ ਬਿਸਵਾਸੁ ਸਤਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਗੁਰ ਤੇ ਪਾਈ॥ 6॥

Roop(u) Sut(i), Ja ka Sut(i) usthaan(u).  Purakh(u) Sut(i) kayvul Perdhaan.  Kurtoot(i) Sut(i), Sut(i) ja kee Bani.  Sut(i) Purakh subh maah(i) smaani.  Sut(i) kerm(u), Ja kee ruchna Sut(i).  Mool(u) Sut(i), Sut(i) output(i).  Sut(i) kerni, nirmul, nirmuli.  Jiseh(i) boojhaayay, tiseh(i) subh bhuli.  Sut(i) Naam(u) Prubh ka sookhdaayee.  Bisvaas(u) Sut(i), Nanak, Gur tay paayee.  6. 

Word Meaning: Roop = looks; different forms.   Sut(i) = eternal.   Kurtoot(i) = deeds; administration.  Bani = language; Word; Huqam.   Mool(u) = root; origin.   Ootput = product; creation.   Bhuli = comforting; blissful.  Bisvaas(u) = firm faith.    Sut(i) Nanak Gur = Nanak, Satgur.     6.

MEANING: Waheguru’s different forms, and His place (throne) are eternal.  The eternal and omnipresent Waheguru is the supreme Lord.  His administration of the universe and His Huqam are eternal.  The eternal Waheguru is present in everybody.  His creation and His benevolence (for His creation) are eternal. The root of everything (Waheguru), and the creation are eternal.  His deeds are the purest of the pure and eternal; and His revelation is blissful to the individuals.  Waheguru’s eternal Naam is very comforting, but the firm faith in the Naam can be acquired only through Satguru, 

oh Nanak.   6.

ਸਤਿ ਬਚਨ ਸਾਧੂ ਉਪਦੇਸ॥ ਸਤਿ ਤੇ ਜਨ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਰਿਦੈ ਪ੍ਰਵੇਸ॥ ਸਤਿ ਨਿਰਤਿ ਬੂਝੈ ਜੇ ਕੋਇ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਤ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਗਤਿ ਹੋਇ॥ ਆਪਿ ਸਤਿ ਕੀਆ ਸਭੁ ਸਤਿ॥ ਆਪੇ ਜਾਨੈ ਅਪਨੀ ਮਿਤਿ ਗਤਿ॥

(ਅੰਗ 285)॥  ਜਿਸ ਕੀ ਸ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਸੁ ਕਰਣੈਹਾਰੁ॥ ਅਵਰ ਨ ਬੂਝਿ ਕਰਤ ਬੀਚਾਰੁ॥ ਕਰਤੇ ਕੀ ਮਿਤਿ ਨ ਜਾਨੈ ਕੀਆ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜੋ ਤਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਸੋ ਵਰਤੀਆ॥ 7॥

Sut(i) Buchun Sadhu oopdaysh.  Sut(i) tay jun ja kaiy ridaiy pervaysh.  Sut(i) nirt(i), boojhaiy jay koyay. Naam(u) juput ta kee gut(i) hoyay.  Aap(i) Sut(i), keeya subh(u) Sut(i). Aapay jaanaiy, Apuni mit(i) gut(i).

(PAGE 285):     Jis kee srisht(i) su kernaiyhaar(u). Uver na boojh(i) kert beechaar(u). Kertay kee mit(i) na jaanaiy keeya. Nanak jo Tis bhaavaiy, su vertiya.   7.

Word Meaning:     Buchun = Oopdaysh = Teaching.  Tay Jun = those devotees.    Pervaysh = enters; is embedded.   Nirt(i) = dance (some devotees worship their deities by dancing).   Aap(i) = Waheguru.   Sat(i) = real.   Mit(i) = measurement (capabilities).    Gut(i) = spiritual level.   Boojh(i) = understanding.   Keeya = created (by Waheguru); man.   Vertiya = happens.   7.

MEANING: The Word, the Teachings of the Guru, are eternal; and those individuals also become eternal who embed Guru’s Teachings in their heart (because they will become “one” with the eternal Waheguru).  If a devotee understands the real meaning of dancing (his mind dances in bliss with Waheguru’s Naam), he finds salvation by reciting Waheguru’s Naam.   Waheguru is eternal Himself, and so is His creation.  He alone knows His capabilities and His spiritual heights (transcendence). The universe belongs to its Creator, and whatever He wishes, happens in the world.  (Only He knows His secrets), and nobody else can know them by pondering on His creation.  His created (man) does not have the ability to know Him, oh Nanak.   7.

ਬਿਸਮਨ ਬਿਸਮ ਭਏ ਬਿਸਮਾਦ॥ ਜਿਨਿ ਬੂਝਿਆ ਤਿਸੁ ਆਇਆ ਸਵਾਦ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੈ ਰੰਗਿ ਰਾਚਿ ਜਨ ਰਹੇ॥ ਗੁਰ ਕੈ ਬਚਨਿ ਪਦਾਰਥ ਲਹੇ॥ ਓਇ ਦਾਤੇ ਦੁਖ ਕਾਟਨਹਾਰ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ ਤਰੈ ਸੰਸਾਰ॥ ਜਨ ਕਾ ਸੇਵਕ ਸੋ ਵਡਭਾਗੀ॥ ਜਨ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ ਏਕ ਲਿਵ ਲਾਗੀ॥ ਗੁਨ ਗੋਬਿਦ ਕੀਰਤਨੁ ਜਨੁ ਗਾਵੈ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਫਲੁ ਪਾਵੈ॥ 8॥16॥

Bismun, bisum, bhuyay bismaad. Jin(i) boojhiya, tis(u) aayaa svaad.  Prubh kaiy rung(i) raach(i) jun ruhay.  Gur kaiy Buchun(i) Pdaruth luhay.  Oye daatay dookh kaatunhaar. Ja kaiy sung(i) turaiy sunsaar.  Jun ka sayvuk so vudbhaagi. Jun kaiy sung(i) Aik liv laagi.  Goon Gobind, keertun jun(u) gaavaiy. Gur persaad(i) Nanak ful(u) paavaiy.  8.16.

Word Meaning:   Bismun = mesmerized.    Bismaad = mesmerization.    Rung(i) = in love of.    Raach(i) = engrossed; absorbed.   Buchun = Teaching.    Pdaruth = valuable object = Waheguru’s Naam.   Luhay = acquires.   Daatay = benefactors.   Jun = devotee.   Liv = concentration; union with God.    Ful(u) = reward.   8. 

MEANING:  Impressed by the beauty of Nature, the man is deeply mesmerized.  But the individual who realizes Waheguru enjoys bliss.  The devotee stays absorbed in Waheguru’s love, and he acquires Waheguru’s Naam with the Guru’s advice.  Waheguru’s devotees are people’s benefactors, and remove their troubles; people find salvation in their company.  One connects with God in their company, and those who serve them are highly fortunate. A devotee, who recites Waheguru’s virtues and sings His praise, reaps the sweet rewards by Guru’s grace, oh Nanak. 8.16.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਆਦਿ ਸਚੁ ਜੁਗਾਦਿ ਸਚੁ॥ ਹੈ ਭਿ ਸਚੁ ਨਾਨਕ ਹੋਸੀ ਭਿ ਸਚੁ॥

Sloak(u): Aad(i) Sach(u), Joogaad(i) Sach(u).  Haiy bh(i) Sach(u), Nanak hoasi bh(i) Sach(u).

MEANING: Aad(i) = the beginning.   Sach(u) = eternal (Waheguru).   Joogaad(i) = at the beginning of the eons (joogs or yugas).  1.

MEANING: Waheguru has been there right from the beginning, was there at the beginning of the eons, is here now, and will be there in the future too, oh Nanak. 1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਚਰਨ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਪਰਸਨਹਾਰ॥ ਪੂਜਾ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਸੇਵਦਾਰ॥ ਦਰਸਨੁ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਪੇਖਨਹਾਰ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਧਿਆਵਨਹਾਰ॥ ਆਪਿ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਸਭ ਧਾਰੀ॥ ਆਪੇ ਗੁਣ ਆਪੇ ਗੁਣਕਾਰੀ॥ ਸਬਦੁ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਬਕਤਾ॥ ਸੁਰਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਜਸੁ ਸੁਨਤਾ॥ ਬੁਝਨਹਾਰ ਕਉ ਸਤਿ ਸਭ ਹੋਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸਤਿ ਸਤਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਸੋਇ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Churn Sat(i), Sat(i) persunhaar. Pooja Sat(i), Sat(i) sayvdaar. Dershun Sat(i), Sat(i) paykhunhaar. Naam(u) Sat(i), Sat(i) dhiyaavunhaar. Aap(i) Sat(i), Sat(i) subh dhaari. Aapay goon, Aapay goonkaari. Shabad(u) Sat(i), Sat(i) Prubh(u) bukta. Soort(i) Sat(i), Sat(i) Jus(u) soonta.  Boojhunhaar kuou Sat(i) subh hoyay.  Nanak, Sat(i), Sat(i) Prubh(u) soyay.  1.

Word Meaning:  Churn = (Waheguru’s) feet (Naam).   Sat(i) = everlasting.   Persunhaar = those who touch (worship).   Pooja = worship.   Sayvdaar = servants (worshippers).     Dershun(u) = sight (of Waheguru).    Paykhunhaar = viewers.   Dhaari = creation.   Goon = virtues.   Goonkaari = source of virtues.   Shabad(u) = (Waheguru’s) Huqam (Will).   Bukta = narrator; the creator of Huqam (Lord).  Soort(i) = focus of mind; meditation.     Boojhunhaar = one who understands (connected with Waheguru).  1. 

MEANING: Waheguru’s Naam is eternal, and those who recite the Naam will also become eternal (as they become “one” with Waheguru).  Waheguru’s worship will exist forever, and His worshippers will become eternal too. Waheguru’s sight is eternal (humans have longed to have His sight ever since), and those who are really able to have His sight (connect with Him) become eternal by merging into Him. Waheguru’s Naam is eternal, and so are those who recite the Naam.  Waheguru is everlasting, and so is His creation.  Waheguru is virtuous and is also the source of virtues.  Waheguru’s Word (Huqam) is eternal, and so is the Lord of Huqam, Waheguru.  Those who meditate on His Naam are eternal, and so are those who listen to His praise. The individual, who recognizes Waheguru (gets connected with Him), finds everything about Waheguru to be everlasting. Waheguru is always there, oh Nanak.1. 

ਸਤਿ ਸਰੂਪ ਰਿਦੈ ਜਿਨਿ ਮਾਨਿਆ॥ ਕਰਨ ਕਰਾਵਨ ਤਿਨਿ ਮੂਲੁ ਪਛਾਨਿਆ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਰਿਦੈ ਬਿਸਵਾਸੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਆਇਆ॥ ਤਤੁ ਗਿਆਨੁ ਤਿਸੁ ਮਨਿ ਪ੍ਰਗਟਾਇਆ॥ ਭੈ ਤੇ ਨਿਰਭਉ ਹੋਇ ਬਸਾਨਾ॥ ਜਿਸ ਤੇ ਉਪਜਿਆ ਤਿਸੁ ਮਾਹਿ ਸਮਾਨਾ॥ ਬਸਤੁ ਮਾਹਿ ਲੇ ਬਸਤੁ ਗਡਾਈ॥ ਤਾ ਕਉ ਭਿੰਨ ਨ ਕਹਨਾ ਜਾਈ॥ ਬੂਝੈ ਬੂਝਨਹਾਰੁ ਬਿਬੇਕ॥ ਨਾਰਾਇਨ ਮਿਲੇ ਨਾਨਕ ਏਕ॥ 2॥

Sat(i) sroop ridaiy jin(i) maaniya. Kern Kraavun, tin(i) mool(u) puchhaaniya.  Ja kaiy ridaiy bisvaas(u) Prubh aaya. Tut(u) Giyaan tis(u) mun(i) pergtaaya. Bhaiy tay nirbhuou hoyay busaana.  Jis tay oopjiya, Tis(u) maah(i) smaana. Bust(u) maah(i) lay bust(u) gudaayee.  Ta kuou bhinn na kehna jaayee.  Boojhaiy, boojhunhaar bibayk.   Narayin milay, Nanak aik.  2.

Word Meaning:   Sroop = Image; persona; identity.    Maaniya = believed; implanted in the heart.   Kern = the doer; the actor; the creator.  Kraavun = making others do.    Mool(u) = root; basis.  Puchhaaniya = recognized.   Bisvaas(u) = firm faith.    Tut(u) =  the origin.   Pergtaaya = cause to appear.   Oopjiya = produced; born.   Smaana = merges into.   Bust(u) = some article.   Gudaayee = mixed well; blended.  Bhinn = different.   Boojhaiy = uncovers (the secrets).    Bibayk = the ability to make a distinction.   Naraayin = God.     2. 

MEANING:  A devotee, who implants Waheguru’s image (His Naam) in his heart, recognizes that Waheguru is the basis of all the creation, and He gets others to do whatever He wants them to do. The devotee, who fosters a firm belief about Waheguru in his heart, gets enlightened with the knowledge about Waheguru, which is the root (basis) of everything.  From worrisome, the devotee's life becomes worry-free as he merges into Waheguru, who produced him.  Just as the same liquid in two containers is mixed with each other, it becomes one (blends in perfectly), and no part can be seen apart from the other, a devotee also becomes “one” with Waheguru and cannot be seen apart.  But only those individuals experience this state who acquire the Divine Knowledge by merging into Waheguru and becoming “one” with Him, oh Nanak.   3. 

ਠਾਕੁਰ ਕਾ ਸੇਵਕ ਆਗਿਆਕਾਰੀ॥ ਠਾਕੁਰ ਕਾ ਸੇਵਕ ਸਦਾ ਪੂਜਾਰੀ॥ ਠਾਕੁਰ ਕੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਪਰਤੀਤਿ॥ ਠਾਕੁਰ ਕੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਕੀ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਰੀਤਿ॥ ਠਾਕੁਰ ਕਉ ਸੇਵਕ ਜਾਨੈ ਸੰਗਿ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕਾ ਸੇਵਕ ਨਾਮ ਕੈ ਰੰਗਿ॥ ਸੇਵਕ ਕਉ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਪਾਲਨਹਾਰਾ॥ ਸੇਵਕ ਕੀ ਰਾਖੈ ਨਿਰੰਕਾਰਾ॥ ਸੋ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਜਿਸੁ ਦਇਆ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਧਾਰੈ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੋ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਸਾਸਿ ਸਾਸਿ ਸਮਾਰੈ॥ 3॥

Tthaakur ka sayvuk aagiyakaari.  Tthaakur ka sayvuk suda poojaari.  Tthaakur kay sayvuk kaiy mun(i) perteet(i).  Tthaakur kay sayvuk kee nirmul reet(i). Tthaakur kuou sayvuk jaanaiy sung(i). Prubh ka sayvuk Naam kaiy rung(i). Sayvuk kuou Prubh paalunhaara. Sayvuk kee raakhaiy Nirunkaara. So sayvuk(u) jis(u) duyya Prubh(u) dhaaraiy. Nanak, so sayvuk saas(i) saas(i) smaaraiy.  3.

Word Meaning:  Tthaakur = the Lord (Waheguru).    Agiyaakaari = obedient.  Perteet(i) = full faith.   Nirmul = pure.   Reet(i) = routine; dealings.   Rung(i) = (imbued) in love.    Paalunhaara = nourisher.   Dhaaraiy = provides; delivers.   Smaaraiy = remembers.  3.

MEANING:  The devotee of the Lord obeys His Huqam gladly and worships Him all the time. The devotee has full faith (in Waheguru) in his mind, and his living is very pure.  He always feels that Waheguru is close to Him and is imbued in His Naam. Waheguru nourishes His devotees (spiritually) and protects them (from attacks of Maya).  A devotee is one who is showered with Waheguru's kindness; such a devotee remembers Waheguru with every breath, oh Nanak.  3.

ਅਪੁਨੇ ਜਨ ਕਾ ਪਰਦਾ ਢਾਕੈ॥ ਅਪਨੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਕੀ ਸਰਪਰ ਰਾਖੈ॥ ਅਪਨੇ ਦਾਸ ਕਉ ਦੇਇ ਵਡਾਈ॥ ਅਪਨੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਕਉ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਾਈ॥ ਅਪਨੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਕੀ ਆਪਿ ਪਤਿ ਰਾਖੈ॥ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਗਤਿ ਮਿਤਿ ਕੋਇ ਨ ਲਾਖੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਕਉ ਕੋ ਨ ਪਹੂਚੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੇ ਸੇਵਕ ਊਚ ਤੇ ਊਚੇ॥ ਜੋ ਪ੍ਰਭਿ ਅਪਨੀ ਸੇਵਾ ਲਾਇਆ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੋ ਸੇਵਕ ਦਹ ਦਿਸਿ ਪ੍ਰਗਟਾਇਆ॥ 4॥

Apunay jun ka purdah dhaakaiy.  Apnay sayvuk kee surpur raakhaiy.  Apnay daas kuou dayay vudaayee.  Apnay sayvuk kuou Naam(u) jupaayee.  Apnay sayvuk kee Aap(i) put(i) raakhaiy.  Ta kee gut(i) mit(i) koyay na laakhaiy.  Prubh kay sayvuk kuou ko na puhoochaiy.  Prubh kay sayvuk ooch tay oochay. Jo Prubh(i) apni sayva laaya. Nanak, so sayvuk deh dis(i) pergtaaya.  4.

Word Meaning:  Jun = Sayvuk = devotee.   Purdah = secrets; weaknesses.   Dhaakaiy = covers; protects.    Surpur = for sure.    Vudaayee = respect.   Put(i) = dignity.    Laakhaiy = understand; find out.   Puhoochay = match.   Ooch Tay Oochay = have high morality (transcendent).   Pergtaaya = made famous (well known).    4. 

MEANING: Waheguru covers the weaknesses of His devotees and protects their dignity under all circumstances.  He bestows honour on His devotees and makes them recite His Naam.  He maintains the dignity of His devotees, but no one can discover His measurements or spiritual heights.  No worldly desire can reach Waheguru’s devotee (and rob him of any virtue), nor can anybody rival the devotee, as the devotees are highly transcendent (have very high character).  An individual, whom Waheguru puts to His service (makes him accept His Will), is made famous in every direction (all over), oh Nanak.      4.

ਨੀਕੀ ਕੀਰੀ ਮਹਿ ਕਲ ਰਾਖੈ॥ ਭਸਮ ਕਰੈ ਲਸਕਰ ਕੋਟਿ ਲਾਖੈ॥ ਜਿਸ ਕਾ ਸਾਸਿ ਨ ਕਾਢਤ ਆਪਿ॥

(ਅੰਗ 286)॥     ਤਾ ਕਉ ਰਾਖਤ ਦੇ ਕਰਿ ਹਾਥ॥ ਮਾਨਸ ਜਤਨ ਕਰਤ ਬਹੁ ਭਾਤਿ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੇ ਕਰਤਬ ਬਿਰਥੇ ਜਾਤਿ॥ ਮਾਰੈ ਰਾਖੈ ਅਵਰੁ ਨ ਕੋਇ॥ ਸਰਬ ਜੀਆ ਕਾ ਰਾਖਾ ਸੋਇ॥ ਕਾਹੇ ਸੋਚ ਕਰਹਿ ਰੇ ਪ੍ਰਾਣੀ॥ ਜਪਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਅਲਖ ਵਿਡਾਣੀ॥ 5॥

Neeki keeri meh(i) kul raakhaiy.  Bhusm kuraiy lushkur koat(i) laakhaiy.  Jis ka saas(i) na kaadhut Aap(i). 

(PAGE 286): Ta kuou raakhaiy day kur(i) haath. Maanus jutun kert buhou bhaant(i).  Tis kay kertub birthay jaat(i).  Maaraiy raakhaiy uver(u) na koyay. Serb jeeyaan ka raakha soyay. Kaahay soach kureh(i) ray praani. Jup(i) Nanak Prubh alukh vidaani. 5

Word Meaning:  Keeri = an ant; a humble and helpless man.    Bhusm = (make into) ash.    Lushkur = army.   Koat(i), Laakhaiy = millions.    Raakhut = saves.   Maanus = man.    Jutun = efforts.   Kertub = clever plans.   Birthay = empty; ineffective.  Soyay = Him. Praani = oh man.   Alukh = incomprehensible.   Vidaani = amazing.  5.

MEANING:  If Waheguru places His power in a weak and helpless man, he can destroy an army of millions and turn them into ash. However, if Waheguru does not want an individual to die, He protects him by extending His own hand, no matter how hard someone tries to kill him; all his (the killer’s) plans, no matter how clever, become ineffective.  Waheguru is the guardian of all, and nobody can kill or save an individual.  What else are you thinking about, oh man?  Just recite the Naam of the incomprehensible and amazing Waheguru, oh Nanak.5.   

ਬਾਰੰਬਾਰ ਬਾਰ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਜਪੀਐ॥ ਪੀ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤੁ ਇਹੁ ਮਨੁ ਤਨੁ ਧ੍ਰਪੀਐ॥ ਨਾਮ ਰਤਨੁ ਜਿਨਿ ਗੁਰਮੁਖਿ ਪਾਇਆ॥ਤਿਸੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਅਵਰੁ ਨਹੀ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਾਇਆ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਧਨ ਨਾਮੋ ਰੂਪੁ ਰੰਗੁ॥ ਨਾਮੋ ਸੁਖੁ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮ ਕਾ ਸੰਗੁ॥ਨਾਮ ਰਸਿ ਜੋ ਜਨ ਤ੍ਰਿਪਤਾਨੇ॥ ਮਨ ਤਨ ਨਾਮਹਿ ਨਾਮਿ ਸਮਾਨੇ॥ ਊਠਤ ਬੈਠਤ ਸੋਵਤ ਨਾਮ॥ ਕਹੁ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਨ ਕੈ ਸਦ ਕਾਮ॥ 6॥

Baarumbaar, baar Prubh(u) jupiyaiy.  Pee Amrit(u) eho mun(u) tun(u) dhrupiyaiy. Naam ruttan(u) jin(i) Gurmukh(i) paaya. Tis(u) kichh(u) uver(u) nuhee drishtaaya. Naamm(u) dhun, Naamo roop(u) rung(u). Naamo, sookh(u) Hur(i) Naam ka sung(u). Naam rus(i) jo jun triptaanay. Mun tun Naameh(i) Naam(i) smaanay.  Ootthut, baiytthut, sovut Naam. Kuhou Nanak, jun kaiy sud kaam.  6.

Word Meaning: Baarumbaar = again and again.    Dhrupiyaiy = get contented.   Ruttan = (precious) like a jewel.  Drishtaaya = sees; likes.  Triptaanay = contented.   Smaanay = stay absorbed.    Kaam = deeds; routine.   6.

MEANING:  We should recite Waheguru’s Naam again and again.  Waheguru’s Naam is that Amrit which causes contentment in our mind and body. A devotee, who acquires jewel-like precious Naam of Waheguru in the shelter of the Guru, does not like anything else.  For him, Waheguru’s Naam is his wealth and his good looks.  Waheguru’s company (shelter) and His Naam are all the comforts for him.  The devotees, who are contented with the sweetness of the Naam, stay absorbed in the Naam by body and by mind.  They recite the Naam while standing, sitting (resting), and even sleeping; and this is their regular routine, oh Nanak.   6.

ਬੋਲਹੁ ਜਸੁ ਜਿਹਬਾ ਦਿਨੁ ਰਾਤਿ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭਿ ਅਪਨੈ ਜਨ ਕੀਨੀ ਦਾਤਿ॥ ਕਰਹਿ ਭਗਤਿ ਆਤਮ ਕੈ ਚਾਇ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਅਪਨੇ ਸਿਉ ਰਹਹਿ ਸਮਾਇ॥ ਜੋ ਹੋਆ ਹੋਵਤ ਸੋ ਜਾਨੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਅਪਨੇ ਕਾ ਹੁਕਮੁ ਪਛਾਨੈ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੀ ਮਹਿਮਾ ਕਉਨੁ ਬਖਾਨਉ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਗੁਨੁ ਕਹਿ ਏਕ ਨ ਜਾਨਉ॥ ਆਠ ਪਹਰ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਬਸਹਿ ਹਜੂਰੇ॥ ਕਹੁ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੇਈ ਜਨ ਪੂਰੇ॥

Boalhou jus(u) jihba din(u) raat(i). Prubh(i) apnaiy jun keeni daat(i).  Kureh(i) bhugut(i) aatum kaiy chaayay. Prubh apnay siyoun ruheh(i) smaayay. Jo hoa, hovut so jaanaiy. Prubh apnay ka Huqam(u) puchhaanaiy. Tis kee mehma kaun bukhaanou. Tis ka goon keh aik na jaanou. Aatth pehr Prubh buseh(i) hujooray. Kuhou Nanak sayee jun pooray.  7.

Word Meaning: Jus(u) = praise.    Daat(i) = gift.   Aatum = soul; mind.   Chaayay = gusto.   Mehma = greatness; splendour.     Bukhaanou = describe.   Aatth Pehr = all twenty-four hours.    Hujooray = right in front; close by.   Pooray = perfect.   7.

MEANING: Recite Waheguru Naam with your tongue day and night, oh my friend. Waheguru’s devotee worships Him with full gusto and stays absorbed in Him.  (Actually, worship of Waheguru) is a gift from Him to His devotees.  The devotee recognizes (accepts) Waheguru’s Huqam and accepts things as they happen. Which greatness of the devotees can I describe? I do not know (cannot describe) even a single virtue of them, so I cannot say anything about them.  They “see” Waheguru around them all the twenty-four hours, and they are perfect, oh Nanak.  7.

ਮਨ ਮੇਰੇ ਤਿਨ ਕੀ ਓਟ ਲੇਹਿ॥ ਮਨੁ ਤਨੁ ਅਪਨਾ ਤਿਨ ਜਨ ਦੇਹਿ॥ ਜਿਨਿ ਜਨਿ ਅਪਨਾ ਪ੍ਰਭੂ ਪਛਾਤਾ॥ ਸੋ ਜਨ ਸਰਬ ਥੋਕ ਕਾ ਦਾਤਾ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੀ ਸਰਨਿ ਸਰਬ ਸੁਖ ਪਾਵਹਿ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੈ ਦਰਸਿ ਸਭ ਪਾਪ ਮਿਟਾਵਹਿ॥ ਅਵਰ ਸਿਆਨਪ ਸਗਲੀ ਛਾਡੁ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਕੀ ਤੂ ਸੇਵਾ ਲਾਗੁ॥ ਆਵਨੁ ਜਾਨੁ ਨ ਹੋਵੀ ਤੇਰਾ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਕੇ ਪੂਜਹੁ ਸਦ ਪੈਰਾ॥ 8॥17॥

Mun mayray, tin kee oat layh(i). Mun(u), tun(u) apna tin jun dayh(i). Jin(i) jun apna Prubhoo puchhaata.  So jun serb thoak ka daata.  Tis kee shern(i) serb sookh paaveh(i).  Tis kaiy durs(i) subh paap mitaaveh(i). Uver siyaanup suglee chhaad(u). Tis(u) jun kee tu sayva laag(u).  Aavun jaan(u) na hoavi tayra.  Nanak, tis(u) jun kay poojhou sud paiyra.   8.17. 

Word Meaning:   Oat = shelter.  Jun = devotee.  Puchhaata = recognized; got connected with.    Serb Thoak = everything.   Daata = source.   Shern(i) = shelter.  Ders(i) = (with the) sight.   Aavun(u) Jaan(u) = birth and death.   8.17.

MEANING:  Seek the shelter of the devotees of Waheguru, and offer your body and mind to them, oh my friend.  The devotees, who have recognized (made a connection with) their Waheguru, become the source of everything (become like Waheguru).  You enjoy all comforts in their shelter, and all sins are washed away with their kind sight.  So, give up all your wisdom, and start serving the devotees, oh my friends. Worship the feet of Waheguru’s devotees (act on their advice), and put an end to your birth and death cycle, oh Nanak.  8.17. 

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਸਤਿ ਪੁਰਖੁ ਜਿਨਿ ਜਾਨਿਆ ਸਤਿਗੁਰੁ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਨਾਉ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ ਸਿਖੁ ਉਧਰੈ ਨਾਨਕ ਹਰਿ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਉ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Sat(i) Purakh(u) jin(i) jaaniya, Satgur(u) Tis ka naaoun. Tis kaiy sung(i) Sikh(u) oodhraiy, Nanak, Hur(i) goon gaaou. 

Word Meaning: Sat(i) Purakh(u) = the eternal Waheguru.   Jaaniya = realized; made a connection with.         Oodhraiy = is salvaged. 

MEANING:  A holy saint, who has realized Waheguru (has become “one” with Him), is the Satguru.  A Sikh finds salvation in His company (shelter); so, sing Waheguru’s praise, oh Nanak.  1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਸਤਿਗੁਰੁ ਸਿਖ ਕੀ ਕਰੈ ਪ੍ਰਤਿਪਾਲ॥ ਸੇਵਕ ਕਉ ਗੁਰੁ ਸਦਾ ਦਇਆਲ॥ ਸਿਖ ਕੀ ਗੁਰੁ ਦੁਰਮਤਿ ਮਲੁ ਹਿਰੈ॥ ਗੁਰ ਬਚਨੀ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਉਚਰੈ॥ ਸਤਿਗੁਰੁ ਸਿਖ ਕੈ ਬੰਧਨ ਕਾਟੈ॥ ਗੁਰ ਕਾ ਸਿਖੁ ਬਿਕਾਰ ਤੇ ਹਾਟੈ॥ ਸਤਿਗੁਰੁ ਸਿਖ ਕਉ ਨਾਮ ਧਨੁ ਦੇਇ॥ ਗੁਰ ਕਾ ਸਿਖੁ ਵਡਭਾਗੀ ਹੇ॥ ਸਤਿਗੁਰੁ ਸਿਖ ਕਾ ਹਲਤੁ ਪਲਤੁ ਸਵਾਰੈ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸਤਿਗੁਰੁ ਸਿਖ ਕਉ ਜੀਅ ਨਾਲ ਸਮਾਰੈ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Satgur(u) Sikh kee kuraiy pritpaal. Sayvuk kuou Gur(u) suda duyaal. Sikh kee Gur(u) doormut(i) mul(u) hiraiy.  Gur Buchni Hur(i) Naam(u) oochraiy.  Satgur(u) Sikh kaiy bundhun kaataiy. Gur ka Sikh(u) bikaar tay haataiy.  Satgur(u) Sikh kuou Naam dhun(u) dayay. Gur ka Sikh vudbhaagi hay.  Satgur(u) Sikh ka hult(u) pult(u) svaaraiy. Nanak, Satgur(u) Sikh kuou jea naal(i) smaaraiy.  1.

Word Meaning:  Pritpaal = nurtures and cares.  Doormut(i) = ignoble thinking. Hiraiy = removes; takes away.  Bundhun = bonds (of Maya).  Bikaar(u) = vices.   Haataiy = desists; abstains.  Hult(u) = this world.  Pult(u) = the next world.   Svaaraiy = makes attractive (pleasant).   Smaaraiy = remembers; keeps in mind. 

MEANING: Satgur(u) spiritually nurtures and cares for His Sikhs and is always kind to them.   Satguru removes (washes away) the ignoble thinking of His Sikh, who recites Waheguru’s Naam with the Words (Shabads) of Satguru.  Satgur(u) cuts the bonds of Maya of His Sikh, and the Sikh abstains from all vices. Satgur(u)’s Sikh is very fortunate because Satgur(u) bestows the wealth of Waheguru’s Naam on His Sikh. Satgur(u) makes this world and the next world very pleasant for His Sikhs, and keeps them in mind like His own life, oh Nanak.1.   

ਗੁਰ ਕੈ ਗ੍ਰਿਹਿ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਜੋ ਰਹੈ॥ ਗੁਰ ਕੀ ਆਗਿਆ ਮਨ ਮਹਿ ਸਹੈ॥ ਆਪਸ ਕਉ ਕਰਿ ਕਛੁ ਨ ਜਨਾਵੈ॥ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਰਿਦੈ ਸਦ ਧਿਆਵੈ॥ ਮਨੁ ਬੇਚੈ ਸਤਿਗੁਰ ਕੈ ਪਾਸਿ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਸੇਵਕ ਕੇ ਕਾਰਜ ਰਾਸਿ॥ ਸੇਵਾ ਕਰਤ ਹੋਇ ਨਿਹਕਾਮੀ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕਉ ਹੋਤ ਪਰਾਪਤਿ ਸੁਆਮੀ॥

(ਅੰਗ 287)॥    ਅਪਨੀ ਕ੍ਰਿਪਾ ਜਿਸੁ ਆਪਿ ਕਰੇਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੋ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਗੁਰ ਕੀ ਮਤਿ ਲੇਇ॥2॥

Gur kaiy greh(i) sayvuk(u) jo ruhaiy. Gur kee aagiya mun meh(i) suhaiy.  Aapus kuou kur(i) kuchh(u) na junaavaiy.  Hur(i) Hur(i) Naam(u) ridaiy sud dhiyaavaiy. Mun(u) baychaiy Satgur kaiy paas(i). Tis(u) sayvuk kay kaaruj raas(i). Sayva kert hoyay nihkaami. Tis kuou hoat praaput(i) Soami. 

(PAGE 287):                          Apni kirpa jis(u) aap(I) kuray. Nanak so sayvuk(u) Gur kee mut(i) layay.  2.

Word Meaning:  Greh(i) = house; shelter.   Sayvuk = devotee; Sikh.  Suhaiy = endures; accepts gladly.   Junaavaiy = try to get recognition.   Baychaiy = sells; loses all claims.   Raas(i) = bear fruit.    Nihkaami = without any wish.   Soami = Lord.  2.
