MEANING:  A Sikh, who stays in the shelter of the Guru, obeys His orders (Will) from the depth of his mind; never tries to get his hard labour recognized by the Guru; recites Waheguru’s Naam all the time in his mind; surrenders his mind to Satguru, and relinquishes all the claims on his mind, gets all his objectives achieved.  Such a Sikh serves the Guru with dedication, without any expectation of rewards, and realizes Waheguru.  But only that devotee receives Guru’s guidance, whom Waheguru is kind to, oh Nanak.     2.

 

ਬੀਸ ਬਿਸਵੇ ਗੁਰ ਕਾ ਮਨੁ ਮਾਨੈ॥ ਸੋ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਪਰਮੇਸੁਰ ਕੀ ਗਤਿ ਜਾਨੈ॥ ਸੋ ਸਤਿਗੁਰ ਜਿਸੁ ਰਿਦੈ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਉ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਬਾਰ ਗੁਰ ਕਉ ਬਲਿ ਜਾਉ॥ ਸਰਬ ਨਿਧਾਨ ਜੀਅ ਕਾ ਦਾਤਾ॥ ਆਠ ਪਹਰ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਰੰਗਿ ਰਾਤਾ॥ ਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਮਹਿ ਜਨੁ ਜਨ ਮਹਿ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ॥ ਏਕਹਿ ਆਪਿ ਨਹੀ ਕਛੁ ਭਰਮੁ॥ ਸਹਸ ਸਿਆਨਪ ਲਇਆ ਨਾ ਜਾਈਐ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਐਸਾ ਗੁਰੁ ਬਡਭਾਗੀ ਪਾਈਐ॥ 3॥

Bees bisvay Gur ka mun(u) maanaiy.  So sayvuk(u) Permaysur kee gut(i) jaanaiy. So Satgur jis(u) ridaiy Hur(i) Naaoun.  Anik baar Gur kuou bul(i) jaaou.  Serb nidhaan, jea ka Daata. Aatth pehr Parbraham rung(i) raata.  Braham meh(i) jun(u), jun meh(i) Parbraham. Aikeh(i) Aap(i) nuhee kuchh(u) bherm(u). Sehs siyaanup luyya na jaayiyaiy. Nanak aisa Gur(u) budbhaageen paayiyaiy.  3.

Word Meaning:  Bees bisvaiy = one hundred percent; all.  Mun = mind (Wishes); Commands.  Gut(i) = spiritual height.     Nidhaan = treasures.   Daata = provider.  Rung(i) Raata = Imbued in love.   Jun(u) = devotee.   Bherm(u) = doubt; misgiving.    Sehs = thousands.   Siyaanup = cleverness.   Budbhaageen = with great luck.    3.

MEANING:  A devotee, who obeys all the commands of the Guru, recognizes the spiritual height of Waheguru (makes a connection with Him).  The Satgur is that holy saint who has Waheguru’s Naam at heart all the time; I wish to lay down my life for the Guru countless times.   The Guru is always imbued with the love of Waheguru and (like Waheguru) is the source of everything, providing for the needs of His Sikhs. The Guru resides in Waheguru while Waheguru resides in the Guru; there should be absolutely no doubt that Waheguru is “one” with the Guru.  Such a Guru cannot be realized with any cleverness, even if one has thousands of them; He can be realized only with great fortune, oh Nanak.   3.

ਸਫਲ ਦਰਸਨੁ ਪੇਖਤ ਪੁਨੀਤ॥ ਪਰਸਤ ਚਰਨ ਗਤਿ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਰੀਤਿ॥ ਭੇਟਤ ਸੰਗਿ ਰਾਮ ਗੁਨ ਰਵੇ॥ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਕੀ ਦਰਗਹ ਗਵੇ॥ ਸੁਨਿ ਕਰਿ ਬਚਨ ਕਰਨ ਆਘਾਨੇ॥ ਮਨਿ ਸੰਤੋਖੁ ਆਤਮ ਪਤੀਆਨੇ॥ ਪੂਰਾ ਗੁਰੁ ਅਖ੍ਹਓ ਜਾ ਕਾ ਮੰਤ੍ਰ॥ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਪੇਖੈ ਹੋਇ ਸੰਤ॥ ਗੁਣ ਬਿਅੰਤ ਕੀਮਤਿ ਨਹੀ ਪਾਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਲਏ ਮਿਲਾਇ॥ 4॥

Suful durshun(u) paykhut pooneet.  Persut churn gut(i) nirmul reet(i).  Bhaytut sung(i) Ram goon ruvay.  Parbraham kee Durgeh guvay.  Soon(i) kur(i) Buchun kern aghaanay. Mun(i) suntoakh(u) aatum putiyaanay. Poora Gur(u) ukhiyo ja ka Muntar(u). Amrit drisht(i) paykhaiy hoyay sunt.  Goon biyunt, keemut(i) nuhee paayay. Nanak, jis(u) bhaavaiy, tis(u) luyay milaayay.  4.

Word Meaning:  Suful = full of rewards.   Paykhut = just on seeing.    Pooneet = pious; pure.    Persut = just by touching.   Churn = feet.   Nirmul = pure; holy.   Reet(i) = living-style; daily routine.     Bhytut = on meeing.     Ruvay = recites.    Guvay = has access.  Buchun = Words; Advice.   Kern = ears.   Aghaanay = are contented.   Suntoakh = patience; contentment.   Putiyaanay = is won over; gets comfortable.   Akhiyo = immortal.  Muntar = Teaching.   Drisht(i) = sight.       Paykhaiy = on seeing.    Biyunt = countless.    Keemut(i) = value.   4.

MEANING:  The sight of the Guru is highly rewarding (blissful), and the devotee’s mind becomes pure by simply having His sight (with full faith and reverence).   The devotee attains a higher spiritual state by touching the Guru's feet (surrendering himself completely), and his way of life becomes very pure.  On meeting the Guru, the Sikh (devotee) begins to sing of Waheguru’s virtues and gains access to Waheguru’s Court (becomes connected with Him).  His ears are fully satisfied by listening to the Word of the Guru (has no desire to listen to any other entertaining talk). His mind is completely won over by the Guru’s Word, and he acquires full mental contentment.  The Guru, whose Word is immortal, has an Amrit-raining kind sight; the devotee, who gets the Guru’s kind sight cast on him, becomes a saint instantly.  Waheguru, who has countless virtues that no one can evaluate, connects the individual of His choice with Him, oh Nanak.  4.

ਜਿਹਬਾ ਏਕ ਉਸਤਤਿ ਅਨੇਕ॥ ਸਤਿ ਪੁਰਖ ਪੂਰਨ ਬਿਬੇਕ॥ ਕਾਹੂ ਬੋਲ ਨ ਪਹੁਚਤ ਪ੍ਰਾਨੀ॥ ਅਗਮ ਅਗੋਚਰ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਨਿਰਬਾਨੀ॥ ਨਿਰਾਹਾਰ ਨਿਰਵੈਰ ਸੁਖਦਾਈ॥ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਕੀਮਤਿ ਕਿਨੈ ਨ ਪਾਈ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਭਗਤ ਬੰਦਨ ਨਿਤ ਕਰਹਿ॥ ਚਰਨ ਕਮਲ ਹਿਰਦੈ ਸਿਮਰਹਿ॥ ਸਦ ਬਲਿਹਾਰੀ ਸਤਿਗੁਰ ਅਪਨੇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਿਸੁ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਐਸਾ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਜਪਨੇ॥ 5॥

Jihba aik, oostut(i) anayk. Sat(i) Purakh, poorun bibayk.  Kaahoo boal na puhoochut praani.  Agum agoachur Prubh nirbaani.  Nirahaar, nirvair sookhdaayee. Ta kee keemut(i) kinaiy na paayee.   Anik bhugut bundun nit kureh(i). Churn kumul hirdaiy simreh(i). Sud Bulihaari Sargur apnay. Nanak, jis(u) persaad(i) aiysa Prubh jupnay.  5

Word Meaning:  Jihba = tongue.     Oostut(i) = (virtues worthy of) praise.    Bibayk = Divine Knowledge.    Boal = words.     Puhoochut = can match; rival.   Agumm = inaccessible.    Agoachur = beyond comprehension.     Nirbaani = fully detached.    Nirahaar = needs no food.     Bundun = bow to (in reverence).     Churn Kumul = lotus-like feet (Naam).   Simreh(i) = remember.    5.

MEANING: Waheguru is immortal, omnipresent, is (source of) all  Knowledge, and has countless praise-worthy virtues; but the man has only one tongue (so how can he sing all virtues of Waheguru)?  No words can reach (describe) all the virtues of Waheguru as He is inaccessible, beyond comprehension, and completely detached from Maya.  He needs no food, bears no enmity with anyone (is perfectly just) and comforting; and nobody can ever evaluate Him.  Countless devotees bow to Him every day (all the time) and recite His Naam in their hearts.  I am always willing to lay down my life for my Satguru, with whose grace one recites Waheguru’s Naam, oh Nanak.   5. 

ਇਹੁ ਹਰਿ ਰਸੁ ਪਾਵੈ ਜਨੁ ਕੋਇ॥ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤੁ ਪੀਵੈ ਅਮਰੁ ਸੋ ਹੋਇ॥ ਉਸੁ ਪੁਰਖ ਕਾ ਨਾਹੀ ਕਦੇ ਬਿਨਾਸ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਪਰਗਟੇ ਗੁਨਤਾਸ॥ ਆਠ ਪਹਰ ਹਰਿ ਕਾ ਨਾਮੁ ਲੇਇ॥ ਸਚੁ ਉਪਦੇਸੁ ਸੇਵਕ ਕਉ ਦੇਇ॥ ਮੋਹ ਮਾਇਆ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ ਨ ਲੇਪੁ॥ ਮਨ ਮਹਿ ਰਾਖੈ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਏਕੁ॥ ਅੰਧਕਾਰ ਦੀਪਕ ਪਰਗਾਸੇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਭਰਮ ਮੋਹ ਦੁਖ ਤਹ ਤੇ ਨਾਸੇ॥ 6॥

Ehou Hur(i) rus(u) paaaiy jun(u) koyay. Amrit peevaiy amar(u) so hoyay.  Oos(u) purakh ka naahi kuday binaas. Ja kaiy mun(i) pergutay Goontaas. Aatth pehr Hur(i) ka Naam(u) layay. Sach(u) oopdaysh(u) sayvuk kuou dayay.  Moh Maya kaiy sung(i) na layp(u).  Mun meh(i) raakhaiy Hur(i) Hur(i) aik(u).  Undhkaar deepuk pergaasay. Nanak, bherm moh dookh teh tay naasay.  6.

Word Meaning:   Hur(i) Rus(u) = Amrit-like sweet taste of Naam.   Amar(u) = immortal.   Binaas = destruction; death.    Goontaas = treasure of virtues (Waheguru).   Pergutay = appears; takes dwelling.   Sach(u) Oopdaysh = everlasting advice.   Layp(u) = smeared with.    Pergaasay = lights up.    Bherm = doubts.   6.

MEANING:  Some rare devotees drink the sweet Amrit of Waheguru’s Naam, and whosoever drinks it, becomes immortal (by getting merged into Waheguru).  The individual, in whose mind the treasure of virtues, Waheguru, appears, never dies (becomes immortal).  He recites Waheguru’s Naam constantly and offers everlasting advice to his followers. He is not smeared with (is unaffected by) the love for Maya, and keeps only Waheguru in mind.  Like a lamp, which dispels darkness with its light, Guru’s Teachings enlighten the mind of the devotee, dispelling all their misgivings, worldly attachments, and discomforts, oh Nanak.   6.

ਤਪਤ ਮਾਹਿ ਠਾਢਿ ਵਰਤਾਈ॥ ਅਨਦੁ ਭਇਆ ਦੁਖੁ ਨਾਠੇ ਭਾਈ॥ ਜਨਮ ਮਰਨ ਕੇ ਮਿਟੇ ਅੰਦੇਸੇ॥ ਸਾਧੂ ਕੇ ਪੂਰਨ ਉਪਦੇਸੇ॥ ਭਉ ਚੂਕਾ ਨਿਰਭਉ ਹੋਇ ਬਸੇ॥ ਸਗਲ ਬਿਆਧਿ ਮਨ ਤੇ ਖੈ ਨਸੇ॥ ਜਿਸ ਕਾ ਸਾ ਤਿਨਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਧਾਰੀ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਜਪਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਮੁਰਾਰੀ॥ ਥਿਤਿ ਪਾਈ ਚੂਕੇ ਭ੍ਰਮ ਗਵਨ॥ ਸੁਨਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਜਸੁ ਸ੍ਰਵਨ॥ 7॥

Tupt maah(i) tthaandh(i) vertaayee.  Anund bhuyaa, dookh(u) naatthay bhaayee.  Junum mern kay mitay andaysay. Sadhu kay poorun oopdaysay.  Bhuou chooka, nirbhuou hoyay busay.  Sugul biyaadh(i) mun tay khaiy, nussay. Jis ka sa Tin(i) kirpa dhaari. Sadh sung(i) jup(i) Naam(u) Mooraari. Thit(i) paayee, chookay bherm, guvun. Soon(i) Nanak, Hur(i) Hur(i) jus(u) servun.  7.

Word Meaning:  Tupt = heat; fire of desires.    Tthaandh(i) = calmness; peace.   Vertaayee = created; disseminated.    Anund = blissfulness.  Andaysay = worries; anxieties.   Chooka = ended.    Biyaadh(i) = ailments.   Khaiy = vanish.  Nussay = ran away; disappeared.     Tin(i) = that (Waheguru).    Mooraari = destroyer of the demon Moor (Waheguru).    Thit(i) = peace of mind.   Bherm = misgivings.    Guvun = rebirths.   Servun = (with) ears.    7.

MEANING:  The Guru disseminates peace and calmness by His Words on the minds suffering from the fire of desires. As a result, all their troubles run away, and the devotees feel blissful, and all their worries about death and rebirth are dispelled, too.  All their worldly fears come to an end, and they lead a fearless life, and all their mental ailments vanish.  When Waheguru, to whom the man belongs, becomes very kind to His devotees, they worship His Naam in the holy shelter of the Guru.  By listening to the praise of Waheguru with the ears, the devotee attains peace of mind; all his doubts are cleared, and he is not worried about rebirths, oh Nanak.    7. 

ਨਿਰਗੁਨੁ ਆਪਿ ਸਰਗੁਨ ਭੀ ਓਹੀ॥ ਕਲਾ ਧਾਰਿ ਜਿਨਿ ਸਗਲੀ ਮੋਹੀ॥ ਅਪਨੇ ਚਰਿਤ ਪ੍ਰਭਿ ਆਪਿ ਬਨਾਏ॥ ਅਪੁਨੀ ਕੀਮਤਿ ਆਪੇ ਪਾਏ॥ ਹਰਿ ਬਿਨੁ ਦੂਜਾ ਨਾਹੀ ਕੋਇ॥ ਸਰਬ ਨਿਰੰਤਰਿ ਏਕੋ ਸੋਇ॥ ਓਤਿ ਪੋਤਿ ਰਵਿਆ ਰੂਪ ਰੰਗ॥ ਭਏ ਪਰਗਾਸ ਸਾਧ ਕੈ ਸੰਗ॥

(ਅੰਗ 288)॥     ਰਚਿ ਰਚਨਾ ਅਪਨੀ ਕਲ ਧਾਰੀ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਬਾਰ ਨਾਨਕ ਬਲਿਹਾਰੀ॥ 8॥18॥

Nirgoon Aap(i) surgoon bhee Ohee.  Kula dhaar(i) Jin(i) suglee mohee.  Apnay chrit Prubh(i) aap(i) bunaayay.  Apuni keemut(i) Aapay paayay. Hur(i) bin(u) dooja naahi koyay. Serb niruntar(i) aiko Soyay. Oat(i) poat(i) ruviya roop rung(i). Bhuyay pergaas Sadh kaiy sung. 

(PAGE 288):        Ruch(i) ruchna apni kul dhaari. Anik baar Nanak bulihaari.  8.18.

Word Meaning:  Nirgoon = formless; above the effects of Maya.   Aap(i) = Waheguru Himself.   Surgoon = in the form of visible nature.    Kula = power.   Dhaar(i) = supports.   Mohi = captivated.   Chrit = amazing structures.   Niruntar(i) = continuous.  Oat(i) Poat(i) = like cross threads in fabric.    Ruviya = present.    Pergaas = enlightenment.   Ruchna = creation.   Bulihaari = offer life.   8.18

MEANING: Waheguru is formless (above the effects of Maya), and is also present in His creation at the same time. He has captivated the whole world with His powers in different forms.  He has created amazing structures, and only He knows His worth (powers). There is none like Him, and He is present in the whole world without any gap.  He is mingled with His nature like the weft and woof (cross threads) of fabric in different colours and forms.  He is revealed (one gets enlightened) in the shelter of the Guru.  After creating the world, He is supporting it with His powers. I wish to sacrifice my life for Him countless times, oh Nanak.   8.18.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਸਾਥਿ ਨਾ ਚਾਲੈ ਬਿਨੁ ਭਜਨ ਬਿਖਿਆ ਸਗਲੀ ਛਾਰੁ॥ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਕਮਾਵਨਾ ਨਾਨਕ ਇਹੁ ਧਨੁ ਸਾਰੁ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Saath(i) na chaalaiy bin(u) bhujun, bikhiya suglee chhaar(u).  Hur(i) Hur(i) Naam(u) kmaavna, Nanak eho dhun(u) saar(u).   1.

Word Meaning: Bikhiya = Maya (and its enjoyment).  Chhaar(u) = ash; dust.  Kmaavna = earn; work towards.   Saar(u) = supreme. 

MEANING: Nothing, other than Waheguru’s, accompanies the man after death. The enjoyments of Maya are like dust (which mixes with the dust of the man).  Waheguru’s Naam is the supreme wealth that a man can earn, oh Nanak. 1. 

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਸੰਤ ਜਨਾ ਮਿਲਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਬੀਚਾਰੁ॥ ਏਕੁ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਨਾਮ ਆਧਾਰੁ॥ ਅਵਰਿ ਉਪਾਵ ਸਭਿ ਮੀਤ ਬਿਸਾਰਹੁ॥ ਚਰਨ ਕਮਲ ਰਿਦ ਮਹਿ ਉਰਿ ਧਾਰਹੁ॥ ਕਰਨ ਕਰਾਵਨ ਸੋ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਸਮਰਥੁ॥ ਦ੍ਰਿੜ ਕਰਿ ਗਹਹੁ ਨਾਮੁ ਹਰਿ ਵਥੁ॥ ਇਹੁ ਧਨੁ ਸੰਚਹੁ ਹੋਵਹੁ ਭਗਵੰਤ॥ ਸੰਤ ਜਨਾ ਕਾ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਮੰਤ॥ ਏਕ ਆਸ ਰਾਖਹੁ ਮਨ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਸਰਬ ਰੋਗ ਨਾਨਕ ਮਿਟਿ ਜਾਹਿ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Sunt junaa mil(i) kurhou beechaar(u). Aik(u) simer(i) Naam aadhaar(u). uver(i) oopaav subh(i) meet bisaarhou. Churn kumul rid meh(i) oor(i) dhaarhou. Kern kraavun so Prubh(u) sumruth(u).  Drirr kur(u) guhou Naam(u) Hur(i) vuth(u). Ehou dhun(u) sunchou, hovhou bhugvunt.  Sunt junaa ka nirmul munt.  Aik aas raakhou mun maah(i). Serb roag Nanak mit jaah(i).  1.

Word Meaning:  Aik(u) = One (Waheguru).   Adhaar = basis; support.   Uver(i) = other.  Oopaav = efforts; plans.   Bisaarhou = forget; give up.   Churn Kumul = lotus-like feet of Waheguru (His Naam).    Oor(i) = in the heart.  Dhaarou = implant.   Sumruth = omnipotent.   Drirr = reinforce with full faith.   Guhou = hold; embrace.  Vuth(u) = (valuable) article.  Sunchou = accumulate.   Bhugvunt = lucky; rich.   Munt = Teaching.   Aas = dependence.   Mit(i) = erased; eradicated.   1.

MEANING: Ponder on Waheguru’s Naam in the company of holy saints, oh my friend.  Waheguru’s Naam is the basis of life, so recite only the Naam. Forget all other plans, and enshrine Waheguru’s Naam in your heart. Waheguru is omnipotent; He does everything and gets others to do whatever He wishes.  His Naam is the most valuable thing, so reinforce it in your heart with full faith. Accumulate this wealth and become (spiritually) wealthy. This is the holy advice of the saints. Have full faith in (dependence upon) Waheguru, and all your ailments will be eradicated, oh Nanak.   1.

ਜਿਸੁ ਧਨ ਕਉ ਚਾਰਿ ਕੁੰਟ ਉਠਿ ਧਾਵਹਿ॥ ਸੋ ਧਨ ਹਰਿ ਸੇਵਾ ਤੇ ਪਾਵਹਿ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਸੁਖ ਕਉ ਨਿਤ ਬਾਛਹਿ ਮੀਤ॥ ਸੋ ਸੁਖੁ ਸਾਧੂ ਸੰਗਿ ਪਰੀਤਿ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਸੋਭਾ ਕਉ ਕਰਹਿ ਭਲੀ ਕਰਨੀ॥ ਸਾ ਸੋਭਾ ਭਜੁ ਹਰਿ ਕੀ ਸਰਨੀ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਉਪਾਵੀ ਰੋਗੁ ਨ ਜਾਇ॥ ਰੋਗੁ ਮਿਟੈ ਹਰਿ ਅਵਖਧੁ ਲਾਇ॥ ਸਰਬ ਨਿਧਾਨ ਮਹਿ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਨਿਧਾਨੁ॥ ਜਪਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਦਰਗਹਿ ਪਰਵਾਨੁ॥ 2॥

Jis(u) dhun kuou chaa(i) koont ootth(i) dhaaveh(i). So dhun Hur(i) sayva tay paaveh(i). Jis(u) sookh kuou nit baanchheh(i) meet.  So sookh(u) Sadhu sung(i) preet(i). Jis(u) sobha kuou kureh(i) bhulee kerni.  Sa sobha bhuj(u) Hur(i) kee sherni. Anik oopaaveen roag(u) na jaayay. Roag(u) mitaiy, Hur(i) avkhudh(u) laayay. Serb nidhaan meh(i) Hur(i) Naam(u) nidhaan(u). Jup(i) Nanak Durgeh pervaan(u).    2.

Word Meaning: Chaar(i) Koont = four directions (all over the world).  Dhaaveh(i) = rush to.    Baanchheh(i) = wish for; seek.  Sobha = praise.   Bhulee Kerni = noble deeds.    Oopaaveen = measures; plans.  Roag(u) = ailment (of selfishness).    Avkhudh(u) = medication.   Nidhaan = treasures.   2.

MEANING:  The wealth, for which you rush in every direction (to enjoy happiness), can be acquired by serving Waheguru (as He is the real source of happiness), oh my friend.  The comfort, which you seek all the time, can be achieved in the company of the Guru (with love and respect).  The praise for which you do all the noble deeds can be acquired by rushing to seek the shelter of Waheguru.  There is no other means to have your ailment (of worldly attachments and selfishness) eradicated than to apply the medication of Waheguru’s Naam, which cures every ailment.   The most useful treasure of all is Waheguru’s Naam; reciting it earns one approval in Waheguru’s Court, oh Nanak.      2.

ਮਨੁ ਪਰਬੋਧਹੁ ਹਰਿ ਕੈ ਨਾਇ॥ ਦਹ ਦਿਸਿ ਧਾਵਤ ਆਵੈ ਠਾਇ॥ ਤਾ ਕਉ ਬਿਘਨੁ ਨਾ ਲਾਗੈ ਕੋਇ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਰਿਦੈ ਬਸੈ ਹਰਿ ਸੋਇ॥ ਕਲਿ ਤਾਤੀ ਠਾਢਾ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਉ॥ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸਦਾ ਸੁਖ ਪਾਉ॥ ਭਉ ਬਿਨਸੈ ਪੂਰਨ ਹੋਇ ਆਸ॥ ਭਗਤਿ ਭਾਇ ਆਤਮ ਪਰਗਾਸ॥ ਤਿਤੁ ਘਰਿ ਜਾਇ ਬਸੈ ਅਬਿਨਾਸੀ॥ ਕਹੁ ਨਾਨਕ ਕਾਟੀ ਜਮ ਫਾਸੀ॥3

Mun(u) perbodhou Hur(i) kaiy Naayay.  Deh dis(i) dhaavut, aavaiy tthaayay.  Ta kuou bighun(u) na laagaiy koyay. Ja kaiy ridaiy busaiy Hur(i) soyay. Kul(i) taati, tthaandha Hur(i) Naaoun. Simer(i) simer(i) suda sookh paaou. Bhuou binsaiy, poorun hoyay aas.  Bhugut(i) bhaayay, aatum pergaas.  Tit(u) ghur(i) jaayay busaiy abinaashi.  Kuhou Nanak kaati jum phaasi.   3.

Word Meaning: Perbodhou = awaken.   Tthaayay = the real place (peace; no wavering).    Bighun = hindrance; impediment.  Kul(i) = the world.   Taati = causing suffering (from the fire of desires).   Tthaandha = cooling.    Simer(i) = by reciting.   Binsaiy = vanishes.   Aatum Pergaas = enlightenment of the mind.  Tit(u) Ghur(i) = Waheguru’s Residence.   Abinaashi = eternal.     Phaasi = noose.   3.

MEANING: Awaken (enlighten) your mind with Waheguru’s Naam whereby it will rest in its own place (quit wandering, and be peaceful), oh my friend.  An individual who has Waheguru’s Naam embedded in his heart faces no impediment (to be “one” with God).  The world is suffering in the fire of worldly desires, but Waheguru’s Naam has a cooling (soothing) effect, and one can enjoy eternal comfort by reciting His Naam. All the fears of the man vanish, all his wishes are fulfilled, and his mind gets enlightened with his love for Waheguru’s worship.  Such a devotee finds his eternal abode in Waheguru, and the noose of the Angel of death for him is severed (his rebirth cycle comes to an end), oh Nanak.   3.

ਤਤੁ ਬੀਚਾਰੁ ਕਹੈ ਜਨੁ ਸਾਚਾ॥ ਜਨਮਿ ਮਰੈ ਸੋ ਕਾਚੋ ਕਾਚਾ॥ ਆਵਾ ਗਵਨੁ ਮਿਟੈ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਸੇਵ॥ ਆਪੁ ਤਿਆਗਿ ਸਰਨਿ ਗੁਰਦੇਵ॥ ਇਉ ਰਤਨ ਜਨਮ ਕਾ ਹੋਇ ਉਧਾਰੁ॥ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਾਨ ਆਧਾਰੁ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਉਪਾਵ ਨ ਛੂਟਨਹਾਰੇ॥ ਸਿੰਮ੍ਰਿਤਿ ਸਾਸਤ ਬੇਦ ਬੀਚਾਰੇ॥ ਹਰਿ ਕੀ ਭਗਤਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਮਨੁ ਲਾਇ॥ ਮਨਿ ਬੰਛਤ ਨਾਨਕ ਫਲ ਪਾਇ॥ 4॥

Tut(u) beechaar(u) kuhaiy jun(u) Saacha.  Junum(i) muraiy so kaacho kaacha.  Aava guvun(u) mitaiy Prubh sayv.  Aap(u) tiyaag(i) shern(i) Gurdev.  Iyoun ruttan junum ka hoyay oodhaar(u).  Hur(i) Hur(i) simer(i) praan aadhaar(u).  Anik oopaav na chhootunhaaray. Simrit, Shast, Bayd beechaaray.  Hur(i) kee bhugut(i) kurhou mun(u) laayay. Mun(i) bunchhut Nanak ful paayay.   4.

Word Meaning: Tut(i) = essence, the origin (Waheguru). Sacha = spiritually perfect; immortal. Kaacho, Kaacha = imperfect  Aava Guvun(u) = birth and death (cycle).  Aap(u) = selfishness.  Tiyaag(i) = by abandoning; giving up.  Ruttan = (valuable like a) jewel.  Oodhaar = salvation.    Aadhaar = basis.   Anik = countless.   Chhootunhaaray = able to get freedom from the bonds of Maya.  Bunchhut = as wished.   4.

MEANING: A devotee, who ponders on Waheguru and recites His Naam, becomes spiritually perfect and immortal (as he merges into Waheguru), but the individual, who is caught in the birth-death cycle, is imperfect and perishable.  The birth-death cycle is broken (eradicated) by serving Waheguru. (To do that) One needs to take shelter of the Guru and abandon one's selfishness.  The jewel-like, valuable human life can be salvaged by reciting Waheguru’s Naam, which is the basis of all life.  No other measure, like pondering on the Vedas, the Srimad Bhagavatam, and the Shastras, can free a man from the bonds of Maya.  But, by worshipping Waheguru with full dedication (and reverence), one gets the rewards of his choice, oh Nanak.      4.

ਸੰਗਿ ਨ ਚਾਲਸ ਤੇਰੇ ਧਨਾ॥ ਤੂੰ ਕਿਆ ਲਪਟਾਵਹਿ ਮੂਰਖ ਮਨਾ॥ ਸੁਤ ਮੀਤ ਕੁਟੰਬ ਅਰੁ ਬਨਿਤਾ॥ ਇਨ ਤੇ ਕਹਹੁ ਤੁਮ ਕਵਨ ਸਨਾਥਾ॥ ਰਾਜ ਰੰਗ ਮਾਇਆ ਬਿਸਥਾਰ॥ ਇਨ ਤੇ ਕਹਹੁ ਕਵਨ ਛੁਟਕਾਰ॥ ਅਸੁ ਹਸਤੀ ਰਥ ਅਸਵਾਰੀ॥ ਝੂਠਾ ਡੰਫ ਝੂਠੁ ਪਾਸਾਰੀ॥ ਜਿਨਿ ਦੀਏ ਤਿਸੁ ਬੁਝੈ ਨ ਬਿਗਾਨਾ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਬਿਸਾਰਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਪਛੁਤਾਨਾ॥ 5

Sung(i) na chaalus tayray dhuna.  Tu kiya luptaaveh(i), moorukh muna.  Soot, meet, kootumb, ur(u) bunita. Inn tay kuhou, toom kuvun snaatha. Raj rung Maya bisthaar. Inn tay kuhou kuvun chhootkaar.  Us(u), hustee, ruth usvaari. Jhoottha dumf, jhootth(u) paasaari. Jin(i) deeyay, Tis(u) boojhaiy na bigaana.  Naam(u) bisaar(i) Nanak puchhutaana.   5. 

Word Meaning: Sung(i) Na Chaalus = will not accompany.   Luptaaveh(i) = clinging to.   Soot = sons.   Koorumb = family.  Bunita = wife.   Snaatha = guardian; saviour.    Bisthaar = expansion; network.    Chhootkaar = liberator (from the bonds of Maya).  Us(u) = horse.  Hustee = elephant.   Ruth = chariot.   Usvaari = ride.   Jhoottha = false; short-lived.   Dumf = show off.   Boojhaiy = recognize (make connection with).   Bigaana = ignorant.    Bisaar(i) = forgetting.   5.

MEANING: Money will not accompany you at death, so what are you clinging to, oh my foolish mind? Tell me, who can be your saviour among your sons, friends, family, and your wife at the time of your death?  Who can get you freedom from the false pleasures of ruling (some state), and of the network of Maya? Riding of horses, elephants and chariots, and other expansions of Maya, are all false (short-lived) show-offs. The ignorant person makes no connection with Waheguru, who has given him all such luxuries.  Forgetting His Naam, the man repents at the end, oh Nanak.  5.

ਗੁਰ ਕੀ ਮਤਿ ਤੂੰ ਲੇਹਿ ਇਆਨੇ॥ ਭਗਤਿ ਬਿਨਾ ਬਹੁ ਡੂਬੇ ਸਿਆਨੇ॥ ਹਰਿ ਕੀ ਭਗਤਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਮਨ ਮੀਤ॥ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਹੋਇ ਤੁਮ੍ਹਾਰੋ ਚੀਤ॥ ਚਰਨ ਕਮਲ ਰਾਖਹੁ ਮਨ ਮਾਹਿ॥

(ਅੰਗ 289)॥    ਜਨਮ ਜਨਮ ਕੇ ਕਿਲਬਿਖ ਜਾਹਿ॥ ਆਪਿ ਜਪਹੁ ਅਵਰਾ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਾਵਹੁ॥ ਸੁਨਤ ਕਹਤ ਰਹਤ ਗਤਿ ਪਾਵਹੁ॥ ਸਾਰ ਭੂਤ ਸਤਿ ਹਰਿ ਕੋ ਨਾਉ॥ ਸਹਜਿ ਸੁਭਾਇ ਨਾਨਕ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਉ॥ 6॥

Gur kee mut(i) tu layh(i) iyaanay. Bhugut(i) bina buhou doobay siyaanay. Hur(i) kee bhugut(i) kurhou mun meet. Nirmul hoyay toomhaaro cheet.  Churn kumul raakhou mun maah(i).

(PAGE 289):             Junum junum kay kilbikh jaah(i). Aap(i) jup-hou, uveraah Naam(u) jupaavhou. Soonut, keht, reht, gut(i) paavhou. Saar bhoot Sat(i) Hur(i) ko Naaoun.  Sehj(i) soobhaayay, Nanak goon gaaou.   6.

Word Meaning:  Mut(i) = advice.  Iyaanay = immature; ignorant.   Churn Kumul = Waheguru’s Naam.   Kilvikh = sins.   Reht = living (following code of ethics).    Saar = supreme.  Bhoot = products.   Sehj Soobhaayay = with love and a peaceful mind.     6.

MEANING:  Take the advice of the Guru, oh, immature person. Many people, who considered themselves very wise, have drowned in the world-sea without Waheguru’s worship. Worship Waheguru in your mind, and your mind will become pure, oh my friend.  Keep Waheguru’s Naam in your mind all the time, as a result of which, all your sins will disappear.  Recite Waheguru’s Naam yourself, and make others do the same. Those who listen to, or recite Waheguru’s Naam, and live their life as per the Guru’s advice, attain a high spiritual state (get connected with God). Waheguru’s eternal Naam is the supreme of all the desired products.   (Embed Waheguru’s Naam in your heart) and sing His virtues with love and a peaceful mind, oh Nanak.   6.

ਗੁਨ ਗਾਵਤ ਤੇਰੀ ਉਤਰਸਿ ਮੈਲੁ॥ ਬਿਨਸਿ ਜਾਇ ਹਉਮੈ ਬਿਖੁ ਫੈਲੁ॥ ਹੋਹਿ ਅਚਿੰਤੁ ਬਸੈ ਸੁਖ ਨਾਲਿ॥ ਸਾਸਿ ਗ੍ਰਾਸਿ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਸਮਾਲਿ॥ ਛਾਡਿ ਸਿਆਨਪ ਸਗਲੀ ਮਨਾ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਪਾਵਹਿ ਸਚੁ ਧਨਾ॥ ਹਰਿ ਪੂੰਜੀ ਸੰਚਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਬਿਉਹਾਰੁ॥ ਈਹਾ ਸੁਖੁ ਦਰਗਹ ਜੈਕਾਰੁ॥ ਸਰਬ ਨਿਰੰਤਰਿ ਏਕੋ ਦੇਖੁ॥ ਕਹੁ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਮਸਤਕਿ ਲੇਖੁ॥ 7॥

Goon gaavut, tayri ootrus(i) maiyl(u). Binus(i) jaayay haumein bikh(u) phaiyl(u). Hohay achint(u), busaiy sookh naal(i). Saas(i) giraas(i) Hur(i) Naam(u) smaal(i). Chhaad(i) siyaanup suglee muna.  Sadh sung(i) paaveh(i) Sach(u) dhuna. Hur(i) poonji sunch(i) kurhou biyohaar. Eehaan sookh(u) Durgeh jaikaar. Serb niruntar(i) Aiko daykh(u). Kuhou Nanak ja kaiy mustuk(i) laykh(u).  7. 

Word Meaning:   Ootrus(i) = will be washed off.  Maiyl(u) = filth (of vices).  Binus(u) = vanish.   Bikh = poison (Maya).   Phaiyl(u) = deeds; acts.   Achint(u) = worry-free.   Graas(i) = (with every) bite.   Smaal(i) = remember.   Sunch(i) = accumulate.     Biyohaar(u) = business; dealings.  Eehaan = here (in present life).    Jaikaar = hailing; acclaiming.      Niruntar(i) = continuous; everywhere.   7.

MEANING:  All the filth of your mind will be washed away by singing Waheguru’s virtues, and all your ignoble deeds under the influence of your ego and Maya will be erased.  If you remember Waheguru with every breath and every bite of food, all your worries will disappear, and you will live in peace and comfort.  Give up all your wisdom, oh my mind, and join the company of the Guru, where you will acquire the real wealth (Waheguru’s Naam). Make accumulating the wealth of Waheguru’s Naam your business (daily behaviour), as a result of which, you will have comfort in the present life, and will be acclaimed in Waheguru’s Court. But only that lucky individual “sees” Waheguru everywhere who has prewritten fortune on his forehead, oh Nanak.  7.

ਏਕੋ ਜਪਿ ਏਕੋ ਸਾਲਾਹਿ॥ ਏਕੁ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਏਕੋ ਮਨ ਆਹਿ॥ ਏਕਸ ਕੇ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਉ ਅਨੰਤ॥ ਮਨਿ ਤਨਿ ਜਾਪਿ ਏਕ ਭਗਵੰਤ॥ ਏਕੋ ਏਕੁ ਏਕੁ ਹਰਿ ਆਪਿ॥ ਪੂਰਨ ਪੂਰਿ ਰਹਿਓ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਬਿਆਪਿ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਬਿਸਥਾਰ ਏਕ ਤੇ ਭਏ॥ ਏਕੁ ਅਰਾਧਿ ਪਰਾਛਤ ਗਏ॥ ਮਨ ਤਨ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਏਕੁ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਰਾਤਾ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਇਕੁ ਜਾਤਾ॥8॥19॥

Aiko jup(i), Aiko salaah(I). Aiko simer(i) Aiko mun aah(i).  Aikus kay goon gaaou anunt. Mun(i) tun(i) jaap(i) aik Bhugvunt. Aiko aik(u), aik(u) Hur(i) Aap(i). Poorun poor(i) ruhiyo Prubh biyaap(i). Anik bisthaar Aik tay bhuyay. Aik(u) araadh(i) praachhut guyay.  Mun, tun untar(i) aik(u) Prubh raata. Gur persaad(i) Nanak Ikk(u) jaata.  8.19.

Word Meaning:  Aiko = the One (Waheguru).   Saalaah(i) = sing praise.  Aah(i) = wish for.   Anunt = unlimited.   Poorun = perfect.   Poor(i) Ruhiyo = pervading everywhere.   Biyaap(i) = affecting.  Anik = countless.   Praachhut = sins.   Raata = imbued.   Jaata = came to know (got connected with).   8.19. 

MEANING: Recite the Naam of the only “One” (Waheguru), and sing only His praise.   Remember only the “One”, and wish for Him alone in your mind, oh my friend.  Sing the virtues of only the infinite Waheguru, and recite His Naam with body and mind. Waheguru alone is supreme, and the perfect and omnipresent God is impacting (controlling) the entire universe.  There are countless expansions from the “One”, and all sins are dispelled by worshipping Him.  My mind and body are imbued in the love of the “One”, whom I have recognized (got connected) with the grace of the Guru, oh Nanak.  8.19.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਫਿਰਤ ਫਿਰਤ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਆਇਆ ਪਰਿਆ ਤਉ ਸਰਨਾਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਕੀ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਬੇਨਤੀ ਅਪਨੀ ਭਗਤੀ ਲਾਇ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Phirt, phirt Prubh aaya, puriyaa Tuou shernaayay. Nanak kee Prubh baynti,  Apni bhugti laayay.  1.

Word Meaning: Phirt, Phirt = having tried many shelters.    1

MEANING:  After having tried many, many so-called holy places, I have finally come to Your shelter, oh Waheguru.  I, Nanak, pray to You to kindly implant Your worship in my mind.  1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਜਾਚਕ ਜਨੁ ਜਾਚੈ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਦਾਨੁ॥ ਕਰਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਦੇਵਹੁ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ॥ ਸਾਧ ਜਨਾ ਕੀ ਮਾਗਉ ਧੂਰਿ॥ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਮੇਰੀ ਸਰਧਾ ਪੂਰਿ॥ ਸਦਾ ਸਦਾ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੇ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਵਉ॥ ਸਾਸਿ ਸਾਸਿ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਤੁਮਹਿ ਧਿਆਵਉ॥ ਚਰਨ ਕਮਲ ਸਿਉ ਲਾਗੈ ਪ੍ਰੀਤਿ॥ ਭਗਤਿ ਕਰਉ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੀ ਨਿਤ ਨੀਤਿ॥ ਏਕ ਓਟ ਏਕੋ ਆਧਾਰੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕੁ ਮਾਗੈ ਨਾਮੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਸਾਰੁ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Jaachuk jun(u) jaachaiy Prubh daan(u).  Kur(i) kirpa dayvhou Hur(i) Naam(u).  Sadh junaa kee maangoon dhoor(i).  Parbraham mayri sherdha poor(i).  Suda suda Prubh kay goon gaavoon.  Saas(i), saas(i) Prubh Toomeh(i) dhiyaavhoon. Churn kumul siyoun laagaiy preet(i). Bhugut kuroon Prubh kee nit neet(i).  Aik oat, Aiko aadhaar(u). Nanak maangaiy Naam(u) Prubh saar(u).   1.

Word Meaning: Jaachuk = beggar; mendicant.  Jaachaiy = begs for; wishes. Daan(u) = donation.   Dhoor(i) = dust of the feet (serve them).   Sherdha = devotion; wish.   Churn Kumul = lotus-like feet of (Waheguru).     Oat = shelter.  Aadhaar(u) = support.     Saar(u) = supreme. 

MEANING:  This beggar wishes for the donation of Your Naam, oh, Waheguru. Kindly bestow Your Naam on me.  I beg for the dust of the feet of the holy saints (an opportunity to serve them).  Kindly fulfill my wish, oh Waheguru.  Bless me, that I may sing Your praise all the time, and remember You with every breath. I should love Your lotus-like Feet (obey Your Commands) and always worship You. I need only Your shelter and Your support.  I, Nanak, beg for Your Naam, which is the supreme of all wishes, oh Waheguru.    1.

ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੀ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਮਹਾ ਸੁਖੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਹਰਿ ਰਸੁ ਪਾਵੈ ਬਿਰਲਾ ਕੋਇ॥ ਜਿਨ ਚਾਖਿਆ ਸੇ ਜਨ ਤ੍ਰਿਪਤਾਨੇ॥ ਪੂਰਨ ਪੁਰਖ ਨਹੀ ਡੋਲਾਨੇ॥ ਸੁਭਰ ਭਰੇ ਪ੍ਰੇਮ ਰਸ ਰੰਗਿ॥ ਉਪਜੈ ਚਾਉ ਸਾਧ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ॥ ਪਰੇ ਸਰਨਿ ਆਨ ਸਭ ਤਿਆਗਿ॥ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਪਰਗਾਸ ਅਨਦਿਨੁ ਲਿਵ ਲਾਗਿ॥ ਬਡਭਾਗੀ ਜਪਿਆ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਸੋਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਨਾਮਿ ਰਤੇ ਸੁਖੁ ਹੋਇ॥ 2॥

Prubh kee drisht(i) muha sookh(u) hoyay. Hur(i) rus(u) paavaiy birla koyay. Jin chaakhiya say jun triptaanay. Poorun Purakh nuhee dolaanay.  Soobhur bhuray praym rus rung(i). Oojaiy chaaou Sadh kaiy sung(i).  Puray shern aan subh tiyaag(i). Untar(i) pergaas undin(u) liv laag(i).  Budbhaageen jupiya Prubh soyay. Nanak Naam(i) ruttay sookh(u) hoyay.  2.

Word Meaning:   Drisht(i) = kind sight.      Birla = rare.   Chaakhiya = tasted.   Triptaanay = contented.   Dolaanay = weakening of the mind.    Soobhur = overflowing.   Oopjaiy = is produced.     Chaaou = zest.     Aan Subh = everything else.   Tiyaag(i) = giving up; abandoning.    Pergaas = enlightenment.    Liv = concentration; connected with Waheguru.  Budbhaagee = the fortunate.   Ruttay = imbued.   2.

MEANING:  There is a great comfort (blissfulness) in the kind sight of Waheguru, but some rare lucky individual enjoys the sweet taste of His Naam.  Whoever tastes Waheguru’s Naam gets fully contented. Such individuals become perfect saints, and their mind never weakens (get out of control).  Their minds become zestful for Waheguru’s Naam in the shelter of the Guru, and their minds overflow with the sweetness of Waheguru’s love.  They abandon all other interests and come to the Guru’s shelter. With their minds enlightened, they remain connected to Waheguru at all times.  Those who recite Waheguru’s Naam are fortunate.  Those who are imbued with Waheguru’s love always enjoy comfort, oh Nanak.   2.

ਸੇਵਕ ਕੀ ਮਨਸਾ ਪੂਰੀ ਭਈ॥ ਸਤਿਗੁਰ ਤੇ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਮਤਿ ਲਈ॥ ਜਨ ਕਉ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਹੋਇਓ ਦਇਆਲੁ॥ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਕੀਨੋ ਸਦਾ ਨਿਹਾਲੁ॥ ਬੰਧਨ ਕਾਟਿ ਮੁਕਤਿ ਜਨੁ ਭਇਆ॥ ਜਨਮ ਮਰਨ ਦੂਖੁ ਭ੍ਰਮੁ ਗਇਆ॥ ਇਛ ਪੁਨੀ ਸਰਧਾ ਸਭ ਪੂਰੀ॥ ਰਵਿ ਰਹਿਆ ਸਦ ਸੰਗਿ ਹਜੂਰੀ॥ ਜਿਸ ਕਾ ਸਾ ਤਿਨਿ ਲੀਆ ਮਿਲਾਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਭਗਤੀ ਨਾਮਿ ਸਮਾਇ॥ 3॥

Sayvuk kee munsa poori bhuyee.  Satgur tay nirmul mut(i) luyee.  Jun kuou Prubh(u) hoyo duyaal(u).  Sayvuk keeno suda nihaal(u). Bundhun kaat(i) mookut(i) jun(u) bhuyya. Junum mern dookh bherm guyya.  Ichh poonni, sherdha subh poori. Ruv(i) ruhiya sud sung(i) hujoori. Jis ka sa Tin(i) leeya milaayay. Nanak bhugtee Naam(i) smaayay.  3.

Word Meaning:  Sayvuk = attendant; devotee.  Munsa = wish.   Nirmul = pure.    Nihaal(u) = blissful.   Bundhun = bonds (of Maya).  Mookut(i) = liberated.    Bherm = misgivings.     Poonni = fulfilled.   Sherdha = devotion.   Hujoori = close by.   Tin(i) = that (Waheguru).   Smaayay = merges into.    3.

MEANING:  When a devotee takes the holy advice of the Guru, his wish is fulfilled. (When) Waheguru becomes kind to his devotee and fulfills the devotee's wish to receive Guru’s holy advice; the devotee becomes blissful (attains a high spiritual state).  His bonds with Maya are severed, and he becomes liberated.  His suffering of rebirths comes to an end, and all his misgivings disappear.  All his wishes are fulfilled, and his devotion is rewarded.  He finds Waheguru to be present close to him all the time. Waheguru, to whom the devotee belongs, absorbs him in Himself.  It is because of the worship of Waheguru that a devotee gets fully absorbed in Waheguru’s Naam, oh Nanak.       3.

ਸੋ ਕਿਉ ਬਿਸਰੈ ਜਿ ਘਾਲ ਨ ਭਾਨੈ॥ ਸੋ ਕਿਉ ਬਿਸਰੈ ਜਿ ਕੀਆ ਜਾਨੈ॥

(ਅੰਗ 290)॥   ਸੋ ਕਿਉ ਬਿਸਰੈ ਜਿਨਿ ਸਭੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਦੀਆ॥ ਸੋ ਕਿਉ ਬਿਸਰੈ ਜਿ ਜੀਵਨ ਜੀਆ॥ ਸੋ ਕਿਉ ਬਿਸਰੈ ਜਿ ਅਗਨਿ ਮਹਿ ਰਾਖੈ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਕੋ ਬਿਰਲਾ ਲਾਖੈ॥ ਸੋ ਕਿਉ ਬਿਸਰੈ ਜਿ ਬਿਖੁ ਤੇ ਕਾਢੈ॥ ਜਨਮ ਜਨਮ ਕਾ ਟੂਟਾ ਗਾਢੈ॥ ਗੁਰਿ ਪੂਰੈ ਤਤੁ ਇਹੈ ਬੁਝਾਇਆ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਅਪਨਾ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਨ ਧਿਆਇਆ॥   4॥

So kiyoun bisraiy, j(i) ghaal na bhaanaiy.  So kiyoun bisraiy, j(i) keeya jaanaiy. 

(PAGE 290):                   So kiyoun bisraiy Jin(i) subh(u) kichh(u) deeya. So kiyoun bisraiy J(i) jeevun jeayaan.  So kiyoun bisraiy, J(i) ugun(i) meh(i) raakhaiy. Gur persaad(i) ko birla laakhaiy. So kiyoun bisraiy J(i) bikh(u) tay kaadhaiy. Junum, junum ka toota gaandhaiy. Gur(i) Pooraiy Tut(u) ihaiy boojhaaya. Prubh apna Nanak jun dhiyaaya.  4.

Word Meaning: Bisraiy = forgotten.  J(i) = Jo = who.  Ghaal = hard labour.     Bhaanaiy = unrewards.    Keeya Jaanaiy = knows what we do.    Jeevun Jeayaan = basis of life of the living beings.   Ugun(i) = heat of the womb.    Laakhaiy = realizes.   Bikh(u) = poison (Maya).     Gaandhaiy = reconnects.   Tut(u) = essence; reality.    4.

MEANING:  How can we forget the Waheguru, which does not let our hard labour go unrewarded, and who knows full well the kind of deeds we perform? How can we forget the Waheguru who has given us everything that we need, and who is the source of, and the supporter of, the life of all living beings?  How can we forget the Waheguru who protects us in the heat of the womb? But some rare individual understands all this by the grace of the Guru.  How can we forget the Waheguru, who pulls us out of the attractions of Maya, and reconnects us, who had been separated from Him for many, many lives, with Himself?  The Perfect Guru explained all this reality to us; so, now, I am reciting the Naam of my Waheguru, oh Nanak.   4.

ਸਾਜਨ ਸੰਤ ਕਰਹੁ ਇਹੁ ਕਾਮੁ॥ ਆਨ ਤਿਆਗਿ ਜਪਹੁ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ॥ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸੁਖ ਪਾਵਹੁ॥ ਆਪਿ ਜਪਹੁ ਅਵਰਹ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਾਵਹੁ॥ ਭਗਤਿ ਭਾਇ ਤਰੀਐ ਸੰਸਾਰੁ॥ ਬਿਨੁ ਭਗਤੀ ਤਨੁ ਹੋਸੀ ਛਾਰੁ॥ ਸਰਬ ਕਲਿਆਣ ਸੂਖ ਨਿਧਿ ਨਾਮੁ॥ ਬੂਡਤ ਜਾਤ ਪਾਏ ਬਿਸ੍ਰਾਮੁ॥ ਸਗਲ ਦੂਖ ਕਾ ਹੋਵਤ ਨਾਸੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਹੁ ਗੁਨਤਾਸ॥ 5॥

Saajun sunt kurhou ehou kaam(u). Aan tiyaag(i) jup-hou Hur(i) Naam(u). Simer(i), simer(i), simer(i) sookh paavhou. Aap(i) jup-hou uvreh Naam(u) jupaavhou. Bhugut(i) bhaayay turiyaiy sunsaar(u).  Bin(u) bhugti tun(u) hoasi chhaar(u). Serb kulyaan sookh nidh(i) Naam(u). Boodut jaat paayay bisraam(u). Sugul dookh ka hovut naas(u). Nanak Naam(u) jup-hou Goontaas. 5.

Word Meaning: Saajun = friends.   Kaam(u) = undertaking.   Aan = others.   Bhaayay = love; dedication.   Chhaar(u) = dust.    Kulyaan = well-being.    Nidh(i) = treasure.   Boodut = drowning (in the world-sea).   Bisraam = rest; peace.     Goontaas = source of virtues (Waheguru).    5.

MEANING:  Giving up everything else, recite the Naam of Waheguru, and make this an undertaking for you, oh my saintly friends. Enjoy all the comforts of life by repeatedly reciting Waheguru’s Naam yourself, and by motivating others to do the same. The world-sea can be swum across by worshipping Waheguru with love and reverence; without Waheguru’s worship, our bodies will simply become dust, and stay as dust through reincarnations. Waheguru’s Naam is the treasure of all comforts and wellbeing; a man drowning in the world-sea (and taking rebirths) finally finds rest (peace in Waheguru’s shelter).  If you recite the Naam of Waheguru, which is the source of all virtues, all your suffering will be eradicated, oh Nanak.  5.

ਪ੍ਰੀਤਿ ਪ੍ਰੇਮ ਉਪਜੀ ਰਸੁ ਚਾਉ॥ ਮਨ ਤਨ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਇਹੀ ਸੁਆਉ॥ ਨੇਤ੍ਰਹੁ ਪੇਖਿ ਦਰਸੁ ਸੁਖੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਮਨੁ ਬਿਗਸੈ ਸਾਧ ਚਰਨ ਧੋਇ॥ ਭਗਤ ਜਨਾ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਤਨਿ ਰੰਗੁ॥ ਬਿਰਲਾ ਕੋਊ ਪਾਵੈ ਸੰਗੁ॥ ਏਕ ਬਸਤੁ ਦੀਜੈ ਕਰਿ ਮਇਆ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਿ ਲਇਆ॥ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਉਪਮਾ ਕਹੀ ਨ ਜਾਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਰਹਿਆ ਸਰਬ ਸਮਾਇ॥ 6॥

Oopji preet(i) praym rus(u) chaaou. Mun tun untar(i) ihee sooaaou. Nayturhou paykh(i) durs(u) sookh(u) hoyay.  Mun bigsaiy Sadh Churn dhoyay. Bhugut junaa kaiy mun(i) tun(i) rung(u). Birla koyoo paavaiy sung(u).  Aik bust(u) deejaiy kur(i) muyya.  Gur persaad(i) Naam(u) jup(i) luyya.  Ta kee oopma kuhee na jaayay. Nanak ruhiya serb smaayay.  6.

Word Meaning:   Oopji = sprouted.    Chaaou = zest; gusto.   Sooaaou = taste.   Paykh(i) = seeing.   Durs(u) = sight.    Bigsaiy = blooms; overjoyed.    Rung(u) = love.   Birla = rare.   Sung(u) = company.    Bust(u) = article.          Muyya = kindness.    Oopma = praise.   Smaayay = pervading.    6.

MEANING:  (By reciting Waheguru’s Naam), the sweetness of affection, love, and zest sprouts in the heart of the devotee, and he enjoys this taste in his body and mind.  His mind blooms by washing the feet of the Guru (serving Him with devotion), and he feels blissful on seeing the Guru with his eyes.  The devotees are full of love for the Guru by body and by mind, but a rare individual gets to enjoy the company of the Guru.  Kindly grant me one thing, oh Waheguru – enable me to recite Your Naam in the shelter of the Guru.  Waheguru’s praise (magnanimity) cannot be described; He is present everywhere, oh Nanak.  6.

ਪ੍ਰਭ ਬਖਸੰਦ ਦੀਨ ਦਇਆਲ॥ ਭਗਤਿ ਵਛਲ ਸਦਾ ਕਿਰਪਾਲ॥ ਅਨਾਥ ਨਾਥ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ ਗੁਪਾਲ॥ ਸਰਬ ਘਟਾ ਕਰਤ ਪ੍ਰਤਿਪਾਲ॥ ਆਦਿ ਪੁਰਖ ਕਾਰਣ ਕਰਤਾਰ॥ ਭਗਤ ਜਨਾ ਕੇ ਪ੍ਰਾਨ ਅਧਾਰ॥ ਜੋ ਜੋ ਜਪੈ ਸੁ ਹੋਇ ਪੁਨੀਤ॥ ਭਗਤਿ ਭਾਇ ਲਾਵੈ ਮਨ ਹੀਤ॥ ਹਮ ਨਿਰਗੁਨੀਆਰ ਨੀਚ ਅਜਾਨ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤੁਮਰੀ ਸਰਨਿ ਪੁਰਖ ਭਗਵਾਨ॥ 7॥

Prubh bukhshund deen duyaal. Bhugut(i) vuchhul suda Kirpaal.  Anaath, Naath, Gobind, Goopaal.  Serb ghutaan kert pritpaal. Aad(i) Purakh kaarun Kartar. Bhugut junaa kay praan adhaar. Jo, jo jupaiy su hoyay pooneet. Bhugut(i) bhaayay laavaiy mun heet. Hum nirgooniyaar, neech, ajaan. Nanak,Toomri  shern(i) Purakh Bhugvaan.  7.

Word Meaning: Bukhshund = forgiving.    Deen = weak; helpless.    Vuchhul = loving.    Anaath = orphan; support-less.    Naath = guardian.   Gobind = Goopaal = one who supports earth (Waheguru).    Ghutaan = hearts (bodies).   Pritpaal = nourish and nurture.   Aad(i) Purakh = the origin of all.    Kaarun = the creation.  Kartar = creator.   Pooneet = holy; pious.   Heet = affection.  Nirgoonyaar = virtue-less.    Neech = ignoble.   Ajaan = ignorant.    7.

MEANING: Waheguru is forgiving and kind to the weak (humble); He loves His worship, and is always benevolent to His devotees.  He is the guardian of the support-less, and nurtures and nourishes the earth (living beings on it), and every heart.  He is the origin of everything, the creator of the universe, and the basis of the life of His devotees.  Whosoever recites His Naam with love and devotion becomes pure (pious).  We are virtue-less, ignoble, and ignorant, oh Nanak. We have come to seek Your shelter (please, protect us), oh Waheguru.7. 

ਸਰਬ ਬੈਕੁੰਠ ਮੁਕਤਿ ਮੋਖ ਪਾਏ॥ ਏਕ ਨਿਮਖ ਹਰਿ ਕੇ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਇ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਰਾਜ ਭੋਗ ਬਡਿਆਈ॥ ਹਰਿ ਕੇ ਨਾਮ ਕੀ ਕਥਾ ਮਨਿ ਭਾਈ॥ ਬਹੁ ਭੋਜਨ ਕਾਪਰ ਸੰਗੀਤ॥ ਰਸਨਾ ਜਪਤੀ ਹਰਿ ਹਰਿ ਨੀਤ॥ ਭਲੀ ਸੁ ਕਰਨੀ ਸੋਭਾ ਧਨਵੰਤ॥ ਹਿਰਦੈ ਬਸੇ ਪੂਰਨ ਗੁਰ ਮੰਤ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਦੇਹੁ ਨਿਵਾਸ॥ ਸਰਬ ਸੂਖ ਨਾਨਕ ਪਰਗਾਸ॥ 8॥20॥

Serb baiykoontth mookut(i) moakh paayay.  Aik nimukh Hur(i) kay goon gaayay. Anik raj, bhoag, budiyaayee. Hur(i) kay Naam kee kutha mun(i) bhaayee. Buhou bhojun kaapur sungeet. Rusna juptee Hur(i) Hur(i) neet. Bhulee su kerni, sobha, dhunvunt. Hirdaiy busay Poorun Gur Munt. Sadh sung(i) Prubh dayhou nivaas. Serb sookh Nanak pergaas.  8.20.

Word Meaning: Baiykoontth = paradise.    Mookut(i) = Moakh = freedom from Maya.   Nimukh = a moment.     Raj Bhoag = luxuries of ruling.   Budiyaayee = praise; honour.      Kutha = story; description of virtues.   Kaapur = clothes.   Rusna = tongue.    Bhulee Kerni = noble deeds.    Sobha = praise.   Gur Munt = Guru’s Word.    Pergaas = enlightenment.    8.20

MEANING:   If an individual recites Waheguru’s virtues (with full faith and dedication) even for a moment, he feels as if he has been liberated from the bonds of Maya and has acquired the luxuries of all the paradises.  If a devotee likes the story of Waheguru (description of His virtues and kindness) in his mind, he feels as if he has enjoyed all the luxuries of a monarchy and has received a huge honour.  An individual, whose tongue recites Waheguru’s Naam all the time, feels as if he has been served many different kinds of food and clothes, and is enjoying some melodious (Divine) music.  An individual who has the Perfect Guru’s Word enshrined in his heart feels as if he has performed all the noble deeds, is enjoying fame, and is very wealthy. Kindly give me the company of the Guru, oh Waheguru, because there is all bliss when one’s heart is enlightened, oh Nanak.    8.20.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਸਰਗੁਨ ਨਿਰਗੁਨ ਨਿਰੰਕਾਰ ਸੁੰਨ ਸਮਾਧੀ ਆਪਿ॥ ਆਪਨ ਕੀਆ ਨਾਨਕਾ ਆਪੇ ਹੀ ਫਿਰਿ ਜਾਪਿ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Surgoon, nirgoon Nirunkaar, soonn smaadhi Aap(i).  Aapun keeya Nanaka, Aapeh(i) hee phir(i) jaap(i). 

Word Meaning:  Surgoon = Waheguru (as present in nature).     Nirgoon = Waheguru (formless and above the effects of Maya).   Soonn Smaadhi = all by Himself in meditation.   Jaap(i) = sitting inside the human beings; He enjoys His praise sung by them. 

MEANING:  The formless Waheguru (above the three effects of Maya) is invisible and visible in nature at the same time.  He is also in the state of meditation in which He is all by himself and thinks of nothing else.   He watches and enjoys His Creation, and His praise being sung by it.    1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਜਬ ਅਕਾਰੁ ਇਹੁ ਕਿਛੁ ਨ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟੇਤਾ॥ ਪਾਪ ਪੁੰਨ ਤਬ ਕਹ ਤੇ ਹੋਤਾ॥ ਜਬ ਧਾਰੀ ਆਪਨ ਸੁੰਨ ਸਮਾਧਿ॥ ਤਬ ਬੈਰ ਬਿਰੋਧ ਕਿਸੁ ਸੰਗਿ ਕਮਾਤਿ॥ ਜਬ ਇਸ ਕਾ ਬਰਨੁ ਚਿਹਨੁ ਨ ਜਾਪਤ॥ ਤਬ ਹਰਖ ਸੋਗ ਕਹੁ ਕਿਸਹਿ ਬਿਆਪਤ॥ ਜਬ ਆਪਨ ਆਪ ਆਪਿ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ॥ ਤਬ ਮੋਹ ਕਹਾ ਕਿਸੁ ਹੋਵਤ ਭਰਮ॥

(ਅੰਗ 291)॥   ਆਪਨ ਖੇਲੁ ਆਪਿ ਵਰਤੀਜਾ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਕਰਨੈਹਾਰ ਨ ਦੂਜਾ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Jub akaar(u) ehou kichh(u) na drishtayta. Paap, poonn tub keh tay hoata? Jub dhaari Aapun soonn smaadh(i).  Tub baiyr biroadh kis(u) sung(i) kmaat(i). Jub iss ka bern(u) chihn(u) na jaaput.  Tub hurkh soag kuhou kiseh(i) biyaaput.  Jub aapun aap aap(i) Parbraham.  Tub moh kuhaa, kis(u) hovut bherm? 

(PAGE 291):  Aapun khayl(u) Aap(i) verteeja. Nanak kernaiyhaar na dooja.   1.

Word Meaning:   Akaar(u) = visible universe.   Drishtayta = visible.   Poonn = accepted religious deeds.      Biroadh(i) = hostility.  Bern(u) = colour.   Chihn(u) = physical features.   Hurkh = happiness.      Soag = sadness.    Bherm = misgivings.   Khayle = drama (of His creation).   Verteeja = is operating.   Kernaiyhaar = creator.    1.

MEANING:  When this universe was not visible (was not there), how could noble deeds be performed or sins committed?   When Waheguru was in His deep meditation without any external thinking, how could animosity and hostility have been carried out? When there were no visible signs of the universe, who could have experienced happiness or sadness?  When Waheguru was all by Himself, who could have any attachment or misgivings? Waheguru Himself created the drama of the universe, and He alone is operating in it.  There is none other like Him, oh Nanak.   1.

ਜਬ ਹੋਵਤ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੇਵਲ ਧਨੀ॥ ਤਬ ਬੰਧ ਮੁਕਤਿ ਕਹੁ ਕਿਸ ਕਉ ਗਨੀ॥ ਜਬ ਏਕਹਿ ਹਰਿ ਅਗਮ ਅਪਾਰ॥ ਤਬ ਨਰਕ ਸੁਰਗ ਕਹੁ ਕਉਨ ਅਉਤਾਰ॥ ਜਬ ਨਿਰਗੁਨ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਸਹਜ ਸੁਭਾਇ॥ ਤਬ ਸਿਵ ਸਕਤਿ ਕਹਹੁ ਕਿਤੁ ਠਾਇ॥ ਜਬ ਆਪਹਿ ਆਪਿ ਅਪਨੀ ਜੋਤਿ ਧਰੈ॥ ਤਬ ਕਵਨ ਨਿਡਰੁ ਕਵਨ ਕਤ ਡਰੈ॥ ਆਪਨ ਚਲਿਤ ਆਪਿ ਕਰਨੈਹਾਰ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਠਾਕੁਰ ਅਗਮ ਅਪਾਰ॥ 2॥

Jub hovut Prubh kayvul dhunee. Tub bundh mookut(i) kuhou kis kuou gunee. Jub aikeh(i) Hur(i) Agumm Apaar. Tub nerk soorg kuhou kaun autaar? Jub nirgoon Prubh sehj soobhaayay.  Tub Shiv shukut(i) kuhou kit(u) tthaayay? Jub Aapeh(i) Aap(i) Apni Jot(i) dhuraiy.  Tub kuvun nidur(u) kuvun kut duraiy? Aapun chulit Aap(i) kernaihaar. Nanak Tthaakur agumm apaar.   2.

Word Meaning:   Dhunee = Lord.   Bundh = bonds (of Maya).     Mookut(i) = free from bonds.    Gunee = considered.    Agumm = unreachable.    Apaar = limitless.    Autaar = come into being.    Sehj Soobhaayay = in meditation; blissful state.    Shiv Shukut(i) = Maya of Waheguru.    Tthaayay = place.     Jot(i) = Light.   Nidur(u) = fearless.    Chulit = amazing feats. 

MEANING:  When the Lord, Waheguru, was all alone (all by Himself), who could be considered a prisoner of Maya or considered free from Maya? When there was only the unreachable and the limitless Waheguru, then how could hell and heaven have come into being? When the formless Waheguru was in His blissful state, where was Maya at that time? (There was no Maya.   When Waheguru’s Light was within Him, who could have claimed to be fearless or afraid of something?  Waheguru is the creator of His own amazing feats.  He is inaccessible and infinite, oh Nanak.   2.

ਅਬਿਨਾਸੀ ਸੁਖ ਆਪਨ ਆਸਨ॥ ਤਹ ਜਨਮ ਮਰਨ ਕਹੁ ਕਹਾ ਬਿਨਾਸਨ॥ ਜਬ ਪੂਰਨ ਕਰਤਾ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਸੋਇ॥ ਤਬ ਜਮ ਕੀ ਤ੍ਰਾਸ ਕਹਹੁ ਕਿਸੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਜਬ ਅਬਿਗਤ ਅਗੋਚਰ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਏਕਾ॥ ਤਬ ਚਿਤ੍ਰ ਗੁਪਤ ਕਿਸੁ ਪੂਛਤ ਲੇਖਾ॥ ਜਬ ਨਾਥ ਨਿਰੰਜਨ ਅਗੋਚਰ ਅਗਾਧੇ॥ ਤਬ ਕਉਨ ਛੁਟੇ ਕਉਨ ਬੰਧਨ ਬਾਧੇ॥ ਆਪਨ ਆਪ ਆਪ ਹੀ ਅਚਰਜਾ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਆਪਨ ਰੂਪ ਆਪ ਹੀ ਉਪਰਜਾ॥ 3॥

Abinaashi, sookh Aapun aasun. Tub junum, mern kuhou kuhaa binaasun?  Jub Poorun Kerta Prubh Soyay.  Tub Jum kee traas kuhou kis(u) hoyay? Jub ubigut agoachur Prubh aika. Tub Chitur Goopt kis(u) poochhut laykha. Jub Naath Nirunjun agoachur agaadhay. Tub kaun chootay, kaun bundhun baandhay? Aapun Aap, Aap hee achurja.  Nanak Aapun roop Aap hee oopurja.  3.

Word Meaning: Abinaashi = immortal.   Aasun = resting place.    Binaasun = destruction.   Poorun = Perfect.    Traas = fear; threat.   Abigut = invisible.   Agochur = incomprehensible.   Chitur, Goopt = two mythical assistants of the angel of Justice.  Laykha = account of deeds.   Naath = Lord.   Nirunjun = Waheguru (above the effects of Maya).     Agaadhay = unfathomable.   Achurja = amazing.   Oopurja = created.   3.

MEANING:  When the eternal Waheguru was all by Himself in a blissful state, how could there be any birth, death or destruction of any kind? When the Perfect Creator, Waheguru, was all by Himself, who could have been afraid of the Angel of death?  When the invisible and the incomprehensible Waheguru was the only power in the universe, whom could Chitur and Goopt have asked for the accounts of their deeds?  When the incomprehensible and unfathomable Lord, which is above Maya, was all by Himself, who could have been free from the bonds of Maya, and who would have been bonded with that?  This is amazing: Waheguru is self-illuminating and creates Himself, oh Nanak.  3.

ਜਹ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਪੁਰਖੁ ਪੁਰਖੁ ਪਤਿ ਹੋਤਾ॥ ਤਹ ਬਿਨੁ ਮੈਲੁ ਕਹਹੁ ਕਿਆ ਧੋਤਾ॥ ਜਹ ਨਿਰੰਜਨ ਨਿਰੰਕਾਰ ਨਿਰਬਾਨ॥ ਤਹ ਕਉਨ ਕਉ ਮਾਨ ਕਉਨ ਅਭਿਮਾਨ॥ ਜਹ ਸਰੂਪ ਕੇਵਲ ਜਗਦੀਸ॥ ਤਹ ਛਲ ਛਿਦ੍ਰ ਲਗਤ ਕਹੁ ਕੀਸ॥ ਜਹ ਜੋਤਿ ਸਰੂਪੀ ਜੋਤਿ ਸੰਗਿ ਸਮਾਵੈ॥ ਤਹ ਕਿਸਹਿ ਭੂਖ ਕਵਨੁ ਤ੍ਰਿਪਤਾਵੈ॥ ਕਰਨ ਕਰਾਵਨ ਕਰਨੈਹਾਰੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਕਰਤੇ ਕਾ ਨਾਹਿ ਸੁਮਾਰੁ॥ 4॥

Jeh Nirmul Purakh, Purakh Put(i) hoata.  Teh bin(u) maiyl(u) kuhou kiya dhoata?  Jeh Nirunjun, Nirunkaar, Nirbaan. Teh kaun kuou maan, kaun abimaan?  Jeh suroop kayvul Jugdeesh.  Teh chhul chhidur lugut kuhou kees?  Jeh Jot(i) Sroopi Jot(i) sung(i) smaavaiy. Teh kiseh(i) bhookh, kuvun(u) triptaavaiy.  Kern Kraavun Kernaihaar(u).  Nanak Kertay ka naahi shoomaar(u).   4.

Word Meaning:  Nirmul Purakh = Pure Person (Waheguru).     Put(i) = Master; Lord. 

Nirunkaar = formless Waheguru.   Nirbaan = fully detached.     Maan = respect.    Abhimaan = disrespect.    Sroop = pretty personality.   Chhul = deceiving.    Chhidur = blame.   Kees = whom.     Triptaavaiy = satisfied; contented.     Shoomaar = limit.   4.

MEANING:  When the Lord of humans, and the pure Waheguru, was all by Himself (and there was no Maya and no filth of vices), who would have had the need to wash away the filth of vices (because there wasn’t any)?  When the formless and completely detached Waheguru, which is also above the effects of Maya, was all by Himself, who would have experienced respect or disrespect? When the Lord of the world, Waheguru, was the only pretty (pure) personality in the universe, who would have suffered from any deceit or blame?  When the universe was still absorbed in the Light of Waheguru, who could have been hungry or satisfied?   Waheguru is the creator of everything, and gets others to do whatever He wants; there is no limit to His powers, oh Nanak.    4. 

ਜਬ ਅਪਨੀ ਸੋਭਾ ਆਪਨ ਸੰਗਿ ਬਨਾਈ॥ ਤਬ ਕਵਨ ਮਾਇ ਬਾਪ ਮਿਤ੍ਰ ਸੁਤ ਭਾਈ॥ ਜਹ ਸਰਬ ਕਲਾ ਆਪਹਿ ਪਰਬੀਨ॥ ਤਹ ਬੇਦ ਕਤੇਬ ਕਹਾ ਕੋਊ ਚੀਨ॥ ਜਬ ਆਪਨ ਆਪੁ ਆਪਿ ਉਰਿਧਾਰੈ॥ ਤਉ ਸਗਨ ਅਪਸਗਨ ਕਹਾ ਬੀਚਾਰੈ॥ ਜਹ ਆਪਨ ਊਚ ਆਪਨ ਆਪਿ ਨੇਰਾ॥ ਤਹ ਕਉਨ ਠਾਕੁਰ ਕਉਨ ਕਹੀਐ ਚੇਰਾ॥ ਬਿਸਮਨ ਬਿਸਮ ਰਹੇ ਬਿਸਮਾਦ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਅਪਨੀ ਗਤਿ ਜਾਨਹੁ ਆਪਿ॥ 5॥

Jub Apuni sobha Aapun sung(i) bunaayee.  Tub nuvun maayay, baap, mittar, soot, bhaayee? Jeh serb kulaa Aapeh(i) perbeen. Teh Bayd, Ktayb kuhaa koyoo cheen? Jub Aapun Aap(u) Aap(i) oordhaaraiy. Tuou shugun, upshugun kuhaa beechaaraiy? Jeh Aapun ooch, Aapun Aap(i) nayra.  Teh kaun Tthaakur, kaun kuhiyaiy chayra? Bismun, bism ruhay bismaad. Nanak, Apni gut(i) jaanhou Aap(i).   5.

Word Meaning:  Sobha = praise; magnificence.    Soot = sons.    Perbeen = expert; Lord.    Cheen = recognize; acquire knowledge of.      Oordhaaraiy = has in mind; supporting.   Shugun Upshugun = auspicious and inauspicious occasions.    Ooch = high; inaccessible.   Nayra = close by.   Tthaakur = Lord.    Chayra = attendant.   Bismaad = amazement; mesmerization.     Gut(i) = higher spiritual state.   

MEANING:  When the magnificence of Waheguru was contained within Him (and there was no universe yet), who could have been considered as the mother, father, friend, son or brother?  When the omnipotent Waheguru was the only Lord, who would have acquired knowledge of the Vedas and Western religious texts?  When Waheguru was supporting only Himself, who would have thought about the auspicious and the inauspicious occasions?  When Waheguru was inaccessible and close only to Himself, who would have been the Lord and the attendant?   Only Waheguru knows His spiritual heights, the man is simply amazed, amazed, and amazed, and recedes into amazement, oh Nanak.  5.

ਜਹ ਅਛਲ ਅਛੇਦ ਅਭੇਦ ਸਮਾਇਆ॥ ਊਹਾ ਕਿਸਹਿ ਬਿਆਪਤ ਮਾਇਆ॥ ਆਪਸ ਕਉ ਆਪਹਿ ਆਦੇਸੁ॥ ਤਿਹੁ ਗੁਣ ਕਾ ਨਾਹੀ ਪਰਵੇਸੁ॥ ਜਹ ਏਕਹਿ ਏਕ ਏਕ ਭਗਵੰਤਾ॥ ਤਹ ਕਉਨੁ ਅਚਿੰਤੁ ਕਿਸੁ ਲਾਗੈ ਚਿੰਤਾ॥ ਜਹ ਆਪਨ ਆਪੁ ਆਪਿ ਪਤੀਆਰਾ॥ ਤਹ ਕਉਨ ਕਥੈ ਕਉਨ ਸੁਨਨੈਹਾਰਾ॥ ਬਹੁ ਬੇਅੰਤ ਊਚ ਤੇ ਊਚਾ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਆਪਸ ਕਉ ਆਪਹਿ ਪਹੂਚਾ॥ 6॥

Jeh Achhull achhayd abhayd smaaya. Oohaa kiseh(i) biyaaput Maya. Aapus kuou Aapeh(i) aadays(u).  Tihou goon ka naahi pervaysh(u).  Jeh aikeh(i) aik aik Bhugvunta. Teh kaun achint(u), kis(u) laagaiy chinta? Jeh Aapun Aap(u) Aap(i) putiyaara.  Teh kaun kuthaiy, kaun soon-naiy haara. Buhou bayunt, ooch tay oocha. Nanak, Aapus kuou Aapeh(i) puhoocha.  6.

Word Meaning: Achhull = which cannot be deceived.   Achhayd = which cannot be subdivided; impenetrable.    Abhayd = whose secrets cannot be known.     Smaaya = present.    Aadays(u) = bow to; obey command.   Tihou goon = the three effects of Maya.    Pervaysh(u) = penetration; impact.   Achint(u) = worry-free.   Putiyaara = entertainer; pleasing.   Bayunt = infinite.   6.

MEANING:  Where the non-deceivable and impenetrable Waheguru, whose secrets cannot be known, is present, who can be affected by Maya there? Where Waheguru obeyed His own Laws (because there was none other there yet), how could Maya have penetrated? When there was only Waheguru, who existed, who was worry-free, and who was worried?  Where Waheguru was pleasing Himself only, who could have been there to describe, and to listen to something?  Waheguru is infinite and higher than the highest.  Only He has access to Himself, oh Nanak.   6.

ਜਹ ਆਪਿ ਰਚਿਓ ਪਰਪੰਚੁ ਅਕਾਰੁ॥ ਤਿਹੁ ਗੁਣ ਮਹਿ ਕੀਨੋ ਬਿਸਥਾਰੁ॥ ਪਾਪੁ ਪੁੰਨ ਤਹ ਭਈ ਕਹਾਵਤ॥

(ਅੰਗ 292)॥    ਕੋਊ ਨਰਕ ਕੋਊ ਸੁਰਗ ਬੰਛਾਵਤ॥ ਆਲ ਜਾਲ ਮਾਇਆ ਜੰਜਾਲੁ॥ ਹਉਮੈ ਮੋਹ ਭਰਮ ਭੈ ਭਾਰ॥ ਦੂਖ ਸੂਖ ਮਾਨ ਅਪਮਾਨ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਪ੍ਰਕਾਰ ਕੀਓ ਬਖ੍ਹਾਨ॥ ਆਪਨ ਖੇਲੁ ਆਪਿ ਕਰਿ ਦੇਖੈ॥ ਖੇਲੁ ਸੰਕੋਚੈ ਤਉ ਨਾਨਕ ਏਕੈ॥ 7॥

Jeh Aap(i) ruchiyo perpunch akaar(u).  Tihou goon meh(i) keeno bisthaar(u). Paap(u), poonn teh bhuyee kuhaavut. 

(PAGE 292):  Koyoo nerk, koyoo soorg bunchhavut.  Aal, jaal, Maya junjaal(u).  Haumein, moh, bherm, bhaiy, bhaar.  Dookh, sookh, maan, upmaan.  Anik perkaar keeyo bukhiyaan.  Aapun khayl(u), Aap(i) kur(i) daykhaiy.  Khayl(u) sunkochaiy, tuou Nanak aikaiy.   7.

Word Meaning:   Ruchiyo = created.   Perpunch Akaar(u) = the visible universe.    Bisthaar = expanse.   Kuhaavut = accepted terms.    Bunchhaavut = wish for; lead to.   Aal, Jaal = the burden of family responsibilities.    Junjaal(u) = bonds; nets.   Upmaan = insult; disrespect.    Bukhiyaan = explained (behaviour and its results).    Khayl(u) = drama of the world.     Sunkochaiy = wraps up.  7.

MEANING:   When Waheguru created the visible universe, He subjected it to the influence of the three effects of Maya.  (Because of the effects of Maya), Sins and noble deeds became the accepted terms, which led people to believe in hell and heaven.  In addition, the burden of family responsibilities, the nets (traps) of Maya, selfishness, ego, attachment to worldly things, the burden of misgivings, and fear arose.  Man’s behaviour, and its results in terms of suffering, comforts, respect, and disrespect, were also expressed.  The universe is a drama created by Waheguru, which He looks after; when He wraps up His drama, He is again left all by Himself, oh Nanak.     7.

ਜਹ ਅਬਿਗਤੁ ਭਗਤੁ ਤਹ ਆਪਿ॥ ਜਹ ਪਸਰੈ ਪਾਸਾਰੁ ਸੰਤ ਪਰਤਾਪਿ॥ ਦੁਹੂ ਪਾਖ ਕਾ ਆਪਹਿ ਧਨੀ॥ ਉਨ ਕੀ ਸੋਭਾ ਉਨਹੂ ਬਨੀ॥ ਆਪਹਿ ਕਉਤਕ ਕਰੈ ਅਨਦ ਚੋਜ॥ ਆਪਹਿ ਰਸ ਭੋਗਨ ਨਿਰਜੋਗ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਆਪਨ ਨਾਇ ਲਾਵੈ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਖੇਲ ਖਿਲਾਵੈ॥ ਬੇਸੁਮਾਰ ਅਥਾਹ ਅਗਨਤ ਅਤੋਲੈ॥ ਜਿਉ ਬੁਲਾਵਹੁ ਤਿਉ ਨਾਨਕ ਦਾਸ ਬੋਲੈ॥ 8॥21॥

Jeh Ubigut bhugut(u), teh Aap(i).  jeh pusraiy pasaar(u) Sunt pertaap(i).  Dohoon paakh ka Aapeh(i) dhuni.  Oon kee sobha oonhoon bunee.  Aapeh(i) koutuk kuraiy anund choaj.  Aapeh(i) rus bhogun Nirjoag.   Jis(u) bhaavaiy, tis(u) Aapun Naayay laavaiy. Jis(u) bhaavaiy, tis(u) khayl khilaavaiy.  Bayshoomaar, athaah, agunut, atoalaiy. Jiyoun boolaavhou, tiyoun Nanak daas boalaiy. 8.21.

Word Meaning: Ubigut(u) = invisible.   Aap(i) = Waheguru.    Pusraiy = spreads.   Pasaar(u) = expanse.   Sunt Pertaap(i) = for the magnificence of the holy saints.   Paakh = sides (this world and the next).    Dhunee = Lord.   Kautuk = Choaj = uncanny feats.   Anund = blissful.      Nirjoag = detached.   Khayl Khilaavaiy = keeps engaged in Maya.   Bayshoomaar = infinite.    Athaah = unfathomable.   Ugunt Atoalaiy = beyond measurements.   8.21.

MEANING:  The invisible Waheguru is present where His devotees are; Waheguru created this huge expanse (of the universe) for the magnificence of the holy saints.  He is the Lord of both the worlds – the present and the next. Praise (splendour) of the holy saints befits them.   Waheguru Himself performs His uncanny feats in His own blissful state.  Even though He is fully detached from the world, He enjoys the tastes of life by being present in His creation.  He motivates the individuals of His liking to recite His Naam, while he keeps others engaged in Maya.  Waheguru is infinite, unfathomable, and immeasurable.  I am His slave, and can sing only that much of His praise as He enables me to do, oh Nanak.  8.21.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਜੀਅ ਜੰਤ ਕੇ ਠਾਕੁਰਾ ਆਪੇ ਵਰਤਣਹਾਰ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਏਕੋ ਪਸਰਿਆ ਦੂਜਾ ਕਹ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਾਰ॥1॥

Sloak(u): Jea junt kay Tthaakura, Aapay vertunhaar.  Nanak, Aiko pusriya, dooja keh drishtaar. 

Word Meaning:  Jea Junt = living beings.    Vertunhaar = operator.    Pusriya = spread out; expanse.    Drishtaar = visible.     1.

MEANING: Waheguru is the Lord of all living beings, and He operates everywhere.  The whole universe is His expanse (creation); there is none other (like Him) to be seen, oh Nanak.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਆਪਿ ਕਥੈ ਆਪਿ ਸੁਨਨੈਹਾਰੁ॥ ਆਪਹਿ ਏਕੁ ਆਪਿ ਬਿਸਥਾਰੁ॥ ਜਾ ਤਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਸ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਉਪਾਏ॥ ਆਪਨੇ ਭਾਣੈ ਲਏ ਸਮਾਏ॥ ਤੁਮ ਤੇ ਭਿੰਨ ਨਹੀ ਕਿਛੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਆਪਨ ਸੂਤਿ ਸਭੁ ਜਗਤੁ ਪਰੋਇ॥ ਜਾ ਕਉ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਜੀਉ ਆਪਿ ਬੁਝਾਏ॥ ਸਚੁ ਨਾਮੁ ਸੋਈ ਜਨੁ ਪਾਏ॥ ਸੋ ਸਮਦਰਸੀ ਤਤ ਕਾ ਬੇਤਾ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਸਗਲ ਸ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ ਕਾ ਜੇਤਾ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Aap(i) kuthaiy, Aap(i) soon-naiy-haar(u).  Aapeh(i) Aik(u), Aap(i) bisthhar(u).  Ja Tis(u) bhaavaiy ta srisht(i) oopaayay. Aapnay bhaanaiy luyay smaayay.  Toom tay bhinn nuhee kichh(u) hoyay.  Aapun soot(i) subh(u) jugut(u) proyay. Ja kuou Prubh jeeo aap(i) boojhaayay. Sach(u) Naam(u) soyee jun(u) paayay. So sumdersi Tut ka bayta.  Nanak sugul srisht ka jayta. 1. 

Word Meaning:  Kuthaiy = describes; narrates.  Bisthaar = expanse.     Smaayay = absorbs.   Bhinn = different; without.     Soot(i) = Huqam; laws.   Proayay = stitch; string.    Boojhaayay = gives the understanding.      Sumdersi = non-discriminating.    Tut = reality; origin; Waheguru.   Bayta = knowledgeable.   Jayta = victorious. 

MEANING: (Being present in all living beings) Waheguru Himself is the narrator (of His virtues), and Himself the listener (audience).   He is present in the whole world, which is His expanse (creation), and at the same time, He is all by Himself (fully detached from the world).  He creates the world whenever He wishes, and absorbs it in Him at His Will. There is nothing without Him, and He has stitched (controlled) the whole world with the thread of His laws.  Only that individual acquires His eternal Naam whom He grants the understanding. That fortunate devotee has the knowledge of Waheguru (is connected to Him), and he views everybody the same way (without any prejudice).  Such a devotee feels as if he is the victor of the whole world (is fully contented), oh Nanak.   1.

ਜੀਅ ਜੰਤ੍ਰ ਸਭ ਤਾ ਕੇ ਹਾਥਿ॥ ਦੀਨ ਦਇਆਲ ਅਨਾਥ ਕਉ ਨਾਥੁ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਰਾਖੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਕੋਇ ਨ ਮਾਰੈ॥ ਸੋ ਮੂਆ ਜਿਸੁ ਮਨਹੁ ਬਿਸਾਰੈ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਤਜਿ ਅਵਰ ਕਹਾ ਕੋ ਜਾਇ॥ ਸਭ ਸਿਰਿ ਏਕੁ ਨਿਰੰਜਨ ਰਾਇ॥ ਜੀਅ ਕੀ ਜੁਗਤਿ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਸਭ ਹਾਥਿ॥ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਬਾਹਰਿ ਜਾਨਹੁ ਸਾਥਿ॥ ਗੁਨ ਨਿਧਾਨ ਬੇਅੰਤ ਅਪਾਰ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਦਾਸ ਸਦਾ ਬਲਿਹਾਰ॥2॥

Jea juntar subh Ta kay haath(i).  Deen duyaal, anaath kuou Naath(u).  Jis(u) raakhaiy tis(u( koyay na maaraiy. So mooaa, jis(u) munhou bisaaraiy. Tis(u) tuj(i) uver kuhaa ko jaayay. Subh sir(i) Aik(u) Nirunjun Raayay. Jea kee joogut(i) Ja kaiy subh haath(i). Untar baahur(i) jaanhou saath(i).  Goon nidhaan, bayunt, apaar. Nanak, dass suda bulihaar.  2.

Word Meaning:  Jea Juntar = living beings.   Haath(i) = in the control.   Deen = weak; helpless.   Anaath = orphan; support-less.     Bisaaraiy = ignores.   Tuj(i) = leaving His shelter.    Joogut(i) = living style.   Bayunt = infinite.    Apaar = endless.     2.

MEANING: Waheguru controls all the living beings; He is kind to the weak (humble) and is the guardian of the helpless.  Nobody can kill a living being whom He protects, but the individual whom He ignores dies a spiritual death.  There is no escape from Him because the lone Royal Waheguru is the Head (controller) of everybody.  The lifestyle of living beings is controlled by Him (some of the living beings are vegetarian while others are carnivores; some live in water, some on the land, and still, some in the air); and one must realize that He is with you inside and outside (everywhere).  Waheguru is infinite, endless, and is the treasure of all virtues.  I am always willing to lay down my life for Him, oh Nanak.  2.

ਪੂਰਨ ਪੂਰਿ ਰਹੇ ਦਇਆਲ॥ ਸਭ ਊਪਰਿ ਹੋਵਤ ਕਿਰਪਾਲ॥ ਅਪਨੇ ਕਰਤਬ ਜਾਨੈ ਆਪਿ॥ ਅੰਤਰਜਾਮੀ ਰਹਿਓ ਬਿਆਪਿ॥ ਪ੍ਰਤਿਪਾਲੈ ਜੀਅਨ ਬਹੁ ਭਾਤਿ॥ ਜੋ ਜੋ ਰਚਿਓ ਸੁ ਤਿਸਹਿ ਧਿਆਤਿ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਲਏ ਮਿਲਾਇ॥ ਭਗਤਿ ਕਰਹਿ ਹਰਿ ਕੇ ਗੁਣ ਗਾਇ॥ ਮਨ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਬਿਸਵਾਸੁ ਕਰਿ ਮਾਨਿਆ॥ ਕਰਨਹਾਰੁ ਨਾਨਕ ਇਕੁ ਜਾਨਿਆ॥ 3॥

Poorun poor(i) ruhay Duyaal.  Subh oopur(i) hovut kirpal.  Apnay kertub jaanaiy Aap(i). Untarjaami ruhiyo biyaap(i).  Pritpaalaiy jeaun buhou bhaant(i). Jo, jo ruchiyo, su Tiseh(i) dhiyaat. Jis(u) bhaavaiy, tis(u) luyay milaayay. Bhugut(i) kureh(i), Hur(i) kay goon gaayay. Mun untar(i) bisvaas(u) kur(i) maaniya.  Kernhaar(u) Nanak Ikk(u) jaaniya.   3.

Word Meaning: Poor(i) Ruhay = pervading the entire space.   Kirpal = kind.   Kertub = amazing feats.    Untarjaami = omniscient.   Biyaap(i) = impacting.   Pritpaalaiy = nurtures and nourishes; raises.   Bhaant(i) = in different ways.   Dhiyaat = recites (His Naam).   Bisvaas(u) = faith.     Kerhaar(u) = Creator.

MEANING:  The perfect and the kind Waheguru is pervading the entire universe, and is kind to everybody.  The omniscient Waheguru controls the entire universe, and only He knows His uncanny feats.  He raises the living beings in a variety of ways, and everybody, which He has created, recites His Naam (obeys His Huqam).  He connects the individuals of His choosing with Him who worship Him and recite His Naam.  Those who believe in Him by their mind with full faith, realize (get connected with) the only Creator (Waheguru), oh Nanak.  3.

                                                                                                 

ਜਨੁ ਲਾਗਾ ਹਰਿ ਏਕੈ ਨਾਇ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕੀ ਆਸ ਨ ਬਿਰਥੀ ਜਾਇ॥ ਸੇਵਕ ਕਉ ਸੇਵਾ ਬਨਿ ਆਈ॥ ਹੁਕਮੁ ਬੂਝਿ ਪਰਮ ਪਦੁ ਪਾਈ॥ ਇਸ ਤੇ ਊਪਰਿ ਨਹੀ ਬੀਚਾਰੁ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਬਸਿਆ ਨਿਰੰਕਾਰੁ॥ ਬੰਧਨ ਤੋਰਿ ਭਏ ਨਿਰਵੈਰ॥ ਅਨਦਿਨੁ ਪੂਜਹਿ ਗੁਰ ਕੇ ਪੈਰ॥ ਇਹ ਲੋਕ ਸੁਖੀਏ ਪਰਲੋਕ ਸੁਹੇਲੇ॥

(ਅੰਗ 293)॥   ਨਾਨਕ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭਿ ਆਪਹਿ ਮੇਲੇ॥ 4॥

Jun(u) laaga Hur(i) aikaiy Naayay. Tis kee aas na birthi jaayay.  Sayvuk kuou sayva bun(i) aayee.  Huqam boojh(i) Perm pud(u) paayee.  Iss tay oopur(i) nuhee beechaar(u). Ja kaiy mun(i) busiya Nirunkaar(u). Bundhun toar(i) bhuyay nirvaiyr. Undin poojeh(i) Gur kay Paiyr. Ih loak sookhiyay, perloak soohaylay. 

(PAGE 293):                    Nanak Hur(i) Prubh(i) aapeh(i) maylay.   4.

Word Meaning:  Jun(u) = devotee.      Birthi = empty; unfulfilled.   Bun(i) aayee = befits.    Perm Pud = highest spiritual state; salvation.   Nirvaiyr = free from enmity.   Gur Kay Paiyr = feet (Word) of the Guru. Ih Loak = this world. Perloak = next world. 4.

MEANING: The devotee, who recites Waheguru’s Naam, never has his wish unfulfilled. The devotee, who has Waheguru’s Naam embedded in his heart, realizes that there is no higher thinking than obeying Waheguru’s Huqam and serving Him. Such thinking befits the devotee perfectly, and as a consequence of that, he attains the highest spiritual state.  By worshipping the Guru’s Feet (obeying Him) all the time, the devotee is able to break all the worldly bonds and becomes free of any enmity.  Waheguru connects such a devotee with Him on His own volition, as a result of which both – the present and the next world – become very pleasant (blissful) for the devotee, oh Nanak.  4.

ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਮਿਲਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਅਨੰਦ॥ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਵਹੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਪਰਮਾਨੰਦ॥ ਰਾਮ ਨਾਮ ਤਤੁ ਕਰਹੁ ਬੀਚਾਰੁ॥

ਦ੍ਰੁਲਭ ਦੇਹ ਕਾ ਕਰਹੁ ਉਧਾਰੁ॥ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤ ਬਚਨ ਹਰਿ ਕੇ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਉ॥ ਪ੍ਰਾਨ ਤਰਨ ਕਾ ਇਹੈ ਸੁਆਉ॥ ਆਠ ਪਹਰ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਪੇਖਹੁ ਨੇਰਾ॥ ਮਿਟੈ ਅਗਿਆਨੁ ਬਿਨਸੈ ਅੰਧੇਰਾ॥ ਸੁਨਿ ਉਪਦੇਸੁ ਹਿਰਦੈ ਬਸਾਵਹੁ॥ ਮਨ ਇਛੇ ਨਾਨਕ ਫਲ ਪਾਵਹੁ॥ 5॥

Sadh sung(i) mil(i) kurhou anund. Goon gaavhou Prubh Permanund.  Ram Naam tut(u) kurhou beechaar(u).  Doorlubh dayh ka kurhou oodhaar(u). Amrit Buchun Hur(i) kay goon gaaou.  Praan tern ka ihaiy sooaaou. Aatth pehr Prubh paykhou nayra.  Mitaiy ugiyaan(u) binsaiy undhayra. Soon(i) Oopdaysh(u) hirdaiy busaavhou. Mun ichhay, Nanak ful paavhou.   5. 

Word Meaning:  Anund = blissfulness.   Permanund(u) = (the source of) supreme bliss (Waheguru).  Tut = origin; reality.    Doorlubh = rare; not easily available.  Oodhaar = salvation.    Praan = life; man.    Tern = swim across the sea of Maya; salvation.     Sooaaou = purpose; objective.   Paykhou = see; find.   Ichhay = as desired.  5.

MEANING:  Sing the virtues of the source of supreme bliss, Waheguru, in the company of the Guru, and enjoy blissfulness, oh man. Ponder on Waheguru’s Naam, which is the only reality, and sing His virtues by the Amrit-like Word of the Guru, as a result of which, you will find salvation in this life. (One needs to remember that human life is so precious that it is not easily available, and the objective of human life is to achieve union with God). Think of (believe) Waheguru as being close to you all the time, whereby the darkness of your ignorance will vanish.  Listen to Guru’s Advice, and implant it in your heart so as to have all your wishes fulfilled, oh Nanak. 5.

ਹਲਤੁ ਪਲਤੁ ਦੁਇ ਲੇਹੁ ਸਵਾਰਿ॥ ਰਾਮ ਨਾਮੁ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਉਰਧਾਰਿ॥ ਪੂਰੇ ਗੁਰ ਕੀ ਪੂਰੀ ਦੀਖਿਆ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਮਨਿ ਬਸੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਸਾਚੁ ਪਰੀਖਿਆ॥ ਮਨਿ ਤਨਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਹੁ ਲਿਵ ਲਾਇ॥ ਦੂਖੁ ਦਰਦੁ ਮਨ ਤੇ ਭਉ ਜਾਇ॥ ਸਚੁ ਵਾਪਾਰੁ ਕਰਹੁ ਵਾਪਾਰੀ॥ ਦਰਗਹ ਨਿਬਹੈ ਖੇਪ ਤੁਮਾਰੀ॥ ਏਕਾ ਟੇਕ ਰਖਹੁ ਮਨ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਬਹੁਰਿ ਨ ਆਵਹਿ ਜਾਹਿ॥ 6॥

Hult(u) pult(u) dooyay layhou suvaar(i).  Ram Naam(u) untar(i) oordhaar(i).  Pooray Gur kee poori deekhiya. Jis(u) mun(i) busaiy, tis Sach(u) preekhiya. Mun(i) tun(i) Naam(u) jup-hou liv laayay. Dookh, durd(u) mun tay bhuou jaayay. Sach(u) vaapaar(u) kurhou vaapaari. Durgeh nibhaiy khayp toomhaari.  Aika tayk rukhou mun maah(i).  Nanak buhour(i) na aaveh(i) jaah(i).  6.

Word Meaning:  Hult = this world.    Pult = the next world (after death).    Svaar(i) =  make successful.     Oodhaar(i) = implant in the heart.    Deekhiya = Teaching.    Preekhiya = tested; realized.   Liv Laayay = (with) full concentration.   Bhuou = fear.  Vaapaar(u) = business.   Nibhaiy = fulfills commitment; earns you honour.    Khayp = merchandise (of Waheguru’s worship).   Aika = of only One (Waheguru).   Tayk Rukhou = depend upon the support.       Buhour(i) = again.    6.

MEANING: By enshrining Waheguru’s Naam in your heart, make this world, and the next, successful for you, oh my friend.  Whoever implants the Perfect Teachings of the Perfect Guru in his heart realizes the eternal Waheguru.  Recite Waheguru’s Naam with full concentration of body and mind, whereby all your troubles, discomforts, and fears of the mind will disappear. This is the everlasting (true in all eons) business of humans as businessmen.  Such a holy merchandise (Waheguru’s worship) will get you honour in Waheguru’s Court.  Depend only on Waheguru’s support, and you will never be in the birth-death cycle again, oh Nanak.   6.

ਤਿਸ ਤੇ ਦੂਰਿ ਕਹਾ ਕੋ ਜਾਇ॥ ਉਬਰੈ ਰਾਖਨਹਾਰੁ ਧਿਆਇ॥ ਨਿਰਭਉ ਜਪੈ ਸਗਲ ਭਉ ਮਿਟੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਤੇ ਪ੍ਰਾਣੀ ਛੁਟੈ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਰਾਖੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਨਾਹੀ ਦੂਖ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਜਪਤ ਮਨਿ ਹੋਵਤ ਸੂਖ॥ ਚਿੰਤਾ ਜਾਇ ਮਿਟੈ ਅਹੰਕਾਰੁ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਕਉ ਕੋਇ ਨ ਪਹੁਚਨਹਾਰੁ॥ ਸਿਰ ਉਪਰਿ ਠਾਢਾ ਗੁਰੁ ਸੂਰਾ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਾ ਕੇ ਕਾਰਜ ਪੂਰਾ॥ 7॥

Tis tay door(i) kuhaa ko jaayay. Oobraiy raakhunhaar(u) dhiyayay. Nirbhuou jupaiy, sugul bhuou mitaiy. Prubh kirpa tay praani chhootaiy.  Jis(u) Prubh raakhaiy, tis(u) naahi dookh. Naam(u) juput mun(i) hovut sookh.  Chinta jaayay, mitaiy ahunkaar(u).  Tis(u) jun kuou koyay na puhoochunhaar(u).  Sir oopur(i) tthaandha Gur(u) soora. Nanak ta kay kaaruj poora.  7.

Word Meaning:   Tis tay = from Waheguru.   Oobraiy = find salvation.   Raakhunhaar = the Saviour (Waheguru).      Chhootaiy = becomes free from all blame.   Puhoochunhaar = compete with.   Tthaandha = standing by (to provide support).   Kaaruj Poora = all objectives achieved.     7.       

MEANING: Where can the man run away from Waheguru?  One can find salvation only by reciting the Naam of the Saviour (Waheguru).  All one’s fears are gone by reciting the Naam of the Fearless (Waheguru), and one finds freedom from the bonds of Maya with Waheguru’s benevolence.  The individual whom Waheguru protects never suffers any discomfort; his mind feels blissful while reciting His Naam. His ego and all his worries vanish. Nobody can rival such a devotee.  The Perfect Guru stands by him, and all his objectives of life are achieved, oh Nanak. 7.

ਮਤਿ ਪੂਰੀ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤੁ ਜਾ ਕੀ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ॥ ਦਰਸਨੁ ਪੇਖਤ ਉਧਰਤ ਸ੍ਰਿਸਟਿ॥ ਚਰਨ ਕਮਲ ਜਾ ਕੇ ਅਨੂਪ॥ ਸਫਲ ਦਰਸਨੁ ਸੁੰਦਰ ਹਰਿ ਰੂਪ॥ ਧੰਨੁ ਸੇਵਾ ਸੇਵਕੁ ਪਰਵਾਨੁ॥ ਅੰਤਰਜਾਮੀ ਪੁਰਖੁ ਪ੍ਰਧਾਨੁ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਮਨਿ ਬਸੈ ਸੁ ਹੋਤ ਨਿਹਾਲੁ॥ ਤਾ ਕੈ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਨ ਆਵਤ ਕਾਲੁ॥ ਅਮਰ ਭਏ ਅਮਰਾ ਪਦ ਪਾਇਆ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਹਰਿ ਧਿਆਇਆ॥ 8॥22॥

Mut(i) poori, Amrit(u) ja kee drisht(i).  Dershun(u) paykhut, oodhrut srisht(i).  Churn Kumul Ja kay anoop.  Suful durshun(u) soondar Hur(i) roop.  Dhunn(u) sayva, sayvuk(u) pervaan(u).  Untarjaami, Purakh(u) perdhaan(u).  Jis(u) mun(i) busaiy, su hoat nihaal(u).  Ta kaiy nicket(i) na aavut kaal(u).  Amar bhuyay, Amra pud paaya. Sadh sung(i) Nanak, Hur(i) dhiyaaya.  8.22.

Word Meaning:  Mut(i) = wisdom; Teachings.   Drisht(i) = (casting of) sight.   Dershun(u) = sight; glimpse.   Oodhrut = is salvaged.   Churn Kumul = lotus-like feet (His Naam).  Anoop = unparalleled.   Suful = fruitful; rewarding.    Dhunn(u) = praise-worthy; good fortune.  Untarjaami = omniscient.     Perdhaan = supreme.    Nihaal(u) = blissful.    Kaal(u) = (spiritual) death.   Amar = immortal.     Pud = level.    8.22.

MEANING:  Waheguru has perfect wisdom, and the casting of His sight is Amrit for the recipient (viewer).   The whole world finds salvation at His holy sight.  His lotus-like Feet (His Words) are unparalleled, and He is pretty (holy and attractive) in every way; His sight is highly rewarding.  The omniscient Waheguru is Supreme, and it is one’s good fortune to serve Him; His devotees are highly lucky and are honoured in His Court.   The individual, who has His Naam embedded in his heart, is blessed, and the fear of death does not come even near him.  Such devotees sing Waheguru’s praise in the company of the Guru, attain the highest spiritual state, and become immortal, oh Nanak.  8.22.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਗਿਆਨ ਅੰਜਨੁ ਗੁਰਿ ਦੀਆ ਅਗਿਆਨ ਅੰਧੇਰ ਬਿਨਾਸੁ॥ ਹਰਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਤੇ ਸੰਤ ਭੇਟਿਆ ਨਾਨਕ ਮਨਿ ਪਰਗਾਸੁ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Giyaan unjun(u) Gur(i) deeya, agiyaan undhayr binaas(u). Hur(i) kirpa tay Sunt bhaytiya, Nanak, mun(i) pergaas(u).  1.

Word Meaning:  Unjun = kohl (which is believed to improve eyesight).   Agiyaan = ignorance.    Binaas(u) = destruction.   Sunt = Guru. Bhaytiya = connected with.   1.

MEANING:  The Guru has given me the kohl of Divine Knowledge (for the eyes of my mind) with which all the darkness of my ignorance is gone. With God’s grace, I got connected to the Guru who enlightened my mind, oh Nanak.   1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਸੰਤ ਸੰਗਿ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਡੀਠਾ॥ ਨਾਮੁ ਪ੍ਰਭੂ ਕਾ ਲਾਗਾ ਮੀਠਾ॥ ਸਗਲ ਸਮਿਗ੍ਰੀ ਏਕਸੁ ਘਟ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਰੰਗ ਨਾਨਾ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟਾਹਿ॥ ਨਉ ਨਿਧਿ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕਾ ਨਾਮੁ॥ ਦੇਹੀ ਮਹਿ ਇਸ ਕਾ ਬਿਸ੍ਰਾਮੁ॥ ਸੁੰਨ ਸਮਾਧਿ ਅਨਹਤ ਤਹ ਨਾਦ॥ ਕਹਨੁ ਨ ਜਾਈ ਅਚਰਜ ਬਿਸਮਾਦ॥ ਤਿਨਿ ਦੇਖਿਆ ਜਿਸੁ ਆਪਿ ਦਿਖਾਏ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਸੋਝੀ ਪਾਇ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Sunt sung(i) untar(i) Prubh(u) deettha. Naam(u) Prubhoo ka laaga meettha. Sugul smugree Aikus ghut maah(i).  Anik rung nana drishtaah(i). Naoun nidh(i) Amrit(u) Prubh ka Naam(u). Dayhi meh(i) iss ka bisraam(u). Soonn smaadh(i) unhut teh naad. Kehn(u) na jaayee achurj bismaad. Tin(i) daykhiya, jis(u) Aap(i) dikhaayay. Nanak tis(u) jun sojhi paayay.   1.

Word Meaning:   Untar(i) = in the heart.    Deettha = saw; realized.    Sugul Smugree = all bodies; the whole world.   Aikus(u) = the One (Waheguru).   Ghut Maah(i) = in His mind.  Nana = different.   Drishtaah(i) = visible.   Naoun Nidh(i) = the nine mythical treasures. Dayhee = body.  Bisraam = dwelling.  Soonn Smaadh(i) = the state in meditation in which no thought originates. Unhut Naad = the auto-playing music that the yogis believe to hear during meditation.   Achurj Bismaad = amazing mesmerization.   Sojhi = understanding.   1.

MEANING:  I found Waheguru’s Naam to be very sweet, and realized Him in my mind in the company (shelter) of the Guru.  Waheguru is seen (presents Himself) in many different colours and ways, and the whole world is on His mind (He worries about, or looks after it).  Waheguru’s Amrit-like Naam is the source of all the nine treasures of the world, and it dwells imperceptibly in the body. When one meditates on the Naam with full concentration, so that no other thought arises in the mind, one attains an amazing spiritual state that cannot be described, because one becomes fully mesmerized and blissful, as if listening to the auto-playing Divine Music. But only those individuals experience such a blissful state whom Waheguru gives understanding and enables to do so, oh Nanak.  1.

ਸੋ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਸੋ ਬਾਹਰਿ ਅਨੰਤ॥ ਘਟਿ ਘਟਿ ਬਿਆਪਿ ਰਹਿਆ ਭਗਵੰਤ॥ ਧਰਨਿ ਮਾਹਿ ਆਕਾਸ ਪਇਆਲ॥ ਸਰਬ ਲੋਕ ਪੂਰਨ ਪ੍ਰਤਿਪਾਲ॥

(ਅੰਗ 294)॥    ਬਨਿ ਤਿਨਿ ਪਰਬਤਿ ਹੈ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮੁ॥ ਜੈਸੀ ਆਗਿਆ ਤੈਸਾ ਕਰਮੁ॥ ਪਉਣ ਪਾਣੀ ਬੈਸੰਤਰ ਮਾਹਿ॥ ਚਾਰਿ ਕੁੰਟ ਦਹ ਦਿਸਿ ਸਮਾਹਿ॥ ਤਿਸ ਤੇ ਭਿੰਨ ਨਹੀ ਕੋ ਠਾਉ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਸੁਖੁ ਪਾਉ॥ 2॥

So untar(i), So baahur(i) Anunt. Ghut(i), ghut(i) biyaap(i) ruhiya Bhugvunt. Dhern(i) maah(i) aakaash puyaal . Serb loak poorun pritpaal. 

(PAGE 294):                  Bun(i) tin(i) perbut(i) haiy Parbraham.  Jaiysi aagiya, taiysa kerm(u).  Paun, paani, baiysuntar maah(i). Chaar(i) koont deh dis(i) smaah(i). Tis tay bhinn nuhee ko tthaaoun. Gur persaad(i) Nanak sookh(u) paaoun.  2. 

Word Meaning:   Anunt = limitless (Waheguru).   Biyaap(i) = impacting; present.   Dhern(i) = earth.   Puyaal = underworld.   Loak = worlds.   Pritpaal = nourish and nurture.   Tin(i) = straws; vegetation.   Aagiya = Command.   Kerm(u) = behaviour; functions.  Baiysuntar = fire.  Char(i) Koont = the four major directions.   Deh Dis(i) = the ten sub directions (four main; four in corners, and up and down).  Bhinn = different; without.       Tthaaoun = place.   2.

MEANING: The limitless Waheguru, which is present inside you, is also present outside (in nature), and is impacting every heart.  Waheguru, which nourishes and nurtures all His creation, is fully filling (is present in) the earth, the sky, and the underworld.  He is present in the forests, in all vegetation, and the mountains, which function in ways they are ordered to (obey His Commands).   Waheguru is present in the air, in the waters, in fire, and in all the four major and the ten sub directions.  No place is without Him.  One learns all this by the grace of the Guru, and enjoys all comforts, oh Nanak.   2. 

ਬੇਦ ਪੁਰਾਨ ਸਿੰਮ੍ਰਿਤਿ ਮਹਿ ਦੇਖੁ॥ ਸਸੀਅਰ ਸੂਰ ਨਖ੍ਹਤਰ ਮਹਿ ਏਕੁ॥ ਬਾਣੀ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੀ ਸਭੁ ਕੋ ਬੋਲੈ॥ ਆਪਿ ਅਡੋਲੁ ਨ ਕਬਹੂ ਡੋਲੈ॥ ਸਰਬ ਕਲਾ ਕਰਿ ਖੇਲੈ ਖੇਲ॥ ਮੋਲਿ ਨ ਪਾਈਐ ਗੁਣਹ ਅਮੋਲ॥ ਸਰਬ ਜੋਤਿ ਮਹਿ ਜਾ ਕੀ ਜੋਤਿ॥ ਧਾਰਿ ਰਹਿਓ ਸੁਆਮੀ ਓਤਿ ਪੋਤਿ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪਰਸਾਦਿ ਭਰਮ ਕਾ ਨਾਸੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਨ ਮਹਿ ਏਹੁ ਬਿਸਾਸੁ॥3

Bayd Puraan simrit(i) meh(i) daykh(u). Sussiur, soor, nukhuttar meh(i) Aik(u). Bani Prubh kee subh(u) ko boalaiy. Aap(i) adoal(u), na kubhoon doalaiy. Serb kulaa kur(i) khaylaiy khayl. Moal(i) na paayiyaiy gooneh amoal. Serb jot(i) meh Ja kee Jot(i). dhaar(i) ruhiyou Soami oat(i) poat(i).  Gur persaad(i) bherm ka naas(u).  Nanak, tin meh(i) ehou bisaas(u).  3.

Word Meaning: Sussiur = moon.     Soor = sun.   Nukhuttar = stars.   Bani = words of praise; Huqam.   Boalaiy = utter; obey.   Adoal(u) = yields to no command (no power above Him).  Khalaiy Khayl = enjoys His creation.   Gooneh Amoal = invaluable virtues.  Jot(i) = light.     Oat(i) Poat(i) = like cross threads in fabric (weft and woof).   Bherm = misgivings; doubts.   Bisaas(u) = faith; confidence. 

MEANING: Waheguru is reflected in all the Hindu religious books (they were written by men created by Waheguru with the ability to write).  The same Waheguru is also reflected (is present) in the moon, in the sun, and in the stars.  They all sing Waheguru’s praise (obey His command), but Waheguru does not yield to any command (there is no power above Him).  He creates the drama of the universe with His powers, and then enjoys it.  He has so many virtues (powers) that they cannot be fully evaluated (or appreciated). His Light (Soul) is present in all living beings, and He is mixed (blended) with them just like the cross threads in fabric.  With Guru’s grace, all the misgivings of the devotee are dispelled, and a firm faith in Waheguru is established, oh Nanak.    3.

ਸੰਤ ਜਨਾ ਕਾ ਪੇਖਨ ਸਭੁ ਬ੍ਰਹਮ॥ ਸੰਤ ਜਨਾ ਕੇ ਹਿਰਦੈ ਸਭਿ ਧਰਮ॥ ਸੰਤ ਜਨਾ ਸੁਨਹਿ ਸੁਭ ਬਚਨ॥ ਸਰਬ ਬਿਆਪੀ ਰਾਮ ਸੰਗਿ ਰਚਨ॥ ਜਿਨਿ ਜਾਤਾ ਤਿਸ ਕੀ ਇਹ ਰਹਤ॥ ਸਤਿ ਬਚਨ ਸਾਧੂ ਸਭਿ ਕਹਤ॥ ਜੋ ਜੋ ਹੋਇ ਸੋਈ ਸੁਖੁ ਮਾਨੈ॥ ਕਰਨ ਕਰਾਵਨਹਾਰੁ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਜਾਨੈ॥ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਬਸੇ ਬਾਹਰਿ ਭੀ ਓਹੀ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਦਰਸਨੁ ਦੇਖਿ ਸਭ ਮੋਹੀ॥ 4॥

Sunt junaa ka paykhun subh(u) Braham.  Sunt junaa kay hirdaiy subh(i) dherm.  Sunt junaa sooneh(i) shoobh buchun. Serb biyaapi Ram sung(i) ruchun. Jin(i) jaata, tis kee ih reht.  Sat(i) Buchun Sadhu subh keht. Jo, jo hoyay soyee sookh(u) maanaiy. Kern kraavunhaar(u) Prubh(u) jaanaiy. Untar(i) busay, baahur(i) bhee ohi.  Nanak dershun(u) daykh(i) subh mohi.  4.

Word Meaning:   Paykhun = seeing.   Dherm = spirituality and high morals.   Shoobh Buchun = noble words.   Serb Biyaapi = omnipresent.    Ruchun = mixed well; fully absorbed.     Reht = living-style; code of ethics.  Sat(i) = True; Divine.   Jaanaiy = recognize; make deep connection.  Mohi = captivated. 

MEANING:  The holy saints “see” Waheguru everywhere, and they have only spirituality and high morals in their hearts. They remain fully absorbed in the omnipresent Waheguru and listen only to the words of spirituality and high morality.  The holy saints utter only the words of eternal truth; this is the kind of living style of the saints who have realized Waheguru.  They take comfort in everything that happens to them and have firm faith that it is Waheguru, the creator of everything, who can make others do whatever He wishes.  The Waheguru that resides in human hearts is also present in nature.  Everybody is captivated by His sight (to see Him in nature), oh Nanak.   4.

ਆਪਿ ਸਤਿ ਕੀਆ ਸਭੁ ਸਤਿ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਤੇ ਸਗਲੀ ਉਤਪਤਿ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਕਰੇ ਬਿਸਥਾਰੁ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਏਕੰਕਾਰੁ॥ ਅਨਿਕ ਕਲਾ ਲਖੀ ਨਹ ਜਾਇ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਲਏ ਮਿਲਾਇ॥ ਕਵਨ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਕਵਨ ਕਹੀਐ ਦੂਰਿ॥ ਆਪੇ ਆਪਿ ਆਪ ਭਰਪੂਰਿ॥ ਅੰਤਰਗਤਿ ਜਿਸੁ ਆਪਿ ਜਨਾਏ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਆਪਿ ਬੁਝਾਏ॥5॥

Aap(i) Sat(i) keeya subh(u) Sat(i).  Tis(u) Prubh tay suglee output(i).  Tis(u) bhaavaiy ta kuray bisthaar(u). Tis(u) bhaaaiy ta Ekunkaar(u).  Anik kulaa lukhee neh jaayay. Jis(u) bhaavaiy, tis(u) luyay milaayay.  Kuvun nicket(i), kuvun kuhiyaiy door(i).  Aapay Aap(i) Aap bhurpoor(i).  Untargut(i) jis(u) Aap(i) junaayay. Nanak tis(u) jun Aap(i) boojhaayay.  5.

Word Meaning: Aap(i) = God Himself.    Sat(i) = eternal.    Keeya = creation.   Ootput(i) = produced; came into being.    Bisthaar = expansion; creation.   Ekunkaar = God by Himself.   Anik Kula = unlimited power.   Lukhi = understood.   Bhurpoor(i) = filling (occupying) the whole space.    Untargut(i) = spiritually.    5.

MEANING: Waheguru is eternal, and so is His creation, which came into being from Him (He created it from Himself).  He expands (creates) the world whenever He wishes to, and is all by Himself at His Will (He destroys it at His Will).   His unlimited powers cannot be understood; He connects the individuals of His choosing with Him.   We cannot say who is close to Him and who is removed from Him, because He pervades everywhere.  Whom He enlightens spiritually, comes to understand Him, oh Nanak.   5.

ਸਰਬ ਭੂਤ ਆਪਿ ਵਰਤਾਰਾ॥ ਸਰਬ ਨੈਨ ਆਪਿ ਪੇਖਨਹਾਰਾ॥ ਸਗਲ ਸਮਗ੍ਰੀ ਜਾ ਕਾ ਤਨਾ॥ ਆਪਨ ਜਸ ਆਪ ਹੀ ਸੁਨਾ॥ ਆਨ ਜਾਨੁ ਇਕੁ ਖੇਲੁ ਬਨਾਇਆ॥ ਆਗਿਆਕਾਰੀ ਕੀਨੀ ਮਾਇਆ॥ ਸਭ ਕੈ ਮਧਿ ਅਲਿਪਤੋ ਰਹੈ॥ ਜੋ ਕਿਛੁ ਕਹਣਾ ਸੁ ਆਪੇ ਕਹੈ॥ ਆਗਿਆ ਆਵੈ ਆਗਿਆ ਜਾਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜੈ ਭਾਵੈ ਤਾ ਲਏ ਸਮਾਇ॥ 6॥

Serb bhoot Aap(i) vertaara.  Serb naiyn, Aap(i) paykhunhaara. Sugul smugree ja ka tuna. Aapun jus Aap hee soona. Aan jaan(u) ikk(u) khayl(u) bunaaya. Aagiyakaari keeni Maya.  Subh kaiy mudh(i) aliputo ruhaiy. Jo kichh(u) kehna, su Aapaiy kuhaiy. Aagiya aavaiy, aagiya jaayay. Nanak jaiy bhaavaiy ta luyay smaayay.  6.

Word Meaning:  Serb Bhoot = all matter; living beings.  Vertaara = provider; server.  Paykhunhaara = one who sees.   Sugul Smugree = all matter (the whole universe).   Tuna = body.     Aan Jaan(u) = birth and death.    Khayl(u) = drama.   Aagiayakaari = subservient.    Mudh(i) = centre; in.    Aliputo = detached.  Kehna = Command; Huqam; Will.    Aagiyya = Huqam.   6.

MEANING: Waheguru is the provider of all the necessities for all the living beings.  (Being omnipresent), He sees (looks after them) with their eyes.  The whole universe is His body, and He is listening to His praise being sung by the living beings (watching how everybody is obeying His Huqam).  Birth and death are like a drama created by Waheguru in which Maya is His subservient. He dwells in everybody, yet he is fully detached, and enforces His own Will.  The living beings take birth and die as per His Huqam.  He absorbs some in Him (connects with Him) as He likes, oh Nanak.  6.

ਇਸ ਤੇ ਹੋਇ ਸੁ ਨਾਹੀ ਬੁਰਾ॥ ਓਰੈ ਕਹਹੁ ਕਿਨੈ ਕਛੁ ਕਰਾ॥ ਆਪਿ ਭਲਾ ਕਰਤੂਤਿ ਅਤਿ ਨੀਕੀ॥ ਆਪੇ ਜਾਨੈ ਅਪਨੇ ਜੀ ਕੀ॥ ਆਪਿ ਸਾਚੁ ਧਾਰੀ ਸਭ ਸਾਚੁ॥ ਓਤਿ ਪੋਤਿ ਆਪਨ ਸੰਗਿ ਰਾਚੁ॥ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਗਤਿ ਮਿਤਿ ਕਹੀ ਨ ਜਾਇ॥ ਦੂਸਰ ਹੋਇ ਤ ਸੋਝੀ ਪਾਇ॥ ਤਿਸ ਕਾ ਕੀਆ ਸਭੁ ਪਰਵਾਨੁ॥ ਗੁਰ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਨਾਨਕ ਇਹੁ ਜਾਨੁ॥ 7॥

iss tay hoyay su naahi boora.  Oraiy kuhou kinaiy kuchh(u) kuraa.  Aap(i) bhulaa, kertoot(i) ut(i) neeki. Aapay jaanaiy, Apnay jee kee.  Aap(i) Saach(u), dhaari subh Saach(u).  oat(i) poat(i) Aapun sung(i) raach(u).  Ta kee gut(i) mit(i) kuhee na jaayay.  Doosur hoyay, ta sojhi paayay.  Tis ka keeya subh(u) pervaan(u).  Gur persaad(i) Nanak ehou jaan(u).  7.

Word Meaning:  Boora = bad; harmful; evil.   Oraiy = in this world.   Bhula = kind; noble.   Kertoot(i) = deeds.   Ut(i) Neeki = extremely pure and just.   Saach(i) = True; eternal.   Dhaari = created.  Raach(u) = mingled.    Gut(i) = higher spiritual state; transcendence.   Mit(i) = measurements.   Sojhi = understanding.   Pervaan(u) = acceptable; just.   Jaan(u) = understand; knowledge.    7.

MEANING:  Whatever Waheguru does is never bad (or harmful) because He is kind, and all His deeds are extremely pure (just).  But only He knows what He has in mind.  Can someone show me if anyone here (in this world) has done anything good for humanity (without using natural resources)?  Waheguru is the eternal truth, and so is His creation with which He is mixed so closely as the cross threads in fabric.  Nobody can tell the level of His transcendence, and His measurements.  Only someone equal to Him can know those.  Whatever Waheguru does is all just, but one can have this knowledge only by the grace of the Guru, oh Nanak.   7.

ਜੋ ਜਾਨੈ ਤਿਸੁ ਸਦਾ ਸੁਖੁ ਹੋਇ॥ ਆਪਿ ਮਿਲਾਇ ਲਏ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਸੋਇ॥ ਓਹੁ ਧਨਵੰਤੁ ਕੁਲਵੰਤੁ ਪਤਿਵੰਤੁ॥ ਜੀਵਨ ਮੁਕਤਿ ਜਿਸੁ ਰਿਦੈ ਭਗਵੰਤੁ॥ ਧੰਨੁ ਧੰਨੁ ਧੰਨੁ ਜਨੁ ਆਇਆ॥

(ਅੰਗ 295)॥   ਜਿਸੁ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦਿ ਸਭੁ ਜਗਤ ਤਰਾਇਆ॥ ਜਨ ਆਵਨ ਕਾ ਇਹੈ ਸੁਆਉ॥ ਜਨ ਕੈ ਸੰਗਿ ਚਿਤਿ ਆਵੈ ਨਾਉ॥ ਆਪਿ ਮੁਕਤੁ ਮੁਕਤੁ ਕਰੈ ਸੰਸਾਰੁ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਕਉ ਸਦਾ ਨਮਸਕਾਰੁ॥ 8॥23॥

Jo jaanaiy, tis(u) suda sookh(u) hoyay.  Aap(i) milaayay luyay Prubh(u) soyay.  Oh(u) dhunvunt(u), koolvunt(u), putvunt(u).  Jeevun mookut(i) jis(u) ridaiy Bhugvunt(u).  Dhunn(u), dhunn(u), dhunn(u) jun(u) aaya.

(PAGE 295):              Jis(u) persaad(i) subh(u) jugut traaya. Jun aavun ka ihaiy sooaaou. Jun kaiy sung(i) chit(i) aavaiy Naaoun. Aap(i) mookut(u), mookut(u) kuraiy sunsaar(u).  Nanak, tis(u) jun kuou suda numuskaar(u).   8.23.

Word Meaning: Jaanaiy = realizes.  Dhunvunt(u) = wealthy.   Koolvunt(u) = of high clan.   Putvunt(u) = respectable.  Mookut(i) = free from the bonds of Maya.   Dhunn(u) = worthy of respect.   Traaya = is salvaged.      Sooaaou = objective; benefit.    8.

MEANING: Whoever realizes that whatever Waheguru does is just, enjoys bliss all the time, and Waheguru connects such a devotee with Him.
