Such a devotee, who has Waheguru enshrined in his heart, is truly wealthy, belongs to a high clan, is respectable, and is free from all bonds of Maya while living in the family.  Such a saint is worthy of worship because with his grace, the world finds salvation. This is the objective of the coming of such a saint in the world that people remember Waheguru in His company. Such a saint is free from all worldly bonds and sets the world free.  I salute such a great saint all the time, oh Nanak.    8.23.

ਸਲੋਕੁ॥ ਪੂਰਾ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਆਰਾਧਿਆ ਪੂਰਾ ਜਾ ਕਾ ਨਾਉ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਪੂਰਾ ਪਾਇਆ ਪੂਰੈ ਕੇ ਗੁਨ ਗਾਉ॥ 1॥

Sloak(u): Poora Prubh(u) araadhiya, Poora Ja ka Naaoun. Nanak Poora paaya, Pooraiy kay goon gaaoun.  1.

Word Meaning:  Poora = Perfet; omnipresent.  Araadhiya = worshipped.   Naaoun = Huqam.  1.

MEANING:  I worship the Perfect, and the omnipresent Waheguru whose Huqam is (laws are) also perfect. I have realized the Perfect Waheguru, and sing praise of the omnipresent Waheguru, oh Nanak.  1.

ਅਸਟਪਦੀ॥ ਪੂਰੇ ਗੁਰੁ ਕਾ ਸੁਨਿ ਉਪਦੇਸੁ॥ ਪਾਰਬ੍ਰਹਮ ਨਿਕਟਿ ਕਰਿ ਪੇਖੁ॥ ਸਾਸਿ ਸਾਸਿ ਸਿਮਰਹੁ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ॥ ਮਨ ਅੰਤਰਿ ਕੀ ਉਤਰੈ ਚਿੰਦ॥ ਆਸ ਅਨਿਤ ਤਿਆਗਹੁ ਤਰੰਗ॥ ਸੰਤ ਜਨਾ ਕੀ ਧੂਰਿ ਮਨ ਮੰਗ॥ ਆਪੁ ਛੋਡਿ ਬੇਨਤੀ ਕਰਹੁ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਅਗਨਿ ਸਾਗਰੁ ਤਰਹੁ॥ ਹਰਿ ਧਨ ਕੇ ਭਰਿ ਲੇਹੁ ਭੰਡਾਰ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਗੁਰ ਪੂਰੇ ਨਮਸਕਾਰ॥ 1॥

Ashtpudi: Pooray Gur(u) ka soon(i) Oopdaysh(u).  Parbraham, nicket(i) kur(i) paykh(u). Saas(i), saas(i) simerhou Gobind. Mun untar(i) kee ootraiy chind. Aas anit tiyaaghou trung. Sunt junaa kee dhoor(i) mun mung. Aap(u) chhoad(i) baynti kurhou. Sadh sung(i) ugun(i) saagur(u) turhou. Hur(i) dhun kay bhur(i) layhou bundaar. Nanak, Gur Pooray numuskaar.  1.

Word Meaning:  Oopdaysh(u) = Teachings.  Nicket(i) = close by.   Paykh(u) = see; believe.   Chind = worries; thinking.   Anit = countless.   Tiyaagou = give up.   Trung = waves.  Dhoor(i) = dust of the feet; service.  Aap(u) = selfishness.  Ugun Saagur = the sea of fire of desires.   Bhundaar = treasures.   Numuskaar = bow down  to; show respect.   1.

MEANING:  if you listen to the Teachings of the Perfect Guru, you will find Waheguru very close to you, oh my friend.  Recite Waheguru’s Naam(u) with every breath, and all your worries will disappear.  Abandon the countless wishes high waves of which rise in your mind, and beg for the service to the holy saints.  Relinquish your selfishness and ego, and pray in the company of the Guru to swim across the sea of the fire of desires (the world sea) (to find salvation).   Bow to the Perfect Guru (obey Him), and fill your treasure (heart) with the wealth of Waheguru’s Naam, oh Nanak.   1.   

ਖੇਮ ਕੁਸਲ ਸਹਜ ਆਨੰਦ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਭਜੁ ਪਰਮਾਨੰਦ॥ ਨਰਕ ਨਿਵਾਰਿ ੳੇਧਾਰਹੁ ਜੀਉ॥ ਗੁਨ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤ ਰਸੁ ਪੀਉ॥ ਚਿਤਿ ਚਿਤਵਹੁ ਨਾਰਾਇਣ ਏਕੁ॥ ਏਕ ਰੂਪ ਜਾ ਕੇ ਰੰਗ ਅਨੇਕ॥ ਗੋਪਾਲ ਦਾਮੋਦਰ ਦੀਨ ਦਇਆਲ॥ ਦੁਖ ਭੰਜਨ ਪੂਰਨ ਕਿਰਪਾਲ॥ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਸਿਮਰਿ ਨਾਮੁ ਬਾਰੰਬਾਰ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜੀਅ ਕਾ ਇਹੈ ਅਧਾਰ॥ 2॥

Khaym, kooshul, sehj, aanund.  Sadh sung(i) bhuj(u) Permanund.  Nerk nivaar(I)  oodhaarhou jeeo. Goon Gobind, Amrit rus(u) peeou.  Chit(i) chituvhou Naraayin aik(u).  Aik roop, Ja kay rung anayk.  Gopaal, Damodar, deen duyaal. Dookh-bhunjun, Poorun Kirpaal.  Simer, simer, Naam(u) baarumbaar. Nanak jea ka ihaiy Adhaar.   2.

Word Meaning:  Khaym Kooshul = wellbeing.    Sehj Aanund = blissfulness.  Bhuj(u) = recite Naam(u).  Permanund = the source of supreme bliss (God).   Nivaar(i) = eliminating.    Jeeo = soul.   Chituvhou = think of.    Naraayin = Waheguru.   Roop = entity; basic identity.    Rung = forms.   Gopaal, Damodar = names of Waheguru.  Dookh- Bhunjun = destroyer of all suffering.  Baarumbaar = repeatedly.  Jea = soul; life.   Adhaar = basis.   2. 

MEANING: Recite the Naam of Waheguru, which is the source of supreme bliss, in the company of the Guru, and enjoy the wellbeing and the bliss of the peaceful state of mind, oh my friend.   Sing praise of Waheguru, and drink the sweetness of the Naam; besides, your fear of any hell will be eradicated, and you will find salvation.  Think only of Waheguru in your mind, which has only one basic entity, but countless forms. The perfect and kind Waheguru is always compassionate to the helpless and is the destroyer of all suffering.  Recite His Naam repeatedly, which is the basis of your soul, oh Nanak. 2.

ਉਤਮ ਸਲੋਕ ਸਾਧ ਕੇ ਬਚਨ॥ ਅਮੁਲੀਕ ਲਾਲ ਏਹਿ ਰਤਨ॥ ਸੁਨਤ ਕਮਾਵਤ ਹੋਤ ਉਧਾਰ॥ ਆਪਿ ਤਰੈ ਲੋਕਹ ਨਿਸਤਾਰ॥ ਸਫਲ ਜੀਵਨੁ ਸਫਲ ਤਾ ਕਾ ਸੰਗੁ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਲਾਗਾ ਹਰਿ ਰੰਗੁ॥ ਜੈ ਜੈ ਸਬਦੁ ਅਨਾਹਦੁ ਵਾਜੈ॥ ਸੁਨਿ ਸੁਨਿ ਅਨਦ ਕਰੇ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਗਾਜੈ॥ ਪ੍ਰਗਟੇ ਗੁਪਾਲ ਮਹਾਤ ਕੇ ਮਾਥੇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਉਧਰੇ ਤਿਨ ਕੈ ਸਾਥੇ॥ 3॥

Ootum Sloak, Sadh kay Buchun. Amooleek laal eh(i) ruttan.  Soonut, kmaavut, hoat oodhaar. Aap(i) turaiy, loakah nistaar. Suful jeevun(u), suful Ta ka sung(u).  Ja kaiy mun(i) laaga Hur(i) rung(u). Jaiy, jaiy, Shabad(u) anaahud(u) vaajaiy.  Soon(i), soon(i) anund kuray, Prubh gaajaiy. Pergutay Goopaal Mahaant kay maathay. Nanak, oodhray tin  kaiy saathay.  3.

Word Meaning:  Ootum = superior; Divine.   Sadh = Guru.   Amooleek = invaluable.   Laal = rubies.    Ruttan = jewels.    Kmaavut = putting into practice.   Oodhaar = Nistaar = salvation.   Suful = successful.   Jaiy, jaiy = long living; eternal.  Gaajaiy = roars; dominates.   Mahaant = saints.   Maathay = on the forehead.   3.

MEANING:  The Words of the Guru are the best spiritual poetry (Teaching), and they are like the invaluable rubies and jewels. Whoever listens to them and puts them in practice (leads life according to them), finds salvation - not just for him, but for the whole world.  A devotee, whose mind is imbued with Waheguru’s love, not only makes his own life successful (achieves the objective of life), but also for all those who are in his company.  Waheguru’s eternal Shabad plays the Divine music in the devotee’s mind, who enjoys bliss when Waheguru’s Naam roars in his mind (he cannot hear any undesirable noise of his mind).  Waheguru appears on the forehead of the holy saints (enlightens their mind); I will also find salvation in their company, oh Nanak.  3.

ਸਰਨਿ ਜੋਗੁ ਸੁਨਿ ਸਰਨੀ ਆਏ॥ ਕਰਿ ਕਿਰਪਾ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਆਪ ਮਿਲਾਏ॥ ਮਿਟਿ ਗਏ ਬੈਰ ਭਏ ਸਭ ਰੇਨ॥ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤ ਨਾਮੁ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਲੈਨ॥ ਸੁਪ੍ਰਸੰਨ ਭਏ ਗੁਰਦੇਵ॥ ਪੂਰਨ ਹੋਈ ਸੇਵਕ ਕੀ ਸੇਵ॥ ਆਲ ਜੰਜਾਲ ਬਿਕਾਰ ਤੇ ਰਹਤੇ ਰਾਮ ਨਾਮ ਸੁਨਿ ਰਸਨਾ ਕਹਤੇ॥ ਕਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਸਾਦੁ ਦਇਆ ਪ੍ਰਭਿ ਧਾਰੀ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਨਿਬਹੀ ਖੇਪ ਹਮਾਰੀ॥ 4॥

Shern(i) joag(u) soon(i) sherni aayay. Kur(i) kirpa Prubh aap milaayay.  Mit(i) guyay baiyr, bhuyay subh rayn. Amrit Naam(u) Sadh sung(i) laiyn. Soopersunn bhuyay Gurdev. Poorun hoyee sayvuk kee sayv. Aal, junjaal bikaar tay rehtay. Ram Naam soon(i) rusna kehtay. Kur(i) persaad(i) duyya Prubh(i) dhaari.  Nanak nibhee khayp humaari.  4.

Word Meaning: Shern(i) Joag(u) = all capable of providing shelter (Satguru).  Baiyr = enmity.   Rayn = dust of the feet; extreme humility.  Soopersunn = overly pleased.     Aal Junjaal = worldly obligations; bonds of Maya.   Bikaar = vices.   Rehtay = removed from.   Dhaari = created; provided; bestowed.    Khayp = merchandise; wealth of worship.   4.

MEANING: Hearing about the provider of complete shelter (Satguru), the devotees come to His shelter when He kindly connects them with Waheguru.  The devotees recite Waheguru’s Amrit-Naam in the company of the Guru, whereby they become extremely humble and overcome all their animosity for others.  Waheguru becomes overly pleased with them, and their hard labour is recognized. They stay away from worldly obligations (attractions) and vices.  They listen to Waheguru’s Naam and recite it with their tongue.  The kind Waheguru became benevolent to me, and my wealth of His worship was successfully delivered to His Court (He approved my hard labour), oh Nanak.  4.

ਪ੍ਰਭ ਕੀ ਉਸਤਤਿ ਕਰਹੁ ਸੰਤ ਮੀਤ॥ ਸਾਵਧਾਨ ਏਕਾਗਰ ਚੀਤ॥ ਸੁਖਮਨੀ ਸਹਜ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ ਗੁਨ ਨਾਮ॥ ਜਿਸੁ ਮਨਿ ਬਸੈ ਸੁ ਹੋਤ ਨਿਧਾਨ॥ ਸਰਬ ਇਛਾ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਪੂਰਨ ਹੋਇ॥ ਪ੍ਰਧਾਨ ਪੁਰਖੁ ਪ੍ਰਗਟੁ ਸਭ ਲੋਇ॥ ਸਭ ਤੇ ਊਚ ਪਾਏ ਅਸਥਾਨੁ॥ਬਹੁਰਿ ਨ ਹੋਵੈ ਆਵਨ ਜਾਨੁ॥ ਹਰਿ ਧਨੁ ਖਾਟਿ ਚਲੈ ਜਨੁ ਸੋਇ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਜਿਸਹਿ ਪਰਾਪਤਿ ਹੋਇ॥5॥

Prubh kee oostut(i) kurhou sunt meet.  Saavdhaan, aikaagur cheet. Sukhmani, sehj, Gobind Goon Naam.  Jis(u) mun(i) busaiy, su hoat nidhaan. Serb ichha ta kee poorun hoyay. Perdhaan, Purakh(u) pergut(u) subh loyay.  Subh tay ooch paayay usthaan(u). Buhour(i) na hovaiy aavun jaan(u). Hur(i) dhun(u) khaat(i) chulaiy jun(u) soyay. Nanak jiseh(i) praaput(i) hoyay.  5. 

Word Meanings: Oostut(i) = praise.   Saavdhaan = alertness.   Aikaaguru Cheet = with a focused mind.   Sukhmani = source of comforts.  Sehj = stability (blissful state) of mind.   Nidhaan = treasure.   Ichha = wishes.   Perdhaan = leader.  Usthaan = position.   Buhour(i) = any more.   Khaat(i) = earn.   5.    Praaput(i) = prewritten in destiny. 

MEANING:  Sing praise of Waheguru with full focus and alertness of mind, oh my saintly friends. Recitation of Waheguru’s Naam and of His virtues is the source of all comforts and of a blissful state of mind.  The heart, in which Waheguru appears, becomes a treasure of virtues, and all his wishes are fulfilled. He is known as a (spiritual) leader (guide) in the whole world. He occupies the highest spiritual position and does not suffer in the birth-and-death cycle ever again.  The individual, who has Waheguru’s Naam prewritten in his destiny, leaves this world with the wealth of the Naam, oh Nanak.    5.

ਖੇਮ ਸਾਤਿ ਰਿਧਿ ਨਵ ਨਿਧਿ॥ ਬੁਧਿ ਗਿਆਨੁ ਸਰਬ ਤਹ ਸਿਧਿ॥ ਬਿਦਿਆ ਤਪੁ ਜੋਗੁ ਪ੍ਰਭ ਧਿਆਨੁ॥

(ਅੰਗ 296)॥  ਗਿਆਨੁ ਸ੍ਰੇਸਟ ਊਤਮ ਇਸਨਾਨੁ॥ ਚਾਰਿ ਪਦਾਰਥ ਕਮਲ ਪ੍ਰਗਾਸ॥ ਸਭ ਕੈ ਮਧਿ ਸਗਲ ਤੇ ਉਦਾਸ॥ ਸੁੰਦਰ ਚਤੁਰੁ ਤਤ ਕਾ ਬੇਤਾ॥ ਸਮਦਰਸੀ ਏਕ ਦ੍ਰਿਸਟੇਤਾ॥ ਇਹ ਫਲ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਕੈ ਮੁਖਿ ਭਨੈ॥ ਗੁਰ ਨਾਨਕ ਨਾਮ ਬਚਨ ਮਨਿ ਸੁਨੇ॥ 6॥

Khaym shaant(i) ridh(i) nuv nidh(i). Boodh(i), Giyan(u), serb teh sidh(i). Bidiya tup(u) joag(u), Prubh dhiyaan(u). 

(PAGE 296):       Giyan sraysht, ootum ishnaan.  Chaar(i) pdaaruth, kumul pergaas.  Subh kaiy mudh(i) sugul tay oodaas. Soondar chutur(u) ka bayta. Sumdersi, Aik drishtayta. Ih ful, tis(u) jun kaiy mookh(i) bhunaiy. Gur Nanak Naam Buchun mun(i) soonay.  6.

Word Meaning:  Khaym = wellbeing.  Shaant(i) = peace.    Ridh(i) Sidh(i) = supernatural powers.   Boodh(i) = wisdom.  Giyaan = enlightenment.  Bidiya = education; knowledge for debates.   Tup(u) = self-affliction to please some god.    Giyaan = connection with Waheguru. Sraysht = supreme.   Ootum = superior.   Ishnaan = dip at holy places.   Chaar(i) Pdaaruth = the four desired things:  morality, wealth, salvation, and fulfilment of wishes.  Kumul Pergass = blooming of the heart.  Mudh(i) = in the middle.   Oodaas = detached.   Soondar = attractive, beloved.  Chutur(u) = wise.    Tut Ka Bayta = knowledgeable about the origin (Waheguru).     Sumdersi = unbiased.   Bhunaiy = utters; recites.  Buchun = (Guru’s) Words.    6.

MEANING:  A devotee, who listens to the words of Waheguru’s praise from the Guru, and recites Waheguru’s Naam with his tongue, reaps the rewards of wellbeing; he achieves peace of mind, and feels as if he has acquired all the supernatural powers, and the nine treasures of the world.  He has enlightened wisdom.  He does not need any of the knowledge for debates (which boost ego), self-affliction, or yogis’ meditation to acquire Waheguru.  A dip in Waheguru’s Naam-pool is the supreme of all the dips in holy waters, and one acquires all the four desired products (morality, wealth, salvation, and fulfilment of all the wishes) which cause the heart of the devotee to bloom.   Such a devotee stays fully detached from Maya even while living in the middle of everything.  He is beloved of everybody, is wise, and is knowledgeable about Waheguru (is connected with Him).  He is unbiased in his views about others, and sees everybody the same way, oh Nanak.  6.

ਇਹੁ ਨਿਧਾਨੁ ਜਪੈ ਮਨਿ ਕੋਇ॥ ਸਭ ਜੁਗ ਮਹਿ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਗਤਿ ਹੋਇ॥ ਗੁਣ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ ਨਾਮ ਧੁਨਿ ਬਾਣੀ॥ ਸਿਮ੍ਰਿਤਿ ਸਾਸਤ੍ਰ ਬੇਦ ਬਖਾਣੀ॥ ਸਗਲ ਮਤਾਤ ਕੇਵਲ ਹਰਿ ਨਾਮ॥ ਗੋਬਿੰਦ ਭਗਤ ਕੈ ਮਨਿ ਬਿਸ੍ਰਾਮ॥ ਕੋਟਿ ਅਪ੍ਰਾਧ ਸਾਧ ਸੰਗਿ ਮਿਟੈ॥ ਸੰਤ ਕ੍ਰਿਪਾ ਤੇ ਜਮ ਤੇ ਛੁਟੈ॥ ਜਾ ਕੈ ਮਸਤਕਿ ਕਰਮ ਪ੍ਰਭਿ ਪਾਏ॥ ਸਾਧ ਸਰਣ ਨਾਨਕ ਤੇ ਆਏ॥7

Ehou nidhaan(u) jupaiy mun(i) koyay. Subh joog meh(i) ta kee gut(i) hoyay.  Goon Gobind, Naam dhoon(i) bani. Simrit(i) Shastar Bayd bukhaani.  Sugul mutaant kayvul Hur(i) Naam. Gobind bhugut kaiy mun(i) bisraam. Koat(i) upraadh Sadh sung(i) mitaiy. Sunt kirpa tay Jum tay chhootaiy. Ja kaiy mustuk(i) kerm Prubh(i) paayay. Sadh shern Nanak tay aayay.  7.

Word Meaning:  Subh Joog Meh(i) = in every eon.   Gut(i) = salvation.  Mutaant = teaching of all faiths.  Bisraam = dwelling.   Koat(i) = millions.   Upraadh = crimes; sins.   Jum = angel of death.  Kerm = good fortune.   Mustuk(i) = on the forehead.   Tay = those. 7.

MEANING:  All the Hindu religious books certify Waheguru’s Naam, His virtues, and concentration on His Bani (words of praise) to be the real treasure; whoever recites this treasure finds salvation, no matter what the eon is.   Waheguru’s Naam, which resides in the mind of His devotees, is the basis of all faiths. Millions of sins are erased in the shelter of the Guru, and the Angel of death can cause no harm to them. Only that devotee seeks Guru’s shelter whose forehead has good fortune written on it by the kindness of Waheguru, oh Nanak.  7. 

ਜਿਸੁ ਮਨਿ ਬਸੈ ਸੁਨੈ ਲਾਇ ਪ੍ਰੀਤਿ॥ ਤਿਸੁ ਜਨ ਆਵੈ ਹਰਿ ਪ੍ਰਭੁ ਚੀਤਿ॥ ਜਨਮ ਮਰਨ ਕਾ ਦੂਖ ਨਿਵਾਰੈ॥ ਦੁਲਭ ਦੇਹ ਤਤਕਾਲ ਉਧਾਰੈ॥ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਸੋਭਾ ਅੰਮ੍ਰਿਤ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਬਾਨੀ॥ ਏਕੁ ਨਾਮੁ ਮਨ ਮਾਹਿ ਸਮਾਨੀ॥ ਦੂਖ ਰੋਗ ਬਿਨਸੈ ਭੈ ਭਰਮ॥ ਸਾਧ ਨਾਮ ਨਿਰਮਲ ਤਾ ਕੇ ਕਰਮ॥ ਸਭ ਤੇ ਊਚ ਤਾ ਕੀ ਸੋਭਾ ਬਨੀ॥ ਨਾਨਕ ਇਹ ਗੁਣ ਨਾਮੁ ਸੁਖਮਨੀ॥ 8॥24॥

Jis(u) mun(i) busaiy, soonaiy, laayay preet(i).  Tis(u) jun aavaiy Hur(i) Prubh(u) cheet(i).  Junum, mern ka dookh nivaaraiy.  Doolubh dayh tutkaal oodhaaraiy.  Nirmul sobha, Amrit ta kee bani.  Aik Naam(u) mun maah(i) smaani. Dookh, roag, binsaiy bhaiy, bherm.  Sadh Naam nirmul ta kay kerm.  Subh tay ooch ta kee sobha bunee. Nanak, ih goon naam(u) Sukhmani.  8.24.

Word Meaning: Busaiy = dwells.   Cheet(i) = in mind.   Nivaaraiy = eradicates.   Doolubh Dayh = human life which is available with great difficulty.   Tutkaal = right away.    Sobha = fame; greatness.  Bani = routine conversation. Smaani = is mixed well; present.   Binsaiy = vanishes.    Kerm = deeds.    Sukhmani = source of bliss.   8

MEANING:  The devotee, who has Waheguru (His Naam) enshrined in his heart, listens to His Naam with love (devotion) and keeps Him in mind.  His suffering of birth and death is eradicated, and he finds salvation right away for his human body, which is available with great difficulty. Waheguru’s Naam pervades his mind, and his routine conversation involves Waheguru’s praise, which is Amrit for life, and he enjoys great fame.  With Guru’s Teachings, all his deeds become pure and just, and all his discomforts, ailments, fears and misgivings vanish.  He earns the highest level of praise (fame), and he becomes a source of comfort (bliss) for others because of these qualities, oh Nanak. 8.24.
